*Mona H. Albaqawi,Eman M. Albalkhi,Joud A. Albaiti,Enrico Lopedoto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向沙特市场的阿拉伯语金融情感分析框架，通过整合官方新闻和社交媒体数据，构建了包含84K样本的大规模阿拉伯语金融语料库，并采用Transformer-based NER和五类情感标注方案，实现了对公司级别情感动态的可靠分析及其与沙特股市行为的关联研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资者情感对金融市场具有重要影响，但阿拉伯语金融环境中的情感建模因语言复杂性和资源匮乏而面临挑战，现有研究缺乏针对阿拉伯语金融领域的大规模情感分析框架。

Method: 构建多阶段流水线：数据收集、清洗、去重、实体链接和情感标注；采用基于Transformer的命名实体识别（NER）结合公司词典，将文本提及映射到标准公司标识符；使用五类情感标注方案。

Result: 构建了包含84K样本的大规模阿拉伯语金融语料库，支持公司级别的情感聚合，实验结果表明该方法在阿拉伯语金融情感分析中具有可靠性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效捕捉沙特市场中机构投资者和公众投资者的情感，并可分析情感动态与沙特股市行为之间的关联，为阿拉伯语金融NLP领域提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Investor sentiment shapes financial markets, yet modeling sentiment in Arabic financial contexts remains challenging due to linguistic complexity and limited resources. We present an Arabic NLP framework for large-scale financial sentiment analysis tailored to the Saudi market, integrating official financial news and social media to capture institutional and public investor sentiment. The framework constructs a large Arabic financial corpus through a multi-stage pipeline encompassing data collection, cleaning, deduplication, entity linking, and sentiment annotation. Transformer-based NER combined with a curated company lexicon links textual mentions to canonical company identifiers, with sentiment labels assigned using a five-class scheme. The resulting dataset of 84K samples supports company-level sentiment aggregation and analysis of sentiment dynamics relative to stock market behavior on the Saudi Exchange. Experimental results demonstrate reliable and scalable Arabic financial sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [30] [Mathematical Reasoning in Large Language Models: Benchmarks, Architectures, Evaluation, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19723)
*Husnain Amjad,Raja Khurram Shahzad,Aamir Shahzad,Mehwish Fatima*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该综述系统回顾了约120篇关于大语言模型数学推理的研究，提出了统一的数据集分类体系，分析了推理架构与训练策略，并指出了当前推理忠实性、基准偏差和泛化局限性等关键问题及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理是评估AI系统能力的重要基准，随着LLM推理能力的提升，全面理解其在数学推理上的表现变得日益重要，但目前缺乏系统性的综述来整合数据集、架构、训练策略和评估协议等方面的进展。

Method: 采用系统性综述方法，分析了约120篇同行评审研究和预印本，构建了统一的分析框架，包括：(1) 提出统一的数学数据集分类体系（预训练语料、监督微调资源、评估基准）；(2) 系统分析推理架构与训练策略（工具集成、验证器引导推理、参数高效适配）；(3) 对比评估现有指标在最终答案准确性与过程级推理验证之间的差距。

Result: 识别出三类主要失败模式：推理忠实性问题、基准偏差和泛化局限性；揭示了当前评估指标在最终答案准确性与过程级推理验证之间存在显著差距；建立了统一的数据集分类体系和推理架构分析框架。

Conclusion: 未来研究应聚焦于改进符号接地、提升评估可靠性、开发更鲁棒和可信赖的LLM推理系统，以推动数学推理能力的发展。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning is essential for problem-solving in education, science, and industry, serving as a crucial benchmark for evaluating artificial intelligence systems. As Large Language Models (LLMs) improve their reasoning capabilities, understanding how well they perform mathematical reasoning has become increasingly important. This survey synthesizes recent advancements in mathematical reasoning with LLMs through a structured analysis of datasets, architectures, training strategies, and evaluation protocols. Our systematic review encompasses approximately 120 peer-reviewed studies and preprints, examining the evolution of this research area and providing a unified analytical framework to understand current progress and limitations. Our study particularly introduces a unified taxonomy of mathematical datasets, distinguishing between pretraining corpora, supervised fine-tuning resources, and evaluation benchmarks across varying levels of reasoning complexity. A systematic analysis of reasoning architectures and training strategies, including tool integration, verifier-guided reasoning, and parameter-efficient adaptation, is presented to assess their effects on reasoning robustness and generalization. Moreover, a comparative evaluation of existing metrics highlights the gap between final-answer accuracy and process-level reasoning verification. By synthesizing insights across these areas, our analysis identifies recurring failure modes, such as reasoning faithfulness issues, benchmark biases, and generalization limitations, and outlines key research directions toward improving symbolic grounding, evaluation reliability, and the development of more robust and trustworthy LLM-based reasoning systems.

</details>


### [31] [ContextRAG: Extraction-Free Hierarchical Graph Construction for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19735)
*Roman Prosvirnin,Sergei Kuznetsov,Seungmin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextRAG 是一种无需LLM进行实体关系抽取的图RAG系统，通过残差量化k-means和形式概念分析构建模糊概念图，在130任务子集上仅用30次LLM调用（22,073 tokens）完成索引构建，相比HiRAG的870次调用和354万tokens效率提升显著，最终在整体任务上取得33.6% F1，多跳任务上取得36.8% F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图RAG系统依赖LLM进行实体、关系和摘要抽取，导致token成本和墙钟时间随语料规模增长而显著增加，亟需一种更高效的图拓扑构建方法。

Method: ContextRAG使用残差量化k-means对分块嵌入进行聚类，结合基于Lukasiewicz剩余逻辑的形式概念分析（FCA）推导模糊概念图；通过软模糊连接（join）和交（meet）操作诱导桥接式和交式上下文节点，而非使用LLM生成图边。

Result: 在130个任务的UltraDomain子集上，ContextRAG仅用30次LLM调用和22,073 tokens完成索引构建；而HiRAG在20任务子集上即需870次调用和354万tokens，且在图构建过程中失败。ContextRAG整体F1为33.6%，多跳任务F1为36.8%。检索到至少一个格派生节点的查询比未检索到的查询F1高出3.9个百分点。

Conclusion: ContextRAG通过避免LLM实体/关系抽取，大幅降低了图RAG系统的索引构建成本，同时维持了有竞争力的问答性能，尤其对多跳任务有效。格派生节点对检索质量的提升具有诊断性关联。

Abstract: Graph-structured retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can improve answer quality on multi-hop questions, but many current systems rely on large language models (LLMs) to extract entities, relations, and summaries during indexing. These calls add token and wall-clock costs that grow with corpus size. We present ContextRAG, a graph RAG system whose graph topology is constructed without LLM-based entity or relation extraction. ContextRAG derives a fuzzy concept graph over chunk embeddings using residual-quantization k-means and Formal Concept Analysis with Lukasiewicz residuated logic. Bridge-like and meet-derived context nodes are induced by soft fuzzy join and meet operations, rather than by LLM-written graph edges. On a 130-task UltraDomain subset, ContextRAG builds its index with 30 LLM calls and 22,073 tokens. In contrast, a local HiRAG reproduction stress test required 870 indexing calls and 3.54M tokens on a 20-task subset before failing during graph construction; linear extrapolation to 130 tasks implies over 23M indexing tokens. ContextRAG obtains 33.6% F1 overall and 36.8% F1 on multi-hop tasks. An activation analysis shows that queries retrieving at least one lattice-derived node in the top five achieve +3.9 percentage points F1 over queries that do not; this association is diagnostic rather than causal.

</details>


### [32] [TERGAD: Structure-Aware Text-Enhanced Representations for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19738)
*Wen Shi,Zhe Wang,Huafei Huang,Qing Qing,Ziqi Xu,Qixin Zhang,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TERGAD通过大语言模型将节点拓扑结构转化为语义描述，再与原始属性自适应融合，利用联合重构误差检测图异常，在六个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本的图异常检测方法虽然利用原始文本特征，但忽略了节点的结构上下文，难以检测因节点内容与其拓扑角色不一致而产生的复杂异常。

Method: 1) 将节点拓扑属性转化为自然语言描述；2) 利用LLM提取高阶语义嵌入；3) 通过门控双分支自编码器自适应融合语义嵌入与原始属性；4) 基于图结构和节点特征的联合重构误差计算异常分数。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上一致优于最先进基线，消融实验验证了结构语义引导和门控融合机制的有效性。

Conclusion: TERGAD通过LLM增强的结构语义表示有效提升了图异常检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to identify atypical graph entities, such as nodes, edges, or substructures, that deviate significantly from the majority. While existing text-rich approaches typically integrate structural context into the data representation pipeline using raw textual features, they often neglect the structural context of nodes. This limitation hinders their ability to detect sophisticated anomalies arising from inconsistencies between a node's inherent content and its topological role. To bridge this gap, we propose TERGAD (Structure-aware Text-enhanced Representations for Graph Anomaly Detection), A novel data augmentation framework that enriches structural semantics for GAD via the semantic reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Specifically, TERGAD translates node-level topological properties into descriptive natural language narratives, which are subsequently processed by an LLM to derive high-level semantic embeddings. These embeddings are then adaptively fused with original node attributes through a gated dual-branch autoencoder to jointly reconstruct both graph structure and node features. The anomaly score is computed based on the integrated reconstruction error, effectively capturing deviations in both observable attributes and LLM-informed semantic expectations. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that TERGAD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Furthermore, our ablation studies validate the indispensable role of structural semantic guidance and the efficacy of the gated fusion mechanism. Code is available at https://github.com/Kantorakitty/TERGAD-main.

</details>


### [33] [Synthesis and Evaluation of Long-term History-aware Medical Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19766)
*Hebin Hu,Renke Dai,Ah-Hwee Tan,Yilin Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MediLongChat——一个通过三阶段知识引导分解（合成患者档案、生成多轮对话、整合为纵向历史数据集）构建的长期医疗对话数据集，并设立了三种推理基准任务来评估医疗智能体的记忆能力。实验表明，即使是先进的LLM在该基准上也表现不佳，凸显了定制化方法的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗对话基准缺乏真实的长期对话时间线，真实临床文本受隐私和伦理限制，而现有基准仅关注孤立交互，无法捕捉跨会话推理能力，因此亟需一个能系统评估医疗智能体长期记忆和推理能力的数据集。

Method: 采用知识引导的三阶段分解方法：(1) 构建具有多样疾病和并发症轨迹的合成患者档案；(2) 为每次就诊生成多轮对话；(3) 整合为连贯的纵向历史数据集MediLongChat。同时建立三种基准任务（对话内推理、跨对话推理、综合推理）和多维评估框架（基于向量的忠实度/连贯性/多样性指标 + LLM评判的正确性/真实性评估）。

Result: 即使是当前最先进的LLM在MediLongChat基准任务上也表现困难，说明该基准具有挑战性和适用性，同时揭示了现有模型在长期医疗对话推理方面的不足。

Conclusion: MediLongChat作为一个高质量的长期医疗对话基准，能够有效评估医疗智能体的记忆和推理能力，研究结果表明需要开发专门针对长期医疗对话的定制化方法。

Abstract: An effective healthcare agent must be able to recall and reason over a patient's longitudinal medical history. However, the absence of datasets with realistic long-term dialogue timelines limits systematic evaluation. Real clinical text is constrained by privacy and ethics, while existing benchmarks focus on isolated interactions, failing to capture cross-session reasoning. We introduce a framework for synthesizing high-quality, long-term medical dialogues with LLMs. Our approach entails a knowledge-guided decomposition into three stages: constructing synthetic patient profiles with diverse disease and complication trajectories, generating multi-turn dialogues per encounter, and integrating them into a coherent longitudinal history dataset, MediLongChat. We establish three benchmark tasks-In-dialogue Reasoning, Cross-dialogue Reasoning, and Synthesis Reasoning-to evaluate the memory capabilities of healthcare agents. To assess data quality, we introduce a multi-dimensional evaluation framework combining vector-based metrics with LLM-as-a-judge assessments. Specifically, we define automatic measures-Faithfulness, Coherence, and Diversity-together with two LLM-based evaluations: Correctness and Realism. Benchmark experiments show that even state-of-the-art LLMs struggle with MediLongChat. These findings highlight the benchmark's applicability and underscore the need for tailored methods to advance healthcare agents.

</details>


### [34] [Chunking German Legal Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19806)
*Max Prior,Natalia Milanova,Andreas Schultz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了面向德国成文法的检索增强生成分块策略，发现基于法律固有结构（如章节、小节）的分块方法在召回率和计算效率上均优于复杂的分块方法，强调了保留领域特定结构对于法律信息检索的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在结构化法律文本（以德国民法典为基准）中，何种分块策略能够最有效地支持检索增强生成，从而提升法律问答系统的检索性能。

Method: 在德国民法典这一结构化基准语料上，对比了多种分块策略：结构单元（章节、小节、句子、命题）、固定大小窗口、上下文分块、语义聚类、Lumber风格分块以及基于RAPTOR的层级检索。所有方法在具有章节级黄金标签的法律问答数据集上评估，指标包括召回率、查询延迟、索引构建时间和存储需求。

Result: 与法律固有结构对齐的分块策略（尤其是章节和小节）取得了最高的召回率，而覆盖这种固有结构的复杂方法表现更差。简单方法在计算效率上也优于LLM密集型技术（如上下文分块、RAPTOR和Lumber）。

Conclusion: 语义增强与运营成本之间存在关键权衡，保留领域特定结构对于有效的法律信息检索至关重要。

Abstract: This paper investigates chunking strategies for retrieval-augmented generation on German statutory law, using the German Civil Code as a structured benchmark corpus. We implement and compare a range of segmentation approaches, including structural units (sections, subsections, sentences, propositions), fixed-size windows, contextual chunking, semantic clustering, Lumber-style chunking, and RAPTOR-based hierarchical retrieval. All methods are evaluated on a legal question-answering dataset with section-level gold labels, measuring recall, query latency, index build time, and storage requirements. Results show that chunking strategies aligned with the inherent legal structure - particularly section and subsection - based retrieval-achieve the highest recall, while more complex approaches that override this structure perform worse. These simpler methods also offer favorable computational efficiency compared to LLM-intensive techniques such as contextual chunking, RAPTOR, and Lumber. The findings highlight a key trade-off between semantic enrichment and operational cost, and demonstrate that preserving domain-specific structure is critical for effective legal information retrieval.

</details>


### [35] [LP-Eval: Rubric and Dataset for Measuring the Quality of Legal Proposition Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19815)
*Shanshan Xu,Johan Lindholm,Amogh Raina,Henrik Palmer Olsen,Daniel Hershcovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究使用大语言模型从欧盟法院判决中自动生成和评估法律命题，提出了LP-Eval三步评估体系，发现LLM能生成高质量法律命题，但专家评估显示基于既有案例的命题质量高于近期案例，且基于规则的LLM评估比直接评分更接近专家判断，但仍无法捕捉细粒度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律命题生成是法律推理和法律学术的核心，但在法律自然语言处理中尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出LP-Eval三步评估体系，将法律命题质量分解为形式有效性和实质性维度；与法律专家合作设计评估标准；发布两位专家对100个LLM生成法律命题的标注数据集；比较不同评估方式（直接评分 vs. 基于规则的LLM评估）与专家评估的一致性。

Result: LLM能够生成结构良好且高质量的法律命题；基于既有案例的命题质量高于近期案例；基于规则的LLM评估比直接整体评分更接近专家评估，但仍无法捕捉人类专家识别的细粒度差异。

Conclusion: LLM在法律命题生成方面具有潜力，但评估任务仍需要人类专家的细粒度判断。

Abstract: Legal proposition generation is central to legal reasoning and doctrinal scholarship, yet remain under-examined in Legal NLP. This paper investigates the automatic generation and evaluation of legal propositions from decisions of the Court of Justice of the European Union using large language models (LLMs). We introduce LP-Eval, a three-step evaluation rubric co-designed with legal experts that decomposes legal proposition quality into formal validity and substantive dimensions. Using this rubric, we release a dataset of two experts' annotations for 100 LLM-generated legal propositions. Our results show that LLMs can generate predominantly well-formed and high-quality propositions, while expert evaluations reveal higher quality for propositions derived from well established cases than from recent ones. We further examine LLMs as evaluators and find that rubric-guided LLM judgments align more closely with expert assessments than direct overall scoring, but remain insensitive to finer-grained distinctions captured by human experts.

</details>


### [36] [Are Tools Always Beneficial? Learning to Invoke Tools Adaptively for Dual-Mode Multimodal LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19852)
*Qinghe Ma,Zhen Zhao,Yiming Wu,Jian Zhang,Lei Bai,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoTool提出了一种自适应工具调用策略，通过强化学习框架和双模态推理机制，让多模态大模型根据查询特征决定是否调用工具，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强推理研究主要关注如何让模型调用工具，但忽略了调用工具的必要性——冗余或不恰当的工具调用会增加推理开销甚至误导预测结果。

Method: 在强化学习框架下，设计了显式的双模态推理策略（工具辅助推理与纯文本推理），并引入模态特定的奖励函数；同时通过联合探索与平衡两种推理模式，防止早期偏向单一模式，并在后期促进自由探索。

Result: 在V*基准上相较基模型准确率提升21.8%，在POPE基准上相较现有工具增强方法效率提升44.9%。

Conclusion: AutoTool在提升多模态推理性能的同时显著降低了推理开销，验证了自适应工具调用策略的有效性。

Abstract: Tool-augmented reasoning has emerged as a promising direction for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, existing studies mainly focus on enabling models to perform tool invocation, while neglecting the necessity of invoking tools. We argue that tool usage is not always beneficial, as redundant or inappropriate invocations largely increase reasoning overhead and even mislead model predictions. To address this issue, we introduce AutoTool, a model that adaptively decides whether to invoke tools according to the characteristics of each query. Within a reinforcement learning framework, we design an explicit dual-mode reasoning strategy with mode-specific reward functions to guide the model toward producing accurate responses. Moreover, to prevent premature bias toward a single reasoning mode, AutoTool jointly explores and balances tool-assisted and text-centric reasoning throughout training, and promotes free exploration in later stages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AutoTool exhibits outstanding performance and high efficiency, yielding a 21.8\% accuracy gain on V* benchmark compared to the base model, and a 44.9\% improvement in efficiency over existing tool-augmented methods on POPE benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/MQinghe/AutoTool.

</details>


### [37] [What Are LLMs Doing to Scientific Communication? Measuring Changes in Writing Practices and Reading Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19936)
*Filip Miletić,Neele Falk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分析ACL Anthology中37,000多篇论文（2020-2024年）的自然语料库以及3,000篇人工写作与LLM改进的合成数据集，研究了大规模语言模型对自然语言处理领域科学写作风格的影响。研究发现：词汇频率和使用语境随时间显著变化，出现语义专业化和泛化现象；LLM修改后的文本更频繁使用某些句法结构，包含更复杂、更长的词汇，且词汇多样性降低；专家评估显示LLM改进文本更易理解和更令人兴奋，但对LLM持有负面态度，表明AI辅助写作对阅读体验存在强烈主观影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大规模语言模型在科学写作中日益广泛使用是否改变了科学交流的风格，以及这种改变的具体表现和主观阅读体验影响。

Method: 构建两个数据集：ACL Anthology中37,000+篇论文（2020-2024）的自然语料库，以及3,000篇人工写作与LLM改进的合成数据集。实施历时词汇分析、复杂文体特征建模，以及20位领域专家的试点标注研究。

Result: 词汇频率和使用语境显著变化，出现语义专业化和泛化；LLM修改文本更多使用特定句法结构，词汇更复杂更长且多样性降低；专家认为LLM改进文本更易理解、更令人兴奋，但对LLM持负面态度。

Conclusion: LLM的使用确实改变了NLP领域的科学写作风格，这种改变在客观文体特征和主观阅读体验上均有体现，但读者对AI辅助写作存在强烈的负面主观态度，反映了技术接受度与客观质量评估之间的分歧。

Abstract: Has the style of scientific communication changed due to the growing use of large language models in the writing process? We address this question in the domain of Natural Language Processing by leveraging two data resources we create: a naturalistic corpus of over 37,000 papers from the ACL Anthology (2020-2024); and a synthetic dataset of 3,000 human-written passages and their LLM-generated improvements. We first implement a series of diachronic lexical analyses, showing that both word frequency and usage contexts have changed significantly over time, indicating semantic specialization in some cases and generalization in others. Broadening our perspective, we then model a range of more complex stylistic features and find that LLM-modified texts more frequently contain certain syntactic constructions, more complex and longer words and a lower lexical diversity. Finally, we connect these changes in writing practices to subjective reading experience through a pilot annotation study with 20 domain experts. They overall rate LLM-improved texts as more understandable and exciting, but also express negative qualitative attitudes towards LLMs, highlighting the strongly subjective effect of AI-assisted writing on reading experience.

</details>


### [38] [FlexDraft: Flexible Speculative Decoding via Attention Tuning and Bonus-Guided Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20022)
*Yaojie Zhang,Jianuo Huang,Junlong Ke,Yuhang Han,Yongji Long,Tianchen Zhao,Biqing Qi,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlexDraft是一种无损推测解码框架，通过注意力调优、奖励引导校准和灵活解码三种设计，解决了并行推测解码在大批量场景下的吞吐量下降问题，实现了对不同批量大小的自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行推测解码方法在小批量时有效，但在大批量时由于奖励令牌和接受长度的不确定性导致草稿验证不匹配，吞吐量增益崩溃；同时现有方法要么需要昂贵的持续预训练且质量下降，要么接受率低。

Method: (1) 注意力调优：仅微调最后几层的注意力投影器在掩码令牌上，冻结自回归路径以保持目标分布；(2) 奖励引导校准：使用轻量级MLP基于已解析的奖励令牌校准草稿logits；(3) 灵活解码：小批量时并行草稿与验证，大批量时切换为顺序草稿然后验证，并根据草稿置信度调整验证长度。

Result: 在小批量和大批量场景下均能保持高质量生成，避免冗余计算，消除草稿验证不匹配问题，实现吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: FlexDraft通过灵活适应不同批量大小的设计，在无损前提下有效解决了并行推测解码在大批量场景的性能退化问题，是一种实用且高效的推测解码框架。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates memory-bound LLM inference without quality degradation by using a fast drafter to propose multiple candidate tokens and the target model to verify them in parallel. However, conventional sequential speculative decoding suffers from mutual waiting between drafting and verification, and repeated exchange of intermediate states further increases memory access overhead. Parallel speculative decoding addresses this limitation by performing drafting and verification within a single target forward pass, allowing future drafts to be prepared while current candidates are being verified. Although effective at small batch sizes, existing parallel speculative decoding methods either require costly continual pretraining with quality degradation or suffer from low acceptance rates. More importantly, this paradigm inherently suffers from uncertainty in both the bonus token and the accepted length, leading to draft verification mismatch and causing throughput gains to collapse at large batch sizes. To address these limitations, we introduce FlexDraft, a lossless speculative decoding framework that flexibly adapts to varying batch sizes through three key designs. (1) Attention Tuning enables block diffusion drafting by tuning only the attention projectors of the final few layers on mask tokens, while keeping the autoregressive path frozen to preserve the target distribution and produce high quality drafts with minimal trainable parameters. (2) Bonus-guided Calibration uses a lightweight MLP conditioned on the resolved bonus token to calibrate draft logits, mitigating draft verification mismatch caused by bonus token uncertainty. (3) Flex Decoding dynamically switches between parallel draft and verify at small batch sizes and sequential draft then verify at large batch sizes, and adjusts verification length based on draft confidence to eliminate redundant computation.

</details>


### [39] [Language Mutations Sustain the Persistences of Conspiracy Theories on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20050)
*Calvin Yixiang Cheng,Dorian Quelle,Scott A. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究基于X平台三年数据集，通过计算语言学和生存模型分析，发现语义突变越大的阴谋论帖子寿命越长。心理语言学属性（代词、社会指代词、认知过程词、风险/健康词汇）和AAT结构（行动者-行动-目标）的突变均与更长的帖子寿命相关。定性分析识别出两种主要突变模式：简化和同化。研究建议内容审核应关注阴谋论的核心主张及其可变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言突变如何影响阴谋论在社交媒体上的持续传播，理解其持久存在的语言机制，为内容审核策略提供理论依据。

Method: 使用X平台三年的阴谋论相关帖子数据集，结合计算语言学分析和生存模型（Survival Modelling）进行定量分析，辅以定性分析识别突变模式。

Result: 语义突变越大的阴谋论帖子寿命越长；心理语言学属性（代词、社会指代词、认知过程词、风险/健康词汇）的突变与更长寿命相关；AAT类别（行动者、行动、目标）的突变也与更长寿命相关；识别出简化和同化两种主要突变模式。

Conclusion: 语言突变是阴谋论在社交媒体上持续传播的重要机制，内容审核应考虑阴谋论主张的可变性，聚焦于能够应对其潜在变化的核心主张。

Abstract: This study investigates how language mutations affect the persistent diffusion of conspiracy theories on social media. Drawing on a three-year dataset of conspiracy-related posts from X, and applying computational linguistic analysis alongside survival modelling, we find that conspiracy claims with greater semantic mutations have substantially longer lifespans. Mutations in psycholinguistic properties, including pronouns, social reference words, cognitive process terms, risk- and health- related vocabularies, are associated with extended lifespans. Mutations in actor, action and target (AAT) categories are associated with longer lifespans as well. Qualitative analysis identifies two predominant mutation patterns: simplification and assimilation, at both linguistic and AAT structural levels. Taken together, the results advance our understanding of how language mutations contribute to conspiracy persistence online and shed lights on longitudinal content moderation strategies. We argue that content moderation should consider the mutability of conspiracy claims and focus on the core claims that can address their potential variations.

</details>


### [40] [PromptRad: Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Label Prompt-Tuning for Low-Resource Radiology Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20052)
*Ying-Jia Lin,Tzu-Chin Lo,Ping-Chien Li,Chi-Tung Cheng,Chien-Hung Liao,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptRad，一种基于知识增强的提示调优方法，用于低资源场景下的放射学报告多标签分类。该方法将多标签分类重构为掩码语言建模，利用UMLS同义词丰富类别表示，在仅有32个标注样本的情况下超越词典和微调基线，性能接近GPT-4但模型更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的标注器难以处理临床报告中多样化的描述，而微调预训练语言模型需要大量标注数据，这在临床环境中往往不可获得。

Method: 将多标签分类重构为掩码语言建模任务，从UMLS元同义词库中引入同义词构建多词言语器以丰富类别表示，在不添加额外分类层的情况下微调PLM。

Result: 在肝脏CT报告上，仅用32个标注训练样本即优于基于词典和微调的基线方法，并使用更小的模型达到了与GPT-4可比的性能；在复杂否定模式捕获方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PromptRad是数据稀缺临床场景下报告标注的有效解决方案，在低资源设置下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Automatic report labeling facilitates the identification of clinical findings from unstructured text and enables large-scale annotation for medical imaging research. Existing rule-based labelers struggle with the diverse descriptions in clinical reports, while fine-tuning pre-trained language models (PLMs) requires large amounts of labeled data that are often unavailable in clinical settings. In this paper, we propose PromptRad, a knowledge-enhanced multi-label \textbf{prompt}-tuning approach for \textbf{rad}iology report labeling under low-resource settings. PromptRad reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and incorporates synonyms from the UMLS Metathesaurus into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich category representations. By fine-tuning the PLM without additional classification layers, PromptRad requires substantially less labeled data than conventional fine-tuning. Experiments on liver CT reports show that PromptRad outperforms dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines with only 32 labeled training examples, and achieves competitive performance with GPT-4 despite using a much smaller model. Further analysis demonstrates that PromptRad captures complex negation patterns more effectively than existing methods, making it a promising solution for report labeling in data-scarce clinical scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ila-lab/PromptRad.

</details>


### [41] [CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20075)
*Dachuan Shi,Hanlin Zhu,Xiangchi Yuan,Wanjia Zhao,Kejing Xia,Wen Xiao,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CopT，一种颠倒思考与回答顺序的新推理流程：先产生草稿答案，再基于该答案进行后续思考（反思与修正）。通过将连续嵌入作为推理时的对比验证器，估计答案可靠性，并在答案不可靠时执行受控的on-policy思考。实验表明，该方法在数学、编程和智能体推理任务中最高可提升23%的准确率，并最多减少57%的token消耗，无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准思维链（CoT）将思考视为回答的前提，但模型有时在完整思考前已能识别正确答案（即表演性推理），这延迟了答案获取并产生了不必要的token开销。为此，本文旨在重新设计推理流程，在保证或提升推理质量的同时，减少不必要的计算成本。

Method: CopT采用"先作答后思考"的流程：先让模型生成一个草稿答案，再基于该答案进行on-policy思考（反思与修正）。核心创新在于将连续嵌入作为推理时的对比验证器：通过对比相同生成token在离散token输入和连续嵌入输入下模型的支持程度，构造序列级别的反向KL散度估计量，用于评估答案可靠性。理论分析表明该估计量等于未解析隐状态与输出答案token之间的互信息，从而捕捉与答案相关的不确定性。当答案被认为不够可靠时，CopT执行进一步的on-policy思考，并利用第二个KL估计量动态控制草稿答案的可见性，在保留有用部分信息的同时降低被不可靠内容误导的风险。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和智能体推理任务上，CopT相比标准CoT基线，峰值准确率最高提升23%，token消耗最高减少57%，且在相同或更高准确率水平下均表现出优势。

Conclusion: CopT通过颠倒思考与回答的顺序，结合推理时对比验证机制，有效缓解了表演性推理问题，实现了推理质量与计算效率的双赢，且无需任何额外训练，具有良好的实用性和通用性。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is a standard approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities from large language models (LLMs). However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of thinking and answering. Instead of thinking before answering, CopT first elicits a draft answer and then invokes subsequent on-policy thinking conditioned on its own draft answer for reflection and correction. To assess whether the draft answer should be trusted, CopT recasts continuous embeddings as inference-time contrastive verifiers. Specifically, it contrasts the model's support for the same generated tokens under discrete-token inputs and continuous-embedding inputs, yielding a sequence-level reverse KL estimator for answer reliability. Our analysis shows that under certain assumptions, the expected estimate equals the mutual information between the unresolved latent state and the emitted answer token, explaining why it captures answer-relevant uncertainty rather than arbitrary uncertainty in the latent state. When the answer is deemed insufficiently reliable, CopT performs further on-policy thinking, where a second KL estimator dynamically controls draft-answer visibility, preserving useful partial information while reducing the risk of being misled by unreliable content. Across mathematics, coding, and agentic reasoning tasks, CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and reduces token usage by up to 57% at comparable or higher accuracy, without any additional training. The code is available at https://github.com/sdc17/CopT.

</details>


### [42] [BalanceRAG: Joint Risk Calibration for Cascaded Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20084)
*Zijun Jia,Yuanchang Ye,Sen Jia,Yiyao Qian,Haoning Wang,Baojie Chen,Diyin Tang,Jinsong Yu,Zhiyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BalanceRAG提出了一种基于二维网格序贯图测试的联合风险校准方法，在不必要使用RAG时避免检索，同时保证系统级错误率受控，在多个开放域QA基准上实现了更高覆盖率和更少检索调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联RAG采用逐阶段校准的方式可能过于保守，因为最终效用取决于LLM-only和RAG两阶段的联合不确定性阈值设定，而非独立校准。

Method: 将每个阈值对视为二维网格上的操作点，利用序贯图测试识别安全操作点，实现风险自适应的阈值校准；可扩展到多风险校准，同时约束检索使用率和选择条件风险。

Result: 在多个开放域QA基准和多种LLM骨干上，BalanceRAG满足预设风险水平，保持更高覆盖率和更多正确接受样本，并减少不必要的检索调用。

Conclusion: 联合校准阈值比逐阶段保守校准更优，能够在不牺牲错误控制的前提下提升系统效率和覆盖率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can enhance factuality via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but applying RAG to every query is unnecessary when the model-only answer is reliable. This motivates cascaded RAG: each query is first handled by an LLM-only branch, escalated to a RAG fallback only if the primary branch is uncertain, and abstained from when neither branch is sufficiently trustworthy. However, calibrating such cascades stage by stage may be conservative, since the final utility depends on joint uncertainty thresholding of LLM-only and RAG. In this work, we develop BalanceRAG to certify threshold pairs at a target risk level. Given uncertainty scores from the two branches, BalanceRAG frames each threshold pair as an operating point on a two-dimensional lattice and identifies safe operating points using sequential graphical testing. This enables risk-adaptive threshold calibration, controlling the system-level error rate among accepted points, while retaining more examples. Furthermore, BalanceRAG extends to multi-risk calibration, allowing retrieval usage to be bounded together with the selection-conditioned risk. Experiments on three open-domain question answering (QA) benchmarks across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that BalanceRAG meets prescribed risk levels, preserves higher coverage and more accepted correct examples, and reduces unnecessary retrieval calls compared with always-on RAG.

</details>


### [43] [ThoughtTrace: Understanding User Thoughts in Real-World LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20087)
*Chuanyang Jin,Binze Li,Haopeng Xie,Cathy Mengying Fang,Tianjian Li,Shayne Longpre,Hongxiang Gu,Maximillian Chen,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ThoughtTrace是首个大规模数据集，记录了用户在与AI对话中的真实想法（发送提示的原因、对助手回复的反应），包含1,058名用户、2,155段对话、17,058轮交互及10,174条想法注释，覆盖20种语言模型。该数据集捕获了长时域、主题多样的交互，且想法在语义上不同于消息文本，难以被前沿LLM从上下文中推断。实验表明，想法能改善用户行为预测，并为训练个性化助手提供细粒度对齐信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI数据集仅记录用户说了什么，而没有记录用户在想什么，缺乏对用户潜在认知过程（如发送提示的原因、对回复的内心反应）的捕捉。为了研究人机交互背后的认知动态，需要建立记录用户真实想法的数据集。

Method: 收集1,058名用户与20种语言模型进行的2,155段多轮对话，标注了17,058轮交互中的10,174条用户"想法"（含发送提示的原因和对助手回复的反应）。通过语义分析验证想法与消息文本的区分性，并测试前沿LLM是否能从上下文中推断出这些想法。进一步将想法用于用户行为预测（作为推理时上下文）和思想引导的回复改写（作为个性化对齐信号）。

Result: ThoughtTrace捕获了长时域、主题多样的交互；用户想法在语义上显著区别于消息文本，且即使是最前沿的LLM也难以从上下文中准确推断用户想法；想法内容丰富多样，并与对话阶段密切相关。在应用上，想法作为推理时上下文能改善用户行为预测；思想引导的回复改写能为训练个性化助手提供细粒度对齐信号。

Conclusion: ThoughtTrace将用户想法确立为人机交互研究的新数据模态，为理解用户潜在目标、偏好和需求提供了基础，有助于构建更能理解和适应用户的个性化AI助手。

Abstract: Conversational AI has now reached billions of users, yet existing datasets capture only what people say, not what they think. We introduce ThoughtTrace, the first large-scale dataset that pairs real-world multi-turn human--AI conversations with users' self-reported thoughts: their reasons for sending prompts and reactions to assistant responses. ThoughtTrace comprises 1,058 users, 2,155 conversations, 17,058 turns, and 10,174 thought annotations collected across 20 language models. Our analysis shows that ThoughtTrace captures long-horizon, topically diverse interactions, and that thoughts are semantically distinct from messages, difficult for frontier LLMs to infer from context, diverse in content, and tied to conversation stages. We further demonstrate the utility of thoughts for downstream modeling. First, thoughts improve user-behavior prediction as inference-time context. Second, thought-guided rewrites provide fine-grained alignment signals for training personalized assistants. Together, ThoughtTrace establishes user thoughts as a new data modality for studying the cognitive dynamics behind human--AI interaction and provides a foundation for building assistants that better understand and adapt to users' latent goals, preferences, and needs.

</details>


### [44] [MixRea: Benchmarking Explicit-Implicit Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20128)
*Yuanqing Cai,Ziyi Huang,Minhao Liu,Lixin Duan,Wen Li,Yanru Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型存在类似人类"非注意盲视"的认知缺陷——在显式指令下忽略细微但重要的上下文线索，并提出MixRea基准和PRCP提示方法来解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类认知中"非注意盲视"理论的启发，探究LLM在嵌入注意力偏差的人类偏好语料训练后，是否也会在显式任务指令下忽略细微但重要的上下文线索。

Method: 构建MixRea基准（包含2,246道选择题，涵盖9种推理类型，混合显式与隐式信息分布）；提出Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP) 提示方法，通过恢复被忽略的因果关联来改善推理。

Result: 评估21个先进LLM，最佳推理模型（Gemini 2.5 Pro）仅达42.8%的一致性，揭示广泛存在的非注意盲视现象。

Conclusion: LLM在显式-隐式推理任务中存在显著的认知盲点，该局限在多种多源推理任务中持续存在，需要开发更具认知对齐的模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into high-stakes decision-making. Inspired by the theory of \emph{inattentional blindness} in human cognition, we investigate whether LLMs, trained on human-preferred corpora that embed attentional biases, exhibit a similar limitation: \emph{failing to attend to subtle yet important contextual cues under explicit task instructions}. To evaluate this, we introduce the task of \textbf{explicit-implicit reasoning} and present \textbf{MixRea}, a benchmark of 2,246 multiple-choice questions across 9 reasoning types with varying distributions of explicit and implicit information. Evaluation of 21 advanced LLMs shows that even the best-performing reasoning model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieves only 42.8\% consistency, revealing widespread inattentional blindness. To mitigate this, we propose \textbf{Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP)}, a prompting method that improves reasoning by recovering overlooked causal relations. Further analysis shows that this limitation persists across diverse multi-source reasoning tasks, highlighting the need for more cognitively aligned models.

</details>


### [45] [IMLJD: A Computational Dataset for Indian Matrimonial Litigation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19346)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍IMLJD数据集，包含3,613份印度婚姻纠纷判决书（2000-2024年最高法院1,474份，2018-2024年卡纳塔克邦高等法院2,139份），结构化结果标签、元数据指标和知识图谱。分析发现最高法院撤销申请成功率（57.6%）显著高于高等法院（39.7%），经时间匹配后差异扩大至19.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有印度婚姻纠纷法律数据集稀缺，且缺乏跨法院层级的系统比较分析，无法支撑定量研究司法决策差异。

Method: 构建了包含3,613份判决的结构化数据集，覆盖IPC第498A条、反家庭暴力法和CrPC第482条，设计结果标签、元数据指标和知识图谱，并对2018-2024年时间段进行匹配比较。

Result: 最高法院撤销申请成功率为57.6%，卡纳塔克邦高等法院为39.7%；时间匹配后最高法院为59.3%，差异扩大至19.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 印度最高法院与高等法院在婚姻纠纷撤销申请成功率上存在显著差异，且该差异在时间匹配后更加明显，数据集开源供进一步研究。

Abstract: We present IMLJD, an open dataset of 3,613 Indian court judgments covering matrimonial disputes under IPC Section 498A, the Protection of Women from Domestic Violence Act, and CrPC Section 482. The dataset covers the Supreme Court of India from 2000 to 2024 (1,474 cases) and the Karnataka High Court from 2018 to 2024 (2,139 cases), with structured outcome labels, metadata-derived indicators, and a knowledge graph. We find that 57.6% of quashing petitions succeed at the Supreme Court level compared to 39.7% at the Karnataka High Court level. On a matched 2018 to 2024 period, the SC quash rate is 59.3%, widening the differential to 19.6 percentage points and confirming the finding is robust to temporal adjustment. The dataset, code, and knowledge graph are released openly at https://github.com/joyboseroy/imljd and https://huggingface.co/datasets/joyboseroy/imljd.

</details>


### [46] [Base Models Look Human To AI Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19516)
*Yixuan Even Xu,Ziqian Zhong,Aditi Raghunathan,Fei Fang,J. Zico Kolter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现现有AI文本检测器（如GPTZero）对基础模型生成的文本倾向判为“人类”，而对指令微调模型生成的文本则能准确识别。基于此，提出HIP方法（迭代释义改写），通过微调基础模型为释义器并迭代应用，在保持语义的同时有效规避检测。实验表明，检测器实际上更多捕捉的是指令微调带来的局部特征，而非真正的机器文本特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业AI文本检测器在实际应用（尤其是教育领域）中广泛使用，但作者发现一个反直觉现象：基础模型生成的文本往往被误判为人类写作，而指令微调版本的文本则能被正确识别。这促使作者探究检测器的工作原理并开发规避方法。

Method: 提出Humanization by Iterative Paraphrasing (HIP)，一种与检测器无关的流水线：首先对基础模型进行最小微调，使其成为释义器（paraphraser），然后迭代应用该释义器对文本进行改写。在Llama-3和Qwen-3系列（0.6B到70B）上进行实验。

Result: HIP在语义保留与检测规避之间取得了比基线方法更好的权衡；跨不同模型规模和系列均一致提升了检测器对人类相似度的判定。

Conclusion: 现有检测器更多是在追踪指令微调和局部上下文的伪影（artifacts），而非真正的不变的机器文本特征。这呼吁未来的检测器设计需要更明确地对这些因素进行建模。

Abstract: As AI-generated text enters the real-world at scale, institutions increasingly use commercial AI-text detectors, especially in education and academic-integrity workflows. We report a surprising empirical finding about such systems: when evaluated by GPTZero and Pangram, generated text from base models is often judged overwhelmingly human, whereas text generated by their instruction-tuned counterparts is not. Building on this observation, we propose Humanization by Iterative Paraphrasing (HIP), a detector-agnostic pipeline that minimally fine-tunes a base model into a paraphraser and applies it iteratively. Compared with the baselines we test, HIP yields a stronger trade-off between semantic preservation and detector evasion on commercial detectors. Across Llama-3 and Qwen-3 families, spanning model sizes from 0.6B to 70B, HIP consistently improves detector human-likeness. Our findings suggest that current detectors are tracking artifacts of instruction tuning and local context more than any invariant notion of machine-generated text. This, in turn, calls for detector designs that model these factors more explicitly.

</details>


### [47] [Less Back-and-Forth: A Comparative Study of Structured Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20149)
*Saurav Ghosh,Gabriella Polach,Abdou Sow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化提示设计对LLM响应质量的影响，通过对比原始提示、清单改进提示和澄清问题提示三种条件，发现清单改进提示在质量评分（7.50/8）和效率（更少token）上均最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在开放式任务中常因提示不明确导致回答质量低且需要额外交互，本文旨在探索结构化提示设计能否在提升响应质量的同时减少用户投入。

Method: 在四种任务类型（摘要、规划、解释、编码）上，使用三种LLM系统（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok）对比三种提示条件（原始提示、清单改进提示、澄清问题提示），采用统一评分量表（任务完成度、正确性、合规性、清晰度）评估输出质量。

Result: 清单改进提示平均评分7.50/8，优于原始提示（5.67）和澄清问题提示（6.67），且平均token数最少，实现了最佳质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 简单的提示清单能有效提升LLM响应质量并减少不必要的交互。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for open-ended tasks, but underspecified prompts can lead to low-quality answers and additional interaction. This paper studies whether structured prompt design improves response quality while reducing user effort. We compare three prompt conditions: a raw prompt, a checklist-improved prompt, and a clarifying-question prompt. We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.

</details>


### [48] [Drifting Objectives for Refining Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19470)
*Daisuke Oba,Hiroki Furuta,Naoaki Okazaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TokenDrift，将连续生成器中的漂移（drifting）原理迁移到离散扩散语言模型（DDLM）中。通过将分类预测提升为软token特征，在冻结语义空间中应用反对称漂移，并将停止梯度特征目标反向传播到DDLM logits。实验表明，该方法在固定步数生成中显著降低了困惑度（MDLM降低89%，DUO降低86%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续生成器中的漂移方法表明，部分采样时的校正可以吸收到训练中。作者希望将这一原理迁移到离散扩散语言模型中，以改善其生成质量。

Method: TokenDrift：将离散DDLM的分类预测提升为软token特征，在冻结的语义空间中应用反对称漂移，然后将停止梯度的特征目标反向传播回DDLM的logits，从而在训练中融入漂移校正。

Result: 在masked和uniform-state扩散骨干的受控持续训练实验中，TokenDrift在固定NFE生成中优于匹配的continue训练基线。在4个NFE下，MDLM的Gen.-PPL降低89%，DUO降低86%。

Conclusion: 漂移可以为DDLM提供实用的细化目标，有效提升离散扩散模型在有限采样步数下的生成质量。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models (DDLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising categorical token sequences, while recent drifting methods for continuous generators suggest that part of this sampling-time correction can instead be absorbed into training through an anti-symmetric fixed-point objective. We study how to transfer this principle to DDLMs, where the main challenge is the interface with discrete text: hard token samples are non-differentiable, and categorical predictions do not directly provide continuous samples to drift. We formulate TokenDrift, a drifting objective that lifts categorical predictions to soft-token features, applies anti-symmetric drifting in a frozen semantic space, and backpropagates the resulting stop-gradient feature target to DDLM logits. In controlled continual-training experiments with masked and uniform-state diffusion backbones, TokenDrift improves fixed-NFE generation quality over matched continuation baselines, reducing Gen.-PPL at 4 NFEs by 89% on MDLM and 86% on DUO. These results suggest that drifting can provide a practical refinement objective for DDLMs.

</details>

### [49] [Dimensional Balance Improves Large Scale Spatiotemporal Prediction Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18793)
*Jing Chen,Shixiang Pan,Yujie Fan,Haocheng Ye,Haitao Xu,Wenqiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于空间和时间熵平衡的通用框架，通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度并扩展时间范围，在交通、气象和流行病预测任务上显著提升准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空模式分析方法存在性能瓶颈，通常只能带来增量改进且跨领域迁移性有限。作者通过时空熵测度分析发现，时空复杂度不匹配越大，预测不确定性越高，尤其在固定模型容量下。这激发了他们设计一种能协调时空特征表示的通用框架。

Method: 1) 使用空间和时间熵作为诊断指标，量化时空复杂度不匹配程度；2) 通过低秩矩阵嵌入压缩空间维度，保留关键结构信息；3) 扩展时间范围以捕获长程依赖，缓解时间异质性导致的累积误差。

Result: 在城市场景交通、气象和流行病数据集上的广泛实验表明，该框架在评估领域取得了显著的准确率提升和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该可扩展的自适应框架在已评估的跨领域任务上表现优异，有望推广到更广泛的时空预测任务中。代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate spatiotemporal pattern analysis is critical in fields such as urban traffic, meteorology, and public health monitoring. However, existing methods face performance bottlenecks, typically yielding only incremental gains and often exhibiting limited cross-domain transferability. We analyze this bottleneck through spatial and temporal entropy measures, which are used as diagnostic indicators of spatiotemporal complexity mismatch rather than as guarantees that entropy alignment alone yields better forecasting. Empirically, larger mismatch is often accompanied by higher prediction uncertainty, especially under a fixed model-capacity budget. Guided by this diagnostic, we propose a scalable, adaptive framework that harmonizes spatial and temporal feature representations. Spatial dimensionality is compressed via low-rank matrix embedding to preserve essential structure, while an extended temporal horizon captures long-range dependencies and mitigates cumulative errors arising from temporal heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on urban traffic, meteorological, and epidemic datasets demonstrate substantial accuracy gains and broad applicability across the evaluated domains, suggesting that the framework is promising for a wide range of spatiotemporal tasks beyond the current study. The code is available on GitHub at https://github.com/ST-Balance/ST-Balance.

</details>


### [50] [HELLoRA: Hot Experts Layer-Level Low-Rank Adaptation for Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18795)
*Jia Wei,Zhonghao Zhang,Ping Chen,Qianyang li,Yancheng Pan,Shaoxun Wang,Ziyi Qiu,Longxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HELLoRA，通过仅在每层最频繁激活的专家上附加LoRA模块，实现MoE模型的高效参数微调。与LoRI结合形成HELLoRI在极端参数预算下进一步压缩。在三个MoE骨干模型和三个任务族上，HELLoRA使用更少参数量和FLOPs，获得更高吞吐量和准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA变体主要针对密集架构，而MoE模型的稀疏激活模式为更高效的适配提供了未被充分利用的机会。本文旨在利用MoE的稀疏激活特性，实现更高效、更经济的参数高效微调。

Method: HELLoRA：仅对每层中最频繁激活的专家附加LoRA模块，减少可训练参数和适配器FLOPs。HELLoRI：进一步结合LoRI，冻结上投影层并稀疏化下投影层，在极端参数预算下压缩模型。

Result: 在OlMoE-1B-7B、Mixtral-8x7B、DeepSeekMoE三个MoE骨干模型以及数学推理、代码生成、安全对齐三个任务上，HELLoRA优于强PEFT基线。相对于OlMoE上的标准LoRA，使用15.7%的可训练参数，减少38.7%的适配器FLOPs，训练吞吐量提升1.9倍，准确率提高9.2%。在DeepSeekMoE上仅用23.2%的LoRA参数量即超越LoRA性能。

Conclusion: 激活感知的适配器放置是将PEFT扩展到MoE语言模型的有效且实用的途径。这种机制可作为一种结构化正则化形式，保留预训练专家专业化能力。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) dominates parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large language models, yet most variants target dense architectures. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale parameters at near-constant per-token compute, and their sparse activation patterns create untapped opportunities for more efficient adaptation. We propose Hot-Experts Layer-level Low-Rank Adaptation (HELLoRA), which attaches LoRA modules only to the most frequently activated experts at each layer. This simple mechanism reduces trainable parameters and adapter-induced FLOPs while improving downstream performance, an effect we attribute to a form of structured regularization that preserves pretrained expert specialization. To stress-test HELLoRA under extreme parameter budgets, we further compose it with LoRI to form HELLoRI, which freezes the up-projection and sparsifies the down-projection. Across three MoE backbones, namely OlMoE-1B-7B, Mixtral-8x7B, and DeepSeekMoE, and three task families covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, and safety alignment, HELLoRA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines. Relative to vanilla LoRA on OlMoE, HELLoRA uses 15.7% of the trainable parameters, reduces adapter FLOPs by 38.7%, achieves 1.9x the training throughput, and improves accuracy by 9.2%. On DeepSeekMoE, HELLoRA outperforms LoRA while using only 23.2% of its trainable parameters. These results demonstrate that activation-aware adapter placement is an effective and practical route to scaling PEFT for MoE language models.

</details>


### [51] [UCCI: Calibrated Uncertainty for Cost-Optimal LLM Cascade Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18796)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UCCI路由框架，通过等渗回归校准层级不确定性为每查询错误概率，并通过约束成本最小化自动选择升级阈值，在75K实体识别查询上以微F1=0.91降低31%推理成本，ECE从0.12降至0.03。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有级联和路由系统使用未校准的置信度分数，且需要针对每个工作负载手动调整阈值，缺乏自动化和高可靠性。

Method: 1) 将token级边缘不确定性通过等渗回归映射为每查询错误概率；2) 通过约束成本最小化选择升级阈值；3) 在三个显式假设下证明阈值策略是成本最优的，且等渗校准在ECE上达到O(n^{-1/3})样本复杂度。

Result: 在75K NER查询、4B/12B模型、H100 GPU上，UCCI在微F1=0.91时降低31%推理成本(95% CI: [27%,35%])，ECE从0.12降至0.03，优于熵阈值法、分裂共形路由和FrugalGPT风格的学习阈值。

Conclusion: 校准优先的路由方法能自动确定最优升级阈值，显著降低推理成本同时保持高精度，且所有结果基于真实模型输出和实测延迟，而非模拟数据。

Abstract: LLM cascades and model routing promise lower inference cost by sending easy queries to a small model and escalating hard ones to a large model, but most deployed routers use uncalibrated confidence scores and require per-workload threshold tuning. We present UCCI, a calibration-first router that maps token-level margin uncertainty to a per-query error probability via isotonic regression and selects the escalation threshold by constrained cost minimization. Under three explicit assumptions, threshold policies on the calibrated score are cost-optimal, and isotonic calibration achieves O(n^{-1/3}) sample complexity for expected calibration error (ECE). On a production named entity recognition workload of 75,000 queries served by 4B and 12B instruction-tuned LLMs on H100 GPUs, UCCI cuts inference cost by 31% (95% CI: [27%, 35%]) at micro-F1 = 0.91 while reducing ECE from 0.12 to 0.03. At the same operating point, UCCI beats entropy thresholding, split-conformal routing, and a FrugalGPT-style learned threshold. All cascade results use end-to-end routing on actual model outputs and measured H100 latency, not simulated routing from global accuracies or nominal API prices.

</details>


### [52] [Simply Stabilizing the Loop via Fully Looped Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18797)
*Rao Fu,Zixuan Yang,Jiankun Zhang,Jing Ma,Hechang Chen,Yu Li,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fully Looped Transformer，通过全循环架构和注意力注入两种无参数修改，解决了Looped Transformer在增加循环次数时的训练不稳定问题（梯度振荡和残差爆炸），支持高达12次循环的稳定训练，并在下游任务上提升最高13.2%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped Transformer通过重复使用相同Transformer块来提升性能，但随着循环次数增加会出现训练不稳定问题，作者旨在分析并解决该不稳定的根源。

Method: 提出两种无参数修改：(1) Fully Looped Architecture——将循环间信号分布到所有层以缓解残差爆炸；(2) Attention Injection——复用现有注意力块抑制梯度振荡。

Result: 支持稳定训练至12次循环（基线方法在该设置下崩溃）；在非崩溃的温和设置下，下游任务性能提升最高13.2%。

Conclusion: Fully Looped Transformer提升了训练稳定性、下游性能，并通过推理时调整循环次数提供了测试时计算预算的自适应能力。

Abstract: Scaling model performance typically requires increasing model size. Looped Transformer offers a compelling alternative by iteratively reusing the same Transformer blocks, trading additional computation for improved performance without increasing parameter count or context length. Because the number of loop iterations can be adjusted at inference, it also provides a natural mechanism for balancing performance and test-time compute. However, Looped Transformer still suffers from training instability when the number of loop iterations increases. Our analysis reveals that this instability stems from two sources: gradient oscillation and residual explosion. To address these two problems, we propose the Fully Looped Transformer, which introduces two parameter-free modifications: (1) Fully Looped Architecture, which distributes inter-loop signals across all layers to mitigate residual explosion; (2) Attention Injection, which reuses the existing attention block to suppress gradient oscillation. These modifications stabilize training dynamics, enabling the Fully Looped Transformer to be trained stably up to 12 loop iterations, whereas other baseline looped models collapse in this regime. In milder settings where Looped Transformer does not collapse, Fully Looped Transformer still improves average downstream-task performance by up to 13.2\%. Overall, our experiments demonstrate that Fully Looped Transformer improves training stability, enhances downstream performance, and provides preliminary adaptability under different test-time compute budgets by varying loop iterations at inference.

</details>


### [53] [Accurate Evaluation of Quickest Changepoint Detectors via Non-parametric Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18798)
*Taiki Miyagawa,Akinori F. Ebihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限且不规则序列长度下的快速变化检测问题，提出了基于生存分析的非参数ARL和ADD估计方法（KM-ARL和KM-ADD），通过类比QCD与生存分析建模检测概率，理论证明了渐近无偏性，实验验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ARL和ADD在理论研究和仿真中广泛使用，但在实际数据集中因序列长度有限且不规则而难以应用，亟需能处理截断数据的鲁棒估计方法。

Method: 借鉴生存分析与QCD的类比，利用Kaplan-Meier估计器对截断检测时间进行非参数估计，提出KM-ARL和KM-ADD，并推导了估计偏差界限及渐近无偏性条件。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，KM-ARL和KM-ADD相比传统方法对有限和不规则序列长度更具鲁棒性，改善了可解释性并支持经验性模型选择。

Conclusion: 所提非参数估计方法有效弥合了QCD理论与实际应用之间的差距，为实践者提供了可直接使用的Python工具。

Abstract: We propose non-parametric estimators for the average run length (ARL) and average detection delay (ADD) in quickest changepoint detection (QCD) under finite and irregular sequence lengths. Although ARL and ADD are widely used as optimality criteria in theoretical and simulation studies, their application to real-world datasets is hindered by limited and irregular sequence lengths. To address this issue, we propose non-parametric estimators for the ARL and ADD, termed KM-ARL and KM-ADD, by drawing an analogy between QCD and survival analysis to model detection probabilities under sequence truncation. We derive estimation bias bounds and prove that they are asymptotically unbiased unless extrapolation is required. Experiments on simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate their practical utility, enhancing robustness against limited and irregular sequence lengths, improving interpretability, and facilitating empirical, intuitive model selection. Our Python code is provided at https://github.com/TaikiMiyagawa/Kaplan-Meier-Average-Run-Length, offering ready-to-use implementations for practitioners.

</details>


### [54] [ReCrit: Transition-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Scientific Critic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18799)
*Wanghan Xu,Yuhao Zhou,Hengyuan Zhao,Shuo Li,Dianzhi Yu,Zhenfei Yin,Yaowen Hu,Fengli Xu,Wanli Ouyang,Wenlong Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCrit框架，将大模型与用户的批评交互建模为初始到批评状态的正确性转换问题，通过四象限奖励机制（纠正、谄媚、鲁棒性、边界）和动态异步rollout策略，在三个科学推理基准上将批评准确率平均提升10-13个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在与用户批评交互时不仅会答错，还可能在原本正确的情况下因用户批评而放弃正确解答，这在科学推理场景中尤其危险。现有方法仅关注最终答案准确率，无法捕捉交互过程中的正确性转换问题。

Method: 提出ReCrit框架：1) 将初始到批评行为分解为四个象限（纠正、谄媚、鲁棒性、边界）；2) 设计转换感知的奖励函数，奖励纠正和鲁棒性、惩罚谄媚、将持续错误视为弱边界信号；3) 使用动态异步rollout与尾部自适应补全策略减少训练等待时间。

Result: 在ChemBench、TRQA、EarthSE三个科学推理基准上，Qwen3.5-4B的平均批评准确率从38.15提升至51.49，Qwen3.5-9B从45.40提升至55.59。消融实验表明最终答案奖励几乎无交互增益，而转换感知奖励和四象限权重能产生更可区分的训练信号。

Conclusion: 转换感知强化学习框架能有效提升大模型在科学推理中面对批评时的鲁棒性，区分有益纠正与有害谄媚是关键，动态异步rollout使交互训练变得实用。

Abstract: Large language models can fail in critic interaction not only by answering incorrectly, but also by abandoning an initially correct scientific solution after user criticism. This is especially risky in scientific reasoning, where user criticism can turn a valid answer into an incorrect one. We frame critic interaction as an inter-turn correctness-transition problem rather than a final-answer accuracy problem, and identify three challenges: transition awareness, decoupling useful correction from harmful sycophancy, and scalable rollout. We propose ReCrit, a transition-aware reinforcement learning framework that decomposes Initial-to-Critic behavior into four quadrants: Correction, Sycophancy, Robustness, and Boundary. ReCrit rewards correction and robustness, penalizes sycophancy, and treats persistent errors as weak boundary signals. To make interaction training practical, ReCrit further uses dynamic asynchronous rollout with tail-adaptive completion to reduce rollout waiting. On three scientific reasoning benchmarks, ChemBench, TRQA, and EarthSE, ReCrit improves average Critic accuracy from 38.15 to 51.49 on Qwen3.5-4B and from 45.40 to 55.59 on Qwen3.5-9B. Ablations show that final-answer rewards provide little interaction-level gain, while transition-aware rewards and quadrant weighting produce more distinguishable training signals and larger net Critic-stage improvement. The code is available at https://github.com/black-yt/ReCrit .

</details>


### [55] [Theory-optimal Quantization Based on Flatness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18800)
*Xiusheng Huang,Zhe Li,Xuanwu Yin,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出双向对角量化（BDQ）框架，通过优化矩阵变换有效分散异常值分布，在LLM后训练量化中取得SOTA性能，首次实现LLaMA-3-8B模型W4A4量化精度下降<1%，在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B的W2A4KV16量化中将性能差距缩小39.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM后训练量化面临激活异常值的核心挑战，现有线性变换方法无法彻底消除异常值模式（权值和激活仍存在集中化幅度分布），缺乏对量化误差与异常值之间数学关系的系统性建模。

Method: 1. 建模量化误差与异常值之间的数学关系；2. 提出新指标Flatness量化异常值分布；3. 推导关于Flatness的理论最优解；4. 提出BDQ框架，通过学习优化的对角变换在矩阵维度间战略性分散异常值幅度。

Result: BDQ在LLaMA-3-8B的W4A4量化中精度下降<1%；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B的W2A4KV16量化中，相比SOTA方法将性能差距缩小39.1%。

Conclusion: BDQ通过双向对角量化及其理论分析建立了新的量化基准，有效解决了LLM低比特量化中的异常值问题。

Abstract: Post-training quantization has emerged as a widely adopted technique for compressing and accelerating the inference of Large Language Models (LLMs). The primary challenges in LLMs quantization stem from activation outliers, which significantly degrade model performance especially at lower bit precision. While recent approaches attempt to mitigate outliers through linear transformations across feature dimensions, our analysis reveals that the transformed weights and activations still exhibit persistent outlier patterns with concentrated magnitude distributions. In this paper, we first model the mathematical relationship between quantization error and outliers, and then introduce a new metric Flatness to quantify the distribution of outliers. Based on this, we derive the theoretical optimal solution with respect to Flatness. Building on these insights, we propose Bidirectional Diagonal Quantization (BDQ), a novel post-training quantization framework that effectively disperses outlier patterns through optimized matrix transformations. BDQ strategically distributes outlier magnitudes across matrix dimensions via learned diagonal operations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BDQ establishes a new quantization benchmark. It achieves less than 1\% accuracy drop in W4A4 quantization on the LLaMA-3-8B model. In the more challenging W2A4KV16 experiment, compared to state-of-the-art approaches, BDQ reduces the performance gap by 39.1\% on the DeepSeek-R1-Distill-LLaMA-70B model.

</details>


### [56] [PROWL: Prioritized Regret-Driven Optimization for World Model Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18803)
*Ahmet H. Güzel,Jenny Seidenschwarz,Benjamin Graham,Jonathan Sadeghi,Jeffrey Hawke,Jack Parker-Holder,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种KL约束的对抗课程学习框架（PROWL），通过训练策略主动暴露扩散世界模型的高误差轨迹，并持续微调模型，配合优先对抗轨迹缓冲池（PAT）聚焦未解决的失败模式，从而提升世界模型在稀有交互关键转换上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动演示数据系统性欠采样高影响交互区域，导致现有动作条件视频世界模型在稀有但关键的转换上不可靠，而下游规划与策略性能高度依赖这些转换。

Method: 1) KL约束对抗课程：训练策略在接近行为分布的同时暴露世界模型高误差轨迹；2) 对抗训练循环：在对抗发现的轨迹上持续微调世界模型；3) 优先对抗轨迹缓冲池（PAT）：基于预测误差、动作保真度和学习进度重新排序轨迹，聚焦未解决的失败模式。

Result: 在MineRL框架上的OOD轨迹评估中，PROWL相比仅使用被动数据训练的模型显著提升了鲁棒性，揭示了弱行为约束下的奖励破解行为，并证明平衡探索性失败发现与显式行为正则化至关重要。

Conclusion: 可扩展世界模型不仅受益于更大数据集，更有赖于选择性生成信息丰富的训练数据。

Abstract: Modern action-conditioned video world models achieve strong short-horizon visual realism, yet remain unreliable on rare, interaction-critical transitions that dominate downstream planning and policy performance. Because passive demonstration data systematically under-samples these high-impact regimes, improving robustness requires actively eliciting model failures rather than relying on their natural occurrence. We introduce a KL-constrained adversarial curriculum in which a policy is trained to expose high-error trajectories of a diffusion-based world model while remaining close to the behavior distribution. The world model is continuously fine-tuned on these adversarially discovered trajectories, yielding an adversarial training loop that converts rare failures into a stable, near-distribution training signal without drifting into out-of-distribution exploitation. To maintain pressure on unresolved weaknesses as the model improves, we propose a Prioritized Adversarial Trajectory (PAT) buffer that re-ranks trajectories based on prediction error, action fidelity, and learning progress, focusing training on unresolved failure modes rather than repeatedly revisiting solved cases. We implement our approach in the MineRL framework and evaluate it on held-out out-of-distribution trajectories; PROWL improves robustness over models trained on passive data alone, reveals reward-hacking behaviors under weak behavioral constraints, and demonstrates that effective adversarial world-model training critically depends on balancing exploratory failure discovery with explicit behavioral regularization. Our results suggest that scalable world models benefit not only from larger datasets, but also from selectively generating informative training data.

</details>


### [57] [Block-Based Double Decoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18807)
*Asher Labovich,Benjamin Bradley,Vanessa Alexander,Chaitanya Harsha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于块的双解码器Transformer架构，采用双重因果块级注意力掩码，在预训练时实现完全损失监督和静态序列打包，结合了解码器-only的训练效率与编码器-解码器的推理效率，在规模法则实验中优于编码器-解码器，推理时KV缓存和逐token计算量减少至少2/3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码器-解码器模型在推理时相比解码器-only模型有显著效率优势，但其预训练目标存在稀疏监督和动态序列长度的问题，导致难以大规模应用。

Method: 提出基于块的双解码器架构，使用双重因果块级注意力掩码，实现完全损失监督和静态序列打包，兼顾解码器-only的训练效率与编码器-解码器的推理效率。

Result: 在规模法则实验中，块双解码器显著优于编码器-解码器，性能接近解码器-only模型；推理时KV缓存和逐token计算量减少至少2/3，且不影响预填充缓存等现有推理优化。

Conclusion: 块双解码器有效弥合了编码器-解码器与解码器-only模型之间的差距，既保持了训练效率又获得了推理效率优势，是一种可大规模部署的Transformer架构。

Abstract: Encoder-decoder models offer substantial inference-time savings over decoder-only models, but their pretraining objectives suffer from sparse supervision and dynamic sequence lengths, keeping them out of practice at scale. We propose block-based double decoders, a novel transformer architecture that utilizes doubly-causal block-based attention masks to train with full loss supervision and static sequence packing, combining decoder-only training efficiency with encoder-decoder inference efficiency. In scaling law experiments, block-based double decoders strongly outperform encoder-decoders and closely track decoder-only models across scales. At inference time, they cut KV-cache memory and per-token compute by at least 2/3 without sacrificing prefill caching or other existing inference optimizations available to decoder-only models.

</details>


### [58] [Compositional Literary Primitives in Instruction-Tuned LLMs: Cross-Architectural SAE Features for Self, Style, and Affect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18808)
*Joao Paulo Cavalcante Presa,Savio Salvarino Teles de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器在两种指令微调大语言模型（Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct 和 Gemma 2 9B-IT）的中层残差流中，发现了文学原语的组合架构，包括命名门控特征、第一人称自特征、文体风格调节器以及组合情感特征。通过5-LLM评委面板在27类情感分类任务上的验证，Llama达到全覆盖，Gemma达到23/27，并揭示了跨架构的非对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型内部是否具有可组合的文学性原始特征（literary primitives），并理解这些特征如何通过组合产生复杂的情感表达，以及不同架构之间的差异。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoders）对两个指令微调LLM（Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct 和 Gemma 2 9B-IT）的中层残差流进行特征提取；采用三阶段验证流水线（logit-lens、LLM-rate、5-LLM评委面板）；使用Cowen-Keltner 27类情感分类体系进行强制选择评判。

Result: Llama通过组合命名门控、多特征配方和单自特征操控达到27/27全覆盖；Gemma达到23/27，崇拜（adoration）为唯一严格失败项。随机评判下每单元通过概率约10^{-3}，假阳性预期可忽略。跨架构不对称性体现在：评委对Llama输出的一致性高于Gemma。两个架构均存在既是语域标记又是情感发射器的自特征。

Conclusion: 大语言模型内部存在可组合的文学原语特征，不同架构在情感表达的显式命名与隐式唤起之间存在差异，且存在一个单一自特征同时承担语域标记和情感发射的双重角色。

Abstract: We characterize a compositional architecture of literary primitives in two instruction-tuned large language models (Llama 3.1 8B-Instruct and Gemma 2 9B-IT) via sparse autoencoders on mid-depth residual streams. Four feature classes emerge: naming-gates that promote lexical tokens of a target affect, an eleven-self cluster of first-person register features, stylistic register modulators (show-don't-tell and defamiliarization), and compositional emotions that arise only from multi-feature steering. Under a forced-choice 5-LLM judge panel applied to a 27-category emotion taxonomy (Cowen-Keltner), Llama reaches full 27/27 coverage by combining naming-gates, multi-feature recipes, and single self-feature steering; Gemma reaches 23/27 with adoration as the single residual strict-fail. Under random judging, the per-cell pass probability is on the order of $10^{-3}$ and the expected number of two-seed false-positive cells across the catalog is negligible, so the observed coverage is not consistent with chance. A cross-architectural asymmetry sits in the strict-versus-soft judge contrast: on the same generations, judges agree more often on Llama outputs than on Gemma outputs because Llama outputs name the target affect more directly while Gemma outputs evoke it through scene and imagery. Both architectures contain self-features that serve simultaneously as register markers and as emotion emitters, including a single most-RLHF-loaded self-feature per architecture that intensifies the institutional Helper-AI persona at one operating regime and produces affect-categorizable output at the same calibrated coefficient. Methodologically, the paper presents a three-stage validation pipeline (logit-lens, LLM-rate, 5-LLM judge) with documented anti-patterns; the total compute is single-GPU and about 15 minutes per emotion-feature discovery cycle.

</details>


### [59] [PASC: Pipeline-Aware Conformal Prediction with Joint Coverage Guarantees for Multi-Stage NLP and LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18812)
*Varun Kotte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PASC提出了一种面向多阶段NLP/LLM流水线的联合覆盖不确定性量化方法，通过将多阶段联合覆盖约简为单个标量共形预测问题，在保证有限样本下无分布假设的K阶段同时覆盖概率≥1-α的同时，比Bonferroni边界更紧且计算更快。实验表明在NER→NED→实体类型流水线上，PASC在相同预测集大小下实现96.4%端到端覆盖（Bonferroni 93.4%，独立CP 86.5%），在分布偏移下保持目标覆盖而独立CP崩溃至59%，并高效扩展至6阶段流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP/LLM流水线中错误会跨阶段累积，但现有不确定性量化方法要么独立校准每个阶段（无联合覆盖保证），要么使用Bonferroni联合界（保守且过紧），缺乏既提供有限样本联合覆盖保证又不保守的高效方法。

Method: 提出Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal (PASC)，将多阶段联合覆盖问题约简为对联合最大非一致性分数（joint maximum nonconformity score）的单个标量共形预测问题，通过单个分位数计算实现K阶段同时覆盖的概率保证，边界紧度仅差1/(n+1)因子。

Result: 在三阶段NER→NED→实体类型流水线（CoNLL-2003）上，PACS实现96.4%端到端覆盖（Bonferroni 93.4%，独立CP 86.5%），相同平均预测集大小1.083；在WNUT-17和WikiNEuRal分布偏移下维持目标覆盖，独立CP降至59%；计算速度比Bonferroni快1.7倍，可扩展至6阶段（独立CP覆盖降至0.53）。

Conclusion: PASC提供了一种高效、紧致、分布自由的联合覆盖保证方法，适用于NER→NED→实体类型等NLP流水线，并可直接推广到复合LLM系统和智能体流水线中。

Abstract: Modern NLP and LLM systems are pipelines: named entity recognition (NER) -> entity disambiguation (NED) -> entity typing, retrieval-augmented generation (retriever -> reader), and agentic chains of planner -> tool -> critic. Errors compound across stages, but existing uncertainty quantification methods either calibrate each stage independently (no joint coverage) or apply a Bonferroni union bound (joint coverage, but conservative). We present PASC (Pipeline-Aware Split Conformal), which reduces multi-stage joint coverage to a single scalar conformal prediction problem on the joint maximum nonconformity score. PASC provides a finite-sample distribution-free guarantee that all K stages are simultaneously covered with probability at least 1 - alpha, and is nearly tight up to a 1/(n+1) factor. On a three-stage NER -> NED -> entity-typing pipeline over CoNLL-2003, PASC achieves 96.4% end-to-end coverage versus 93.4% for Bonferroni and 86.5% for independent CP, at identical average prediction set size (1.083). Under distribution shift to WNUT-17 Twitter and WikiNEuRal Wikipedia data, PASC empirically maintains the target coverage in the tested shift settings while independent CP collapses to 59%. PASC requires a single quantile computation, runs 1.7x faster than Bonferroni, and scales to K = 6 stages where independent CP drops to 0.53 end-to-end coverage. The same joint-maximum-score reduction applies directly to compound LLM systems and agent pipelines.

</details>


### [60] [Composition of Memory Experts for Diffusion World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18813)
*Sebastian Stapf,Pablo Acuaviva Huertos,Aram Davtyan,Paolo Favaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散的专家产品组合框架，通过解耦未来-过去一致性，整合短时记忆、长时记忆和空间长时记忆三种专家模型，克服了Transformer二次注意力和循环模型压缩保真度损失之间的记忆权衡，在模拟和真实基准上提升了时间一致性、历史回忆和导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型架构面临根本性的记忆权衡：Transformer保留局部细节但受二次注意力限制，循环和状态空间模型扩展更高效但以压缩历史为代价损失保真度。需要一种方法在不产生二次计算成本的情况下兼顾局部细节和长期记忆。

Method: 将未来-过去一致性从单一架构中解耦，利用对比专家产品公式整合异构记忆模型。具体实例化三种互补专家：短时记忆专家捕捉精细局部动态；长时记忆专家通过轻量测试时微调将情节历史存储到外部扩散权重中；空间长时记忆专家强制执行几何和空间一致性。采用扩散框架实现组合设计。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准上，该方法在时间一致性、过去观察回忆和导航性能方面均优于现有方法，避免了模式崩溃，能够扩展到长上下文而不产生二次成本。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种构建和操作记忆增强扩散世界模型的新范式，通过专家组合设计有效解决了记忆权衡问题。

Abstract: World models aim to predict plausible futures consistent with past observations, a capability central to planning and decision-making in reinforcement learning. Yet, existing architectures face a fundamental memory trade-off: transformers preserve local detail but are bottlenecked by quadratic attention, while recurrent and state-space models scale more efficiently but compress history at the cost of fidelity. To overcome this trade-off, we suggest decoupling future-past consistency from any single architecture and instead leveraging a set of specialized experts. We introduce a diffusion-based framework that integrates heterogeneous memory models through a contrastive product-of-experts formulation. Our approach instantiates three complementary roles: a short-term memory expert that captures fine local dynamics, a long-term memory expert that stores episodic history in external diffusion weights via lightweight test-time finetuning, and a spatial long-term memory expert that enforces geometric and spatial coherence. This compositional design avoids mode collapse and scales to long contexts without incurring a quadratic cost. Across simulated and real-world benchmarks, our method improves temporal consistency, recall of past observations, and navigation performance, establishing a novel paradigm for building and operating memory-augmented diffusion world models.

</details>


### [61] [DynaTrain: Fast Online Parallelism Switching for Elastic LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18815)
*Yuanqing Wang,Yuchen Zhang,Hao Lin,Junhao Hu,Chunyang Zhu,Quanlu Zhang,Boxun Li,Guohao Dai,Zhi Yang,Daning Cheng,Yunquan Zhang,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DynaTrain是一个分布式训练系统，通过虚拟参数空间(VPS)抽象实现亚秒级在线重配置，支持任意多维并行布局的动态切换，在70B稠密模型和235B MoE模型上分别实现2秒和4.36秒的重配置时间，比现有系统快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练框架基于静态执行模型构建，无法应对LLM训练中资源波动、RLHF阶段切换和集群弹性变化带来的动态并行布局优化需求。

Method: 提出虚拟参数空间(VPS)抽象统一所有分布式训练状态，将并行配置映射为确定性映射并转化为几何交集；设计状态路由与传输层实现内存感知、无死锁的rank本地传输；弹性设备管理器将新世界构建与正在进行的训练重叠以隐藏拓扑变化开销。

Result: 在高达235B参数的稠密和MoE模型上，DynaTrain重配置70B稠密模型耗时不到2秒，235B MoE模型耗时4.36秒，性能比现有基于检查点和弹性系统高出三个数量级，同时保持正确性。

Conclusion: DynaTrain通过VPS抽象和在线重配置机制，有效解决了动态训练环境下的并行布局自适应问题，显著提升了大规模LLM训练的效率与灵活性。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) training is inherently dynamic: resource fluctuations, RLHF phase shifts, and cluster elasticity continually reshape the optimal parallelism layout, posing a significant challenge to existing training frameworks built around a static execution model. We present DynaTrain, a distributed training system for sub-second, online reconfiguration across arbitrary multi-dimensional parallelism. At its core, we propose a Virtual Parameter Space (VPS) abstraction that unifies all distributed training states under one logical coordinate space, turning any parallelism configuration into a deterministic mapping and collapsing complex transition into manageable geometric intersections. On top of VPS, a state routing-and-transition layer executes rank-local transfers under a memory-aware, deadlock-free schedule, and an Elastic Device Manager overlaps new-world construction with ongoing training to mask topology-change cost. On dense and MoE models up to 235B parameters, DynaTrain reconfigures a 70B dense model in under 2s and a 235B MoE model in 4.36s, outperforming state-of-the-art checkpoint-based and elastic systems by up to three orders of magnitude while preserving correctness.

</details>


### [62] [Emergence of Frontier Superposition: Möbius attractor and Cascade Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18820)
*Hongyu Gu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Erdős-Rényi图上，通过Möbius吸引子（架构贡献）和级联监督（监督贡献）的组合，梯度下降能够学习到Transformer的叠加推理（superposition reasoning），从而在常数深度前向传播中并行执行图可达性推理，而无需展开链式思维令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhu等人(2025)手工构造了用于图可达性的等权重广度优先叠加状态，但梯度下降能否在置换对称鞍点中找到这一目标仍是开放问题。本文旨在弥合这一理论空白。

Method: (1) 架构上：识别出Möbius吸引子，在S_n对称性和树型机制下，层间动力学约化为1D Möbius映射，其零集是包含等权重叠加状态的余维一全局最优流形。(2) 监督上：提出级联监督（Cascade Supervision），其反向传播同时提供选择性自举、跨深度梯度持久性和逐步骤判别性。

Result: 端到端监督因内部梯度在扇出维度上以(np)^{-(D-c-2)/2}衰减而失败；级联监督成功。在深度D=3时，最终步余弦值：理论预测0.35 vs. 0.71（端到端vs级联），实验验证0.37 vs. 0.69，每步误差均在0.02以内。

Conclusion: Möbius吸引子 + 级联监督 = 叠加推理的涌现。参数无关的衰减定律与实验高度吻合，验证了理论分析的正确性。

Abstract: Superposition allows Transformers to reason in depth, carrying an entire reasoning frontier in parallel through a bounded-depth forward pass instead of unrolling serial chain-of-thought tokens. While Zhu et al. (2025) hand-crafted an equal-weight breadth-first frontier in a single residual stream for graph reachability, it remained open whether gradient descent could ever find this target amidst permutation-symmetric saddles.
  We close this gap on Reachability-by-Superposition over Erdős-Rényi graphs by isolating architectural and supervisional contributions. Architecturally, we identify a Möbius attractor: under $S_n$-symmetry in the tree regime, layerwise dynamics reduce to a 1D Möbius map whose zero set is a codimension-one manifold of global optima containing the equal-weight superposition state.
  On the supervision side, we identify Cascade Supervision: a loss class whose backward pass simultaneously delivers (A) selectivity bootstrap, (B) gradient persistence across depth, and (C) per-step discrimination (e.g., \mathcal{L}_{sup} and \mathcal{L}_{node}). End-to-end supervision fails condition (B) and is provably insufficient: internal gradients at layer c decay as (np)^{-(D-c-2)/2} in the graph fan-out and stall before the manifold is reached.
  Our thesis: Möbius attractor + Cascade Supervision = emergence of superposition reasoning. The parameter-free decay law predicts a final-step cosine of 0.35 vs. 0.71 (end-to-end vs. cascade) at depth D=3; experiments confirm 0.37 vs. 0.69, matching within 0.02 at every step.

</details>


### [63] [Quantum Adversarial Machine Learning: From Classical Adaptations to Quantum-Native Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18821)
*Roozbeh Razavi-Far,Mohammad Meymani,Erfan Mahmoudinia,Dorsa Vazirzade,Peyman Paknezhad,Fateme Ghasemi,Saeed Saravani,Somayeh Nikkhoo,Kimia Haghjooei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了量子对抗机器学习领域，探讨了量子机器学习模型在面对对抗性攻击时的脆弱性，以及现有的攻击方法和防御策略，并回顾了理论基础、新兴趋势和关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典机器学习模型存在对抗性漏洞，量子机器学习虽有潜力但同样易受攻击，随着量子计算和机器学习的发展，亟需系统研究量子对抗机器学习的脆弱性、攻击方法和防御策略。

Method: 对量子对抗机器学习领域进行系统性综述，整理和分类现有的攻击方法（如量子对抗样本生成）和防御策略（如量子增强防御、对抗训练），并从理论层面分析该领域的基础原理、新兴趋势和关键挑战。

Result: 提供了量子对抗机器学习的详细概览，包括现有攻击手段和防御措施的分类总结，以及该领域理论基础、最新进展和面临的核心问题的系统性梳理。

Conclusion: 量子机器学习虽在复杂任务中展现潜力，但仍面临对抗性威胁；量子对抗机器学习作为新兴领域，亟需更多研究来理解其理论基础并发展有效的防御机制，以推动量子机器学习的安全可靠应用。

Abstract: Machine learning has revolutionized numerous industrial domains. Despite recent advances, machine learning models remain vulnerable to adversarial threats. Adversarial machine learning is a field that studies these vulnerabilities to build robust machine learning models. Quantum machine learning is an interdisciplinary field that bridges quantum computing and classical machine learning. While quantum machine learning shows potentials to outperform classical machine learning in complex tasks such as regression, classification, and generative modeling, it remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Given the recent advancements in quantum computing and machine learning, the quantum adversarial machine learning field has emerged to study the vulnerabilities of quantum machine learning, possible attacks, and novel quantum-enhanced defense strategies. In this survey, we provide a detailed overview on quantum adversarial machine learning and explore the existing attacks and countermeasures. We also review the theoretical underpinnings of this area, emerging trends, and critical challenges.

</details>


### [64] [Hybrid-LoRA: Bridging Full Fine-Tuning and Low-Rank Adaptation for Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18822)
*Chengqian Zhang,Wei Zhu,Kyumin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid-LoRA框架，通过选择性全微调对低秩适应敏感度高的模块（其余模块用LoRA），在10%全微调预算下匹配全微调性能，平均超越最优基线4.36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR后训练中全微调GPU内存高、成本大；现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）虽降低计算成本，但在复杂推理任务后训练中与全微调存在显著性能差距。

Method: 提出Hybrid-LoRA框架：设计Hybrid-LoRA Score指标，按模块对低秩适应的敏感度排序，在固定参数预算下对高敏感度模块进行全微调，其余模块采用LoRA适配。

Result: 在10%全微调模块预算下（其余LoRA），性能接近全微调；一致超越四种SOTA PEFT后训练基线，最高提升5.65%，平均提升4.36%。

Conclusion: Hybrid-LoRA有效弥合了PEFT与全微调在后训练中的性能差距，为LLM复杂推理能力的高效后训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Post-training has become essential for adapting large language models (LLMs) to complex downstream behaviors, including instruction following, preference alignment, and multi-step reasoning. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has recently emerged as a particularly effective post-training paradigm for improving reasoning capabilities, with critic-free algorithms such as GRPO and GSPO enabling scalable optimization. However, RLVR post-training with full fine-tuning (FFT) requires substantial GPU memory and incurs high training costs. Although parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), effectively reduce computational costs, they often suffer from a noticeable performance gap compared to full fine-tuning in post-training for complex reasoning tasks. In this paper, we propose Hybrid-LoRA, an efficient hybrid post-training framework that selectively applies full fine-tuning to a small subset of modules less suited to low-rank adaptation, while adapting the remaining components with LoRA. We introduce a novel Hybrid-LoRA Score to rank candidate modules according to their sensitivity to low-rank adaptation under a fixed parameter budget. Experiments show that Hybrid-LoRA closely matches full fine-tuning performance under a 10% full fine-tuning module budget, with the remaining candidate modules adapted by LoRA, consistently outperforming four state-of-the-art PEFT post-training baselines, achieving improvements of up to 5.65% and on average 4.36% over the best baseline.

</details>


### [65] [Not All Tokens Are Worth Caching: Learning Semantic-Aware Eviction for LLM Prefix Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18825)
*Shaoke Fang,Ziang Li,Wenfei Wu,Jiatong Ji,Qingsong Liu,Ruizhi Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAECache是一种语义自适应的前缀缓存淘汰策略，通过多队列架构、语义感知token加权和全自适应在线学习，在LLM推理服务中实现1.4-2.7倍的TTFT提升，并避免固定参数策略因工作负载不匹配导致的性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前缀缓存淘汰策略（如LRU）将所有缓存块等同对待，忽略了LLM提示中不同token类型（系统提示、用户查询、工具输出、模型响应、思维链推理等）在复用率上存在高达756倍的差异，导致GPU显存利用效率低下。

Method: (1) 多队列架构：将KV块路由到任务特定队列，配备定制化优先级指标，捕获多轮请求中的会话复用和模板化单轮请求中的结构复用；(2) 语义感知token加权机制：通过淘汰反馈在线学习不同token类型的复用价值；(3) 全自适应在线学习架构：自动更新对数正态时序参数、位置衰减幂次、队列权重和元参数，消除手动调参并自动适应部署工作负载特征。

Result: 在异构工作负载下，SAECache相比生产级基线实现1.4-2.7倍的TTFT（首token生成时间）提升；固定参数策略在工作负载不匹配时性能可退化高达2.7倍，而SAECache的完全自适应方法避免了此类失效模式。

Conclusion: SAECache通过语义感知的自适应淘汰策略有效解决了LLM前缀缓存中token复用率差异巨大的问题，在避免手动调参的同时显著提升了缓存效率和服务性能。

Abstract: Prefix caching is a key optimization in Large Language Model (LLM) serving, reusing attention Key-Value (KV) states across requests with shared prompt prefixes to reduce expensive prefill computation. However, its benefit depends critically on the eviction policy as GPU memory is scarce, and existing policies such as LRU largely treat cached blocks uniformly. This view ignores a fundamental property of LLM prompts: not all tokens are equally worth caching. We show that different token types within a prompt, including system prompts, user queries, tool outputs, model responses, and chain-of-thought reasoning, exhibit up to 756x variation in reuse rates, yet no existing eviction policy exploits this signal.
