Result: 在DiT-based MDLM和LLaDA-8B-Instruct上，对WikiText-103、OpenWebText和Alpaca数据集评估表明：SHADOWMASK实现近100%攻击成功率，显著优于标准数据投毒；基本保持干净效用；在全模型和参数高效微调下仍然有效；对代表性防御方法具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SHADOWMASK是首个针对MDLM的训练时后门攻击，展示了MDLM面临的安全威胁，为未来防御研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are emerging as a compelling new paradigm for text generation, but their training-time security remains largely unexplored. Existing backdoor attacks on Gaussian diffusion models or autoregressive language models do not directly apply to MDLMs because MDLMs rely on discrete state corruption and iterative denoising rather than continuous noising or left-to-right prediction. In this work, we present the first systematic study of training-time backdoor attacks on MDLMs. We propose SHADOWMASK, a backdoor attack that modifies the MDLM forward corruption process by replacing the standard all-mask terminal distribution with a trigger-mask mixture prior. This creates a dedicated denoising pathway from trigger-corrupted states to attacker-specified targets while preserving clean denoising behavior. We further provide a principled mathematical formulation by defining the backdoored forward process, deriving the reverse-time posterior, and obtaining the continuous-time training objective. Evaluations on DiT-based MDLM and LLaDA-8B-Instruct across WikiText-103, OpenWebText, and Alpaca show that SHADOWMASK achieves near-100% attack success, substantially outperforms standard data poisoning, largely preserves clean utility, remains effective under full-model and parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and is robust against representative defenses.

</details>


### [137] [From Simple to Complex: Curriculum-Guided Physics-Informed Neural Networks via Gaussian Mixture Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19263)
*Jianan Yang,Yiran Wang,Shuai Li,Fujun Cao,Xuefei Yan,Junmin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CGMPINN，通过高斯混合模型动态拟合PDE残差分布，结合课程学习策略逐步从易到难训练，在六个基准PDE上相对L2误差最高降低97.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINN在强非线性、尖锐梯度或多尺度问题上存在梯度病态、谱偏差和收敛性差的问题。

Method: 周期性地用GMM拟合PDE残差分布以量化空间学习难度；设计平滑课程调度逐步从易区过渡到难区；基于精度的方差调制在早期优化中抑制不可靠聚类；双课程由共享课程参数控制，可结合自适应损失平衡。

Result: 在六个基准PDE上，CGMPINN一致达到最低相对L2误差和最大绝对误差，相对L2误差较标准PINN最高降低97.8%，且计算成本相当。

Conclusion: CGMPINN有效解决了PINN在复杂PDE问题中的训练困难，具有理论收敛保证和泛化界，代码已开源。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) offer a mesh-free framework for solving partial differential equations (PDEs), yet training often suffers from gradient pathologies, spectral bias, and poor convergence, especially for problems with strong nonlinearity, sharp gradients, or multiscale features. We propose the Curriculum-Guided Gaussian Mixture Physics-Informed Neural Network (CGMPINN), which integrates Gaussian mixture modeling with dynamic curriculum learning. Specifically, a GMM is periodically fitted to the PDE residual distribution to quantify spatially varying learning difficulty. A smooth curriculum schedule progressively shifts training focus from easy to harder regions, while precision-based variance modulation suppresses unreliable clusters during early optimization. This dual curriculum is governed by a shared curriculum parameter and can be combined with self-adaptive loss balancing. We further establish theoretical guarantees, including sublinear convergence of the gradient norm for the induced time-varying loss, uniform equivalence between the curriculum-weighted and standard PDE losses, and a generalization bound with an explicit weighting-induced bias characterization. Experiments on six benchmark PDEs spanning elliptic, parabolic, hyperbolic, advection-dominated, and nonlinear reaction-diffusion types show that CGMPINN consistently achieves the lowest relative $L_2$ and maximum absolute errors among all compared methods, reducing relative $L_2$ error by up to 97.8\% over the standard PINN at comparable cost. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Mathematics-Yang/CGMPINN.

</details>


### [138] [CODA: Rewriting Transformer Blocks as GEMM-Epilogue Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19269)
*Han Guo,Jack Zhang,Arjun Menon,Driss Guessous,Vijay Thakkar,Yoon Kim,Tri Dao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CODA是一种GPU内核抽象，将Transformer中的内存受限操作（归一化、激活、残差更新等）表达为GEMM-plus-epilogue程序，通过在GEMM输出块写回内存前在芯片上执行这些操作，消除了全局内存的数据移动开销，兼顾了生产效率和硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer训练系统中，除密集线性代数外的内存受限操作（归一化、激活、残差更新等）频繁在全局内存中移动大中间张量，数据移动成为高度优化的训练栈中日益重要的瓶颈。

Method: 提出CODA内核抽象，将Transformer算子代数重参数化为GEMM-plus-epilogue程序。抽象固定GEMM主循环，暴露少量可组合的epilogue原语（缩放、归约、逐对变换、累加），使得这些计算在GEMM输出块写回内存前在芯片上完成。

Result: 无论是人工编写还是LLM编写的CODA内核，在代表性Transformer工作负载上均实现了高性能，覆盖了标准Transformer块前向和反向传播中几乎所有非注意力计算。

Conclusion: GEMM-plus-epilogue编程范式提供了一条兼顾框架级生产力和硬件级效率的实用路径。

Abstract: Transformer training systems are built around dense linear algebra, yet a nontrivial fraction of end-to-end time is spent on surrounding memory-bound operators. Normalization, activations, residual updates, reductions, and related computations repeatedly move large intermediate tensors through global memory while performing little arithmetic, making data movement an increasingly important bottleneck in otherwise highly optimized training stacks. We introduce CODA, a GPU kernel abstraction that expresses these computations as GEMM-plus-epilogue programs. CODA is based on the observation that many Transformer operators exposed as separate framework kernels can be algebraically reparameterized to execute while a GEMM output tile remains on chip, before it is written to memory. The abstraction fixes the GEMM mainloop and exposes a small set of composable epilogue primitives for scaling, reductions, pairwise transformations, and accumulation. This constrained interface preserves the performance structure of expert-written GEMMs while remaining expressive enough to cover nearly all non-attention computation in the forward and backward pass of a standard Transformer block. Across representative Transformer workloads, both human- and LLM-authored CODA kernels achieve high performance, suggesting that GEMM-plus-epilogue programming offers a practical path toward combining framework-level productivity with hardware-level efficiency.

</details>


### [139] [EviTrack: Selection over Sampling for Delayed Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19283)
*Omer Haq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对延迟消歧场景中序列预测的挑战，本文提出EviTrack框架，通过维护轨迹级假设并基于证据和似然比进行选择，在匹配推理预算下显著优于采样基线方法，证明了在延迟消歧场景中轨迹级选择比增加采样覆盖更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在延迟消歧（delayed disambiguation）场景中，早期观测具有歧义性，多种潜在解释在足够证据累积前均保持合理。标准边际推理方法在此场景下表现不佳，要么过早坍缩不确定性，要么在证据到达后无法恢复。

Method: 提出EviTrack，一种测试时推理框架，在轨迹级（而非边际状态级）上进行操作。维护一组竞争性轨迹假设，基于证据和似然比进行选择，延迟决策直到数据支持，借鉴多假设跟踪（MHT）和检测前跟踪（track-before-detect）的假设管理思想。

Result: 在具有已知潜在真实状态的受控合成基准上，在匹配推理预算下，EviTrack显著优于采样基线方法，实现更快的消歧后恢复。

Conclusion: 在延迟消歧场景中，适度的轨迹级选择比增加采样覆盖更有效，突出了"选择胜过采样"（selection over sampling）作为可靠序列推理的关键原则。

Abstract: Sequential prediction is challenging in regimes of delayed disambiguation, where early observations are ambiguous and multiple latent explanations remain plausible until sufficient evidence accumulates. Standard approaches based on marginal inference struggle in this setting, either collapsing uncertainty prematurely or failing to recover once informative evidence arrives.
  We introduce EviTrack, a test-time inference framework that operates over latent trajectories rather than marginal states. EviTrack maintains a set of competing trajectory hypotheses and applies evidence- and likelihood-ratio-based selection to delay commitment until supported by data, drawing inspiration from hypothesis management in multiple hypothesis tracking and track-before-detect.
  To evaluate this setting, we construct a controlled synthetic benchmark with known latent ground truth that explicitly exhibits delayed disambiguation. At matched inference budget, EviTrack substantially outperforms sampling-based baselines, achieving faster post-disambiguation recovery.
  These results show that, in delayed disambiguation regimes, moderate trajectory-level selection is more effective than increasing sampling coverage, highlighting selection over sampling as a key principle for reliable sequential inference.

</details>


### [140] [An Objective Performance Evaluation of the LSTM Networks in Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19311)
*Sooraj Sunil,Balakumar Balasingam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了LSTM分类器与基于模型的EM分类器在二进制时间序列分类任务中的表现。在标量线性高斯状态空间模型下，EM分类器在数据符合模型假设时表现优异，而LSTM分类器需要更大的噪声统计差异才能实现可靠分类，且在仅测量噪声不同时性能饱和于参考分类器之下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习在时间序列分析中被广泛采用，但在具有明确物理规律的结构化环境中，数据驱动模型与基于模型的方法之间的性能对比缺乏客观评估。

Method: 构建了一个性能评估框架，在标量线性高斯状态空间模型上对比LSTM分类器与基于期望最大化（EM）的分类器，以卡尔曼滤波似然比检验作为最优性能参考，通过蒙特卡洛模拟从任务难度、序列长度和训练集大小三个维度进行评估。

Result: EM分类器在数据符合模型假设时表现强劲；LSTM分类器需要更大的噪声统计差异才能可靠分类；当两模型仅在测量噪声上存在差异时，LSTM性能无法达到参考分类器水平，且不随序列长度或训练集大小增加而改善。

Conclusion: 在结构化环境中，基于模型的EM分类器优于数据驱动的LSTM分类器，尤其是当数据符合模型假设时。LSTM在区分精细的噪声统计差异方面存在根本性局限。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of deep learning has increasingly led to data-driven models replacing classical model-based algorithms, even in domains governed by well-understood physical laws. While data-driven models, such as long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, have become a popular choice for time-series analysis, their performance relative to model-based approaches in structured environments is rarely evaluated objectively. This paper presents a performance evaluation framework comparing an LSTM classifier against a model-based expectation maximization (EM) classifier for binary time-series classification. The evaluation is conducted on two scalar linear Gaussian state space models differing only in their noise statistics, where the Kalman filter likelihood ratio test with true parameters serves as a reference for the best achievable classification performance.Through Monte Carlo simulations, the classifiers are evaluated across three axes: task difficulty, controlled by the separation in process or measurement noise between the two models; sequence length; and training dataset size. The results show that the EM classifier, which exploits the known model structure, performs strongly when the data conform to the assumed model class. The LSTM classifier requires a larger separation in noise statistics to achieve reliable classification, and its performance saturates below the reference classifier when the models differ only in measurement noise, regardless of sequence length or training dataset size.

</details>


### [141] [Inference-Time Scaling in Diffusion Models through Iterative Partial Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19317)
*Taegu Kang,Jaesik Yoon,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代部分精炼（IPR）方法，一种无需外部验证器的推理时扩展策略，通过重新加噪并再生部分区域来改进顺序扩散模型的全局一致性，在MNIST数独任务上将有效解率从55.8%提升至75.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的推理时扩展方法通常依赖外部验证器或奖励模型来筛选样本，限制了其可扩展性；此外，针对顺序扩散中区域级混合噪声条件的推理时扩展尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出迭代部分精炼（IPR），对已生成样本的部分区域重新加噪，然后以剩余区域为条件重新生成这些区域，使模型能在比初始生成时更丰富的上下文下修正早期决策。

Result: 在需要全局约束满足的推理任务上，MNIST数独的有效解率从55.8%提升至75.0%。

Conclusion: 迭代部分精炼本身可作为顺序混合噪声设置下扩散模型的有效推理时扩展策略，无需外部验证器。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling has emerged as a major approach for improving reasoning capabilities, and has been increasingly applied to diffusion models. However, existing inference-time scaling methods for diffusion models typically rely on external verifiers or reward models to rank and select samples, limiting their scalability to settings where such evaluators are available and reliable. Moreover, while recent diffusion models perform sequential inference with region-wise, mixed-noise conditioning, inference-time scaling tailored to this setting remains relatively underexplored. We propose Iterative Partial Refinement (IPR), an inference-time scaling method for sequential diffusion that requires no external verifier. Starting from an already-generated sample, IPR re-noises a subset of regions and regenerates them conditioned on the remaining regions, enabling the model to revise earlier decisions under a richer context than was available during the initial generation. This iterative partial refinement produces more globally consistent samples without external verification. On reasoning tasks requiring global constraint satisfaction, IPR consistently improves performance: on MNIST Sudoku, the valid solution rate increases from 55.8% to 75.0%. These results show that iterative partial refinement alone can serve as an effective inference-time scaling strategy for diffusion models in sequential, mixed-noise settings. Code is available at: https://github.com/ahn-ml/IPR

</details>


### [142] [An Exterior Method for Nonnegative Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19325)
*Qiujing Lu,Tonmoy Monsoor,Ehsan Ebrahimzadeh,Kartik Sharma,Vwani Roychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非负矩阵分解的外部优化框架（eNMF），将低秩近似与非负性约束分离，通过最优无约束分解加旋转投影的方法，在400个实验中99%的算法收敛到等价因子矩阵，并在9种算法、9种初始化方案的对比中实现最高30%的重构误差降低和150%的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMF方法在优化过程中强制保持可行性（内点法），这会在非凸景观中阻碍进展，导致收敛缓慢或收敛到次优驻点。

Method: 提出外部框架eNMF：先从无约束最优分解初始化，再通过旋转过程将无约束因子映射到最接近非负象限的外部点，通过简单迭代更新收敛到KKT驻点。

Result: 在400个NMF实验中，99%的情况下不同算法收敛到等价因子矩阵；在9种算法×9种初始化方案的对比中，eNMF在等时间设置下重构误差降低30%，等误差设置下速度提升150%。

Conclusion: 外部优化框架不仅性能优于现有方法，还提供了NMF解的几何解释，明确了置换和正交变换下的因子分解等价类，在音频处理和推荐任务中也有实际收益。

Abstract: Nonnegative matrix factorization (NMF) seeks a low-rank approximation $X \approx UV^T$ with nonnegative factors and is commonly solved using interior methods that enforce feasibility throughout optimization. We show that such constraint-driven approaches can impede progress in the nonconvex landscape, leading to slow convergence or convergence to suboptimal stationary points. We propose an exterior framework for NMF (eNMF) that separates low-rank approximation from nonnegativity enforcement. Our method initializes from the optimal unconstrained factorization and introduces a rotation procedure that maps unconstrained factors to an exterior point closest to the nonnegative orthant. This viewpoint yields an algorithmic framework in which simple iterative updates converge to KKT-satisfying stationary points on the boundary of the positive orthant. The exterior formulation also enables a geometric interpretation of NMF solutions, clarifying equivalence classes of factorizations under permutation and orthogonal transformations. An intriguing numerical result, involving 400 NMF experiments across both real and synthetic datasets, show that in 99% of the cases, different algorithms tend to converge towards equivalent factor matrices. We benchmark eNMF against 9 state-of-the-art NMF algorithms with 9 initialization schemes across 3 real-world and 2 synthetic datasets. eNMF consistently outperforms all 81 competitors, achieving up to 30% lower reconstruction error under equal-time settings and up to 150% speedup under equal-error settings. The downstream experiments further demonstrate substantial performance gains in audio processing and recommendation tasks, corroborating the practical benefits of the proposed exterior optimization framework. Code is available at https://github.com/roychowdhuryresearch/eNMF

</details>


### [143] [What Makes a Representation Good for Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19343)
*Wenkang Jiang,Yuhang Liu,Yichao Cai,Erdun Gao,Jiayi Dong,Ehsan Abbasnejad,Lina Yao,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PerturbedVAE框架，通过显式分离扰动特异信号与不变信息，解决基因表达中扰动信号稀疏且被主导性不变信息掩盖的问题，在单细胞扰动建模中实现SOTA性能，并提供可辨识性分析保证稀疏扰动效应的可靠恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞扰动建模方法（从因果表征学习到基础模型）未能有效处理基因表达中扰动不变信息占主导、扰动特异信号稀疏的不平衡问题，导致学到的表征要么纠缠不变与扰动信息（产生虚假、不可泛化的预测器），要么完全压制扰动信号（无法用于预测）。

Method: 提出PerturbedVAE框架，显式将扰动特异信息与占主导的不变结构分离，恢复因果表征以有效利用扰动信息进行预测；同时提供可辨识性分析，刻画稀疏扰动效应可被可靠恢复的条件，指导框架在该条件下的具体实现。

Result: 在广泛使用的基准数据集上，PerturbedVAE在多个评估设置中达到SOTA性能，在分布外组合预测任务上取得显著提升，并发现了可解释的扰动响应程序。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离扰动信号与不变信息并辅以可辨识性理论保障，PerturbedVAE有效解决了单细胞扰动建模中的信号不平衡问题，为扰动预测提供了更可靠且可解释的方案。

Abstract: Single-cell perturbation modeling is fundamental for understanding and predicting cellular responses to genetic perturbations. However, existing approaches, from causal representation learning to foundation models, often struggle with an overlooked challenge: gene expression is dominated by perturbation-invariant information, while perturbation-specific signals are intrinsically sparse. As a result, learned representations either entangle invariant and perturbation-specific information, leading to spurious and non-generalizable predictors, or suppress perturbation-specific signals altogether, rendering them ineffective for prediction. To address this, we propose PerturbedVAE, a general framework designed to resolve this signal imbalance. The framework explicitly separates perturbation-specific information from dominant invariant structure and recovers causal representations to effectively utilize such information for prediction. We further provide an identifiability analysis that characterizes the conditions under which sparse perturbation effects can be reliably recovered, thereby clarifying how the framework can be concretely specified under such conditions. Empirically, PerturbedVAE achieves state-of-the-art performance on a widely used benchmark across multiple evaluation settings, yielding significant gains on out-of-distribution combinatorial predictions and uncovering interpretable perturbation-response programs.

</details>


### [144] [Accurate, Efficient, and Explainable Deep Learning Approaches for Environmental Science Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19366)
*Jimeng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文开发了三种基于AI的方法（WaLeF/FIDLAr用于洪水预测与管理、CoDiCast用于全球天气概率预报、Hypercube-RAG用于环境科学问答），以应对环境科学中大数据、复杂物理过程及知识幻觉等挑战，实现环境智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境科学依赖于大规模异构数据，传统基于物理的模型计算密集、难以实时应用；大语言模型在领域问答中易产生幻觉。亟需开发高效、准确、可解释的AI方法以应对这些挑战。

Method: 1) 提出深度学习模型WaLeF用于水位预测，以及基于预测的水位管理模型FIDLAr；2) 提出CoDiCast，一种基于条件扩散模型的概率天气预测方法；3) 提出Hypercube-RAG，基于结构化文本立方体框架的检索增强生成方法。

Result: FIDLAr在佛罗里达州南部的沿海洪水系统中在准确性和效率上均优于基线；CoDiCast实现了准确高效的概率天气预报并提供明确的不确定性量化；Hypercube-RAG同时实现了准确性、效率和可解释性。

Conclusion: AI方法能够有效解决环境科学中的大数据处理、实时预测和知识问答等核心问题，实现环境智能。

Abstract: Environmental science plays a pivotal role in safeguarding ecosystems, a domain driven by large-scale, heterogeneous data. In the big data era, artificial intelligence (AI) has emerged as a transformative tool for learning patterns and supporting decision-making. This dissertation develops AI-based approaches tailored to complex environmental science problems to achieve Environmental Intelligence, studying three specific challenges. First, we focus on flood prediction and management in coastal river systems. Conventional physics-based models are computationally intensive, limiting real-time application. To overcome this, we propose a deep learning (DL)-based model, WaLeF, for water level forecasting, and a forecast-informed DL model, FIDLAr, to manage water levels. Evaluated in a flood-prone coastal system in South Florida characterized by extreme rainfall and sea level fluctuations, FIDLAr outperforms baselines in accuracy and efficiency while providing interpretable outputs. Second, we target global weather prediction, which is challenged by massive data scale. Traditional physics methods are deterministic and computationally heavy. We propose CoDiCast, a conditional diffusion model tailored for probabilistic weather forecasting. Adapted from generative AI for predictive tasks, experiments show CoDiCast achieves accurate, efficient forecasts with explicit uncertainty quantification. Lastly, we address scientific question-answering in environmental science. When answering in-domain questions, large language models (LLMs) often suffer from hallucinations due to out-of-date or limited knowledge. While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) retrieves domain-specific knowledge, existing methods trade off accuracy, efficiency, or explainability. We propose Hypercube-RAG, built on a structured text cube framework, which successfully exhibits all three properties simultaneously.

</details>


### [145] [The Evaluation Game: Beyond Static LLM Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19377)
*Paul Wang,Jade Garcia-Bourrée,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Vincent Corruble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入博弈论框架，将大语言模型的安全对齐建模为评估者（寻找越狱）与训练者（防御微调）之间的两人博弈，利用群作用形式化数据增强。理论分析表明，低于临界阈值时评估者长期保持恒定漏报率；实证发现微调仅带来局部泛化，拒绝率与对抗样本距离高度相关。框架重新定义了基准测试：不是静态提示集，而是群作用下的轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越狱攻击不断被发现，但防御性微调的理论基础尚不清晰，缺乏形式化框架来理解评估者与防御者之间的动态博弈。

Method: 提出博弈论框架，将安全对齐建模为评估者与训练者的二人博弈；使用群作用（如循环平移群）形式化数据增强；在圆环上分析不同泛化范围下的行为；在Llama、Qwen、Mistral三系列模型上进行实证验证。

Result: 理论：低于临界阈值时，评估者漏报率在多个回合中保持恒定。实证：微调仅产生局部泛化，拒绝率与微调提示的距离高度相关。

Conclusion: 基准测试不应被视为静态提示集，而是评估者群作用下的轨道；忽略训练者自适应的审计协议无法区分真正修复与记忆性补丁。

Abstract: As jailbreaks, adversarially crafted inputs that bypass safety constraints, continue to be discovered in Large Language Models, practitioners increasingly rely on fine-tuning as a defensive strategy. Yet the theoretical foundations underlying this robustness fine-tuning remain underexplored. We introduce a game-theoretic framework in which the interaction between an evaluator (auditing the model for jailbreaks) and a trainer is formalized as a two-player game.
  A key feature of our approach is the use of group actions, a mathematical structure that captures symmetries and transformations, to formally represent data augmentation. The simplest non-trivial instance is the circle with cyclic translation groups, where we exhibit various regimes depending on the trainer's generalization range. Below a critical threshold, the evaluator maintains a constant miss ratio for linearly many rounds, whereas other settings can yield very different behaviors. We further provide empirical evidence supporting locality-dependence of the model: for the three model families we tested (Llama, Qwen and Mistral), we have significant evidence that fine-tuning on adversarial prompts induces only local generalization, with refusal rates on test examples highly correlated with the distance to the fine-tuning prompts. Our framework recasts the central object of adversarial evaluation: a benchmark is not a static set of prompts but an orbit under the evaluator's group action, and audit protocols that ignore trainer-side adaptation cannot distinguish a genuine fix from a memorized patch.

</details>


### [146] [A Bitter Lesson for Data Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19407)
*Christopher Mohri,John Duchi,Tatsunori Hashimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，在大模型预训练的高计算、数据稀缺场景下，足够计算量的模型不仅不需要数据过滤，反而能从所谓的“低质量”数据中受益，最优过滤策略是不过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“数据过滤对高质量预训练至关重要”这一普遍认知，探究在高计算资源投入下数据过滤策略的真实效果。

Method: 通过针对高计算、数据稀缺场景的新规模扩展研究，对比不同数据过滤策略对大规模参数模型训练效果的影响。

Result: 足够训练的大参数模型不仅能够容忍低质量和干扰数据，实际上还能从这些“差”数据中获益；在足够计算量下，最佳数据过滤策略是不进行过滤。

Conclusion: 推翻了“数据过滤必不可少”的常见信念，指出在计算资源充足时，保留全部数据（包括低质量数据）反而更有利于模型性能。

Abstract: We investigate data filtering for large model pretraining via new scaling studies that target the high compute, data-scarce regime. In spite of an apparently common belief that filtering data to include only high-quality information is essential, our experiments suggest that with enough compute, the best data filter is no data filter. We find that sufficiently trained large parameter models not only tolerate low-quality and distractor data, but in fact benefit from nominally ``poor'' data.

</details>


### [147] [Unlocking the Potential of Continual Model Merging: An ODE Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19409)
*Lihong Lin,Haidong Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于常微分方程（ODE）驱动的模型合并方法（ODE-M），用于持续模型合并（CMM）场景。现有方法因缺乏对学习容量的显式控制，导致灾难性遗忘。ODE-M通过构建低损耗连接路径，沿时间依赖的速度场进行积分，并施加势垒约束避免损耗增加，实现了对顺序任务合并的可控性和高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持续模型合并方法缺乏对学习容量在已有能力与新合并模型之间分配的显式可控性，导致任务重要性异质时出现严重遗忘。根本原因在于以往方法将每个任务模型视为孤立的参数点，应用固定代数组合，而非显式构建独立训练模型之间在参数空间中的连接过渡。

Method: 受模式连通性（mode connectivity）启发，假设理想合并模型位于低损耗连接路径上，持续合并应沿此路径进行且不跨越导致遗忘的损耗势垒。提出ODE-M方法，通过积分时间依赖的速度场来追踪该路径，并施加势垒约束以防止损耗增加的步骤。

Result: 在主流CMM基准测试上，ODE-M相较于现有竞争方法取得了最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: ODE-M通过显式构建参数空间中的连续过渡路径，有效解决了持续模型合并中的可控性不足和灾难性遗忘问题，为CMM提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual Model Merging (CMM) enables rapid customization of foundation models across sequentially arriving tasks, offering a scalable alternative to repeated retraining. However, existing merging rules lack explicit controllability over the allocation of learning capacity between previously learned capabilities and newly merged models. Consequently, as tasks are merged sequentially, this deficiency accumulates into severe forgetting, particularly in scenarios with heterogeneous task importance, where performance allocation becomes highly inconsistent. The key reason can be attributed to the fact that previous methods treat each task model as an isolated parameter point and apply fixed algebraic combinations, rather than explicitly constructing a transition that respects how independently trained models can be connected in parameter space. Motivated by mode connectivity, we assume that desirable merged models lie on low loss connecting paths, and that continual merging should follow such paths without crossing loss barriers that induce forgetting. Grounded in these insights, we propose a novel ODE-driven Merging (ODE-M) tailored for CMM that traces such a path by integrating a time-dependent velocity field and enforcing barrier constraints to prevent loss-increasing steps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ODE-M achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to its competitors across mainstream CMM benchmarks.

</details>


### [148] [When to Stop Reusing: Dynamic Gradient Gating for Sample-Efficient RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19425)
*Yuchun Miao,Sen Zhang,Yuqi Zhang,Yaorui Shi,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Lefei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现RLVR中多轮复用采样会导致策略偏移和性能下降，并识别出“不成比例权重发散(DWD)”现象：性能退化与lm_head层权重突变同步发生。理论证明lm_head梯度范数是策略偏移的下界信号，据此提出动态梯度门控(DGG)，实现最高2.93倍采样效率和2.14倍实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中rollout样本获取成本高昂，自然想到复用批次进行多次梯度更新，但这会放大策略偏移导致性能严重退化，而如何及时检测退化起始点以停止复用是一个开放且困难的问题。

Method: 识别DWD现象（性能退化与lm_head权重突变同步），理论证明有害梯度集中在lm_head而中间层被结构性地衰减，且lm_head梯度范数是策略散度的下界。据此提出DGG，实时监测lm_head梯度范数并拦截有害梯度。

Result: 在数学、ALFWorld、WebShop和搜索增强QA任务上，DGG一致匹配或超越单次使用基线，实现最高2.93倍采样效率和2.14倍实际加速。

Conclusion: lm_head梯度范数可作为策略灾难性偏移的实时信号，DGG是一种轻量级、高效的干预方法，显著提升了RLVR的样本效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant paradigm for advanced reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs), but rollout samples are expensive to obtain, making sample efficiency a critical bottleneck. A natural remedy is to reuse each rollout batch for multiple gradient updates, a standard practice in classical RL. Yet in RLVR, this amplifies policy shift, leading to severe performance degradation. Detecting the onset of degradation early enough to stop reuse remains an open and challenging problem. We close this gap by identifying the \textit{Disproportionate Weight Divergence (DWD)} phenomenon: performance degradation is synchronized with a sharp surge in the \texttt{lm\_head} weight change, while intermediate layers remain stable. Empirically, we verify that DWD emerges consistently across diverse LLMs and tasks. Theoretically, we prove that (i) harmful gradients concentrate at the \texttt{lm\_head} while intermediate layers are structurally attenuated, and (ii) the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm lower-bounds the policy divergence. These results establish the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm as a principled, real-time signal of catastrophic policy shift. Guided by this insight, we propose \textit{Dynamic Gradient Gating (DGG)}, a lightweight intervention that monitors the \texttt{lm\_head} gradient norm in real time and intercepts harmful gradients before they corrupt the optimizer. DGG consistently matches or exceeds the standard single-use baseline, achieving up to $2.93\times$ sample efficiency and $2.14\times$ wall-clock speedup across math, ALFWorld, WebShop, and search-augmented QA tasks.

</details>


### [149] [When the Majority Votes Wrong, the Intervention Timing for Test-Time Reinforcement Learning Hides in the Extinction Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19444)
*Hongxiang Lin,Zhirui Kuai,Erpeng Xue,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出测试时强化学习（TTRL）在数学推理中报告的性能提升被系统性误解：大部分增益来自对已可解题目的答案锐化，而非真正的学习；错误翻转（正确→错误）的数量超过真正学到的样本，且一旦多数投票锁定错误答案则损伤不可逆。作者提出Correct-Answer Extinction Window概念及TTRL-Guard框架（含FRS、MPS、RCSU三种机制）来缓解此问题，实验表明该方法在多个模型和基准上取得最佳平均pass@1，在AIME 2025上相对TTRL提升+54%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示TTRL方法的根本缺陷：多数投票作为伪标签信号存在系统性误导，导致性能增益被高估、错误翻转不可逆，且低能力问题的正确信号存在短暂活跃窗口后被永久抑制。

Method: 提出TTRL-Guard，包含三种轻量级机制：1) Flip-Rate-Aware Reward Scaling (FRS)：根据翻转率衰减动态降低高风险更新的权重；2) Minority-Preserving Sampling (MPS)：保留少数正确答案的梯度信号；3) Risk-Conditioned Sparse Updatings (RCSU)：在高度极化的问题上暂停更新。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Qwen3-4B上取得最佳平均pass@1，在AIME 2025上相对TTRL相对提升+54%。

Conclusion: TTRL的增益主要来自答案锐化而非真正学习，错误翻转问题严重且不可逆；提出的TTRL-Guard通过针对性保护Correct-Answer Extinction Window内的正确信号，有效缓解了该问题并显著提升性能。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) reports substantial accuracy gains on mathematical reasoning benchmarks using majority vote as a pseudo-label signal. We argue these gains are systematically misinterpreted: most reflect sharpening of already-solvable problems rather than genuine learning, while problems corrupted from correct to incorrect outnumber truly learned ones, and this damage is irreversible once majority vote locks onto a wrong answer. Per-problem tracking reveals that correct-answer signals in low-ability problems are briefly active before being permanently suppressed, a phenomenon we term the \textit{Correct-Answer Extinction Window}, with Flip Rate (FR) as its leading indicator. We thus propose \textbf{TTRL-Guard}, a lightweight framework with three mechanisms targeting the extinction window: Flip-Rate-Aware Reward Scaling (FRS) down-weights at-risk updates as FR declines, Minority-Preserving Sampling (MPS) retains gradient signal from minority correct answers, and Risk-Conditioned Sparse Updatings (RCSU) suspends updates on polarized problems. Experiments across three models and four benchmarks show that TTRL-Guard achieves the best average pass@1 on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Qwen3-4B, improves relatively over TTRL by +54\% on AIME 2025. \footnote{Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/linhxkkkk/TTRL-Guard.

</details>


### [150] [Quantifying the Pre-training Dividend: Generative versus Latent Self-Supervised Learning for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19462)
*Noam Major,Kathy Razmadze,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个受控框架来评估自监督学习（SSL）在时间序列上的预训练收益，发现其效益高度不对称：在异常检测和分类任务上最高提升375%，但对预测任务影响甚微。研究引入DWT增强的LeJEPA和DINO架构，揭示了表征效用由"精度-不变性权衡"决定，且表征质量与数据来源无关，在中等深度饱和，指向大规模合成数据生成的可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然SSL在视觉和NLP领域取得了成功并快速被应用于时间序列，但已有研究主要集中在生成式范式和预测任务上，未能量化学习表征在多样化时序任务中的广泛效用。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估SSL预训练在不同时序任务中的"预训练红利"。

Method: 建立受控框架，系统比较生成式范式与潜对齐架构（引入LeJEPA和DINO的时间序列适配版本），利用离散小波变换（DWT）增强实现局部波动的不变性约束，在多样化时序任务上进行评估。

Result: 预训练红利高度不对称：异常检测和分类任务最高提升375%，而预测任务增益微乎其微。表征质量由精度-不变性权衡决定，与数据来源无关，且在中等模型深度处饱和。

Conclusion: SSL的表征效用不是通用的，其收益取决于任务所需的信号分辨率与学习目标的对齐程度。表征质量在中等深度饱和且与数据来源无关，表明可以通过大规模合成数据生成来实现可扩展的预训练。

Abstract: The success of self-supervised learning (SSL) in vision and NLP has motivated its rapid adoption for time series. However, research has focused primarily on Generative paradigms and forecasting tasks, leaving the broader utility of learned representations unquantified. We establish a controlled framework to evaluate the "pre-training dividend": the value added by SSL across diverse temporal tasks. We systematically compare Generative paradigms against Latent Alignment architectures, introducing adaptations of LeJEPA and DINO for time series. These adaptations utilize Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) augmentations to enforce invariance to local fluctuations. Our analysis reveals that the pre-training dividend is highly asymmetric: SSL yields gains of up to 375% for anomaly detection and classification, yet remains marginal for forecasting. We demonstrate that representational utility is non-universal, governed by a precision-invariance trade-off where the specific signal resolution required by the task must align with the objective. Finally, we show that representation quality is largely independent of data origin and saturates at moderate architectural depths, suggesting a path to scaling via massive synthetic generation. Our code is available at: https://github.com/noammajor/Models

</details>


### [151] [Sampling-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19469)
*Luca Vignola,Bruce D. Lee,Manish Prajapat,Manuel Wendl,Melanie Zeilinger,Andreas Krause,Yarden As*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SBSRL是一种基于模型的强化学习算法，通过在有限动力学样本集上联合施加约束来保证学习过程中的安全性，并利用约束认知不确定性进行探索，在理论和实践上均实现了安全高效的探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全探索是强化学习在现实世界部署中的根本挑战，现有方法难以在学习过程中同时保证安全性和高效的探索。

Method: 提出SBSRL算法：1) 基于模型的方法，在有限动力学样本集上联合施加约束，近似不可处理的最坏情况优化；2) 利用约束认知不确定性引导探索，无需显式探索奖励。

Result: 在模拟环境和真实机器人硬件上实现了安全高效的探索；可扩展至深度集成实现，适用于高维连续控制问题；推导了学习过程中的高概率安全保证和有限时间样本复杂度界。

Conclusion: SBSRL通过在有限动力学样本集上联合约束的方式，有效解决了连续域中的安全探索问题，兼具理论保证和实际可扩展性。

Abstract: Safe exploration remains a fundamental challenge in reinforcement learning (RL), limiting the deployment of RL agents in the real world. We propose Sampling-Based Safe Reinforcement Learning (SBSRL), a model-based RL algorithm that maintains safety throughout the learning process by enforcing constraints jointly across a finite set of dynamics samples. This formulation approximates an intractable worst-case optimization over uncertain dynamics and enables practical safety guarantees in continuous domains. We further introduce an exploration strategy based on constraining epistemic uncertainty, eliminating the need for explicit exploration bonuses. Under regularity conditions, we derive high-probability guarantees of safety throughout learning and a finite-time sample complexity bound for recovering a near-optimal policy. Empirically, SBSRL achieves safe and efficient exploration both in simulation and in real robotic hardware, and readily extends to practical deep-ensemble implementations that scale to high-dimensional continuous control problems.

</details>


### [152] [Adynamical systems view of training generativemodels and the memorization phenomenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19483)
*Siva Athreya,Chiranjib Bhattacharya,Vivek S. Borkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于生成模型中的坍塌现象和随机梯度下降的两时间尺度动力学，从动力系统视角解释了生成模型中的记忆化现象，认为该现象源于训练阶段的动态特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对生成模型中广泛观察到的记忆化现象（模型在长时间段内输出相同或相似结果）缺乏系统性的理论解释，特别是从训练动态角度的理解。

Method: 利用Austin [2016]的结果构建损失函数的风格化模型，其中损失函数对部分变量强依赖、对剩余变量弱依赖，导致常数步长SGD中出现两个不同时间尺度；结合Borkar [2025a]的坍塌模型和Azizian等人[2024]的近期结果，分析常数步长SGD的动态行为。

Result: 从动力系统视角成功解释了生成模型中的记忆化现象，并揭示了记忆化与双下降、坍塌等现象之间的内在关联。

Conclusion: 记忆化现象可以从训练动态的角度得到系统性解释，为理解生成模型中的多种现象提供了一个统一的动力系统理论框架。

Abstract: Using recent works of one of the authors (VSB) on collapse in generative models and two time scale dynamics in stochastic gradient descent in high dimensions, we give a system theoretic explanation of the memorization phenomenon in generative models. This relies purely on the dynamic aspects of the training phase. Specifically, we use a result of Austin [2016] to motivate a stylized model for the loss function for stochastic gradient descent (SGD) wherein the loss function has a strong dependence on some variables and weak dependence on the rest in a precise sense. This naturally leads to two distinct time scales in the constant step size SGD that is commonly used in machine learning. This fact has been used to explain the double descent phenomenon in SGD in Borkar [2026]. In conjunction with a mathematical model for collapse phenomenon in SGD developed in Borkar [2025a], we analyze the constant step size SGD using the recent results of Azizian et al. [2024] in order to explain the phenomenon of memorization wherein a generative model that is concurrently being tuned yields the same or similar outputs for significant stretches of time. This gives a novel perspective on the aforementioned phenomena reported in machine learning literature and their interrelationships, using a dynamical systems viewpoint.

</details>


### [153] [The Silent Hyperparameter: Quantifying the Impact of Inference Backends on LLM Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19537)
*David Pape,Jonathan Evertz,Lea Schönherr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM推理后端（如vLLM、SGLang、llama.cpp等）的选择会显著影响基准测试结果，最高可造成16.6个百分点的分数差异，并导致高比例的输出不一致。研究调查了200种推理引擎，分析了35,000篇ML论文，发现推理栈信息极少被报告。这种偏差源于系统级优化（如前缀缓存、CUDA graphs、定制kernel和logit处理的引擎默认值）。本文呼吁将推理后端视为LLM评估中的重要超参数，并标准化推理栈的报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准测试中SOTA提升常以不到一个百分点的差距衡量，但推理后端的系统级优化可能改变token概率并引入非确定性，从而影响评估结果。然而现有文献几乎不报告推理栈信息，导致评估结果的可重复性和可解释性不足。

Method: 首先调查推理引擎生态（识别200种引擎），分析35,000篇ML出版物中推理栈的报告情况。然后保持模型权重、解码参数和硬件不变，在5种推理引擎（vLLM、SGLang、llama.cpp等）上对多个开源模型进行基准测试。通过隔离后端优化并追踪执行流水线，分析性能差异的来源。

Result: 推理后端的选择可导致基准分数最高16.6个百分点的差异，并产生高比例的输出不一致性。差异主要由系统级优化引起，包括前缀缓存、CUDA graphs、定制kernel以及logit处理中的引擎默认值。

Conclusion: 推理后端是LLM评估中此前未被报告但影响显著的"超参数"，应标准化推理栈的报告以提高基准比较的可重复性和可解释性。

Abstract: Progress in LLMs is increasingly measured through standardized benchmarks, where state-of-the-art improvements are often separated by fractions of a percentage point. At the same time, the computational cost of evaluating modern LLMs has driven widespread adoption of specialized inference backends, software systems that execute trained models efficiently at inference time. While critical for scalability, system-level optimizations, such as custom CUDA kernels and reduced-precision arithmetic, can alter token probabilities and introduce non-determinism, possibly cascading into divergent generation. In this work, we first survey the inference landscape, identifying 200 distinct engines, and analyze 35,000 ML publications, finding that the specific inference stack is rarely reported despite this widespread diversity. We then present a systematic empirical study of how inference backends affect LLM benchmark results. Holding model weights, decoding parameters, and hardware constant, we evaluate five widely used inference engines, including vLLM, SGLang, and llama.cpp, across multiple open-weight models and established benchmarks. We show that the choice of backend alone can shift benchmark scores by up to 16.6 percentage points and induce high rates of output disagreement. By isolating backend optimizations and tracing the execution pipeline, we find this divergence is driven by system-level optimizations like prefix caching and CUDA graphs, custom kernels, and engine-specific defaults in logit processing. Our findings identify the inference backend as a previously unreported but consequential hyperparameter in the evaluation of LLM and advocate standardized reporting of inference stacks to improve the reproducibility and interpretability of benchmark comparisons.

</details>


### [154] [TORQ: Two-Level Orthogonal Rotation for MXFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19561)
*Zukang Xu,Xing Hu,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TORQ，一种无需训练的后训练量化框架，通过两级正交旋转重塑激活空间几何特性，解决MXFP4格式在LLM激活量化中的结构不平衡问题，使4位浮点量化精度接近全精度推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MXFP4格式虽兼具高动态范围和硬件效率，但直接应用于LLM激活量化会导致显著的精度下降，根源在于激活分布与MXFP4块浮点格式之间存在两种结构不平衡：块间方差极端不平衡和块内码本利用率不平衡。

Method: TORQ框架包含两级正交旋转：宏观层面利用Schur-Horn定理通过块间正交旋转重新分配激活能量，防止高方差块抬高共享缩放因子；微观层面通过最大熵引导的块内旋转缓解码本崩溃，最大化MXFP4码本的信息容量。

Result: 在Qwen3-32B上，WikiText困惑度降至8.43（BF16为7.61），平均准确率从直接RTN的38.40%提升至73.63%（BF16为74.82%），大幅缩小了4位浮点量化与全精度推理之间的差距。

Conclusion: TORQ通过无需训练的后训练量化框架有效解决了MXFP4激活量化的结构不平衡问题，在主流LLM上显著提升了精度，为下一代低比特推理部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) advance toward practical deployment, the Microscaling FP4 (MXFP4) format has emerged as a cornerstone for next-generation low-bit inference, owing to its ability to balance high dynamic range with hardware efficiency. However, directly applying MXFP4 to LLM activation quantization inevitably leads to significant accuracy degradation. In this paper, we theoretically analyze the error structure of MXFP4 activation quantization, revealing that the root cause of this performance drop lies in two structural imbalances between activation distributions and the MXFP4 block floating-point format: (1) extreme inter-block variance imbalance and (2) intra-block codebook utilization imbalance. To address these challenges, we propose TORQ (Two-level Orthogonal Rotation for MXFP4 Quantization), a training-free Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) framework designed to reshape the geometric properties of the activation space through optimal coordinate transformations. At the macroscopic level, TORQ leverages the Schur-Horn theorem to redistribute activation energy via inter-block orthogonal rotation, preventing high-variance blocks from driving up shared scaling factors and thereby preserving the precision of small-magnitude elements. At the microscopic level, TORQ employs maximum-entropy-guided intra-block rotation to alleviate codebook collapse and maximize the MXFP4 codebook's information capacity. Experiments on mainstream LLMs such as LLaMA3 and Qwen3 show that TORQ significantly improves the accuracy of MXFP4 activation quantization compared to existing methods: on Qwen3-32B, the perplexity on WikiText is reduced to 8.43 (vs. 7.61 for BF16), and the average accuracy increases from 38.40% with direct RTN to 73.63% (vs. 74.82% for BF16), substantially narrowing the gap between 4-bit floating-point quantization and full-precision inference.

</details>


### [155] [Online Market Making and the Value of Observing the Order Book](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19584)
*Davide Maran,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线做市问题，提出一种受真实限价订单簿启发的行动依赖反馈模型，相比标准bandit反馈模型能显著提升后悔界。在随机设定下提出消除算法实现O(√T)后悔，在均值回复价格过程中同样达到O(√T)，在对抗设定下通过探索-扰动算法达到O(T^{2/3})后悔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线学习做市公式假设完全删失反馈（仅观察到交易是否发生），而真实限价订单簿中未发生交易时能揭示供需信息。作者旨在量化观察订单簿信息的价值，证明行动依赖反馈能从根本上改变问题的可学习性。

Method: 1) 随机i.i.d.市场价设定：提出基于消除的算法，无需交易者估值分布的平滑性假设。2) 均值回复价格过程：考虑局部自回归动态和基于累积偏离均值的全局漂移条件，利用新的浓度不等式。3) 对抗设定（ oblivious prices）：设计探索-扰动算法。

Result: 随机设定下高概率O(√T)后悔界；均值回复价格过程下同样达到高概率O(√T)后悔界；对抗设定下实现期望O(T^{2/3})后悔界。

Conclusion: 行动依赖的有限反馈（观察订单簿）能显著改善在线做市中的后悔保证，相比标准bandit反馈模型具有本质优势。

Abstract: We study an online market-making problem in which a learner sequentially posts bid and ask prices for a single asset while interacting with traders holding private valuations. Unlike existing online learning formulations that assume fully censored feedback, we introduce an action-dependent feedback model inspired by real limit order books: when a trade occurs, the trader's valuation remains hidden, whereas when no trade occurs, informative feedback about supply and demand is revealed.
  We show that this additional information fundamentally changes the learnability of the problem. In the stochastic setting with i.i.d. market prices, we propose an elimination-based algorithm that achieves $O(\sqrt T)$ regret with high probability, without requiring any smoothness assumptions on the distribution of trader valuations. We then extend this result to a broad class of mean-reverting price processes by considering both local, autoregressive dynamics and a weaker global drift condition based on cumulative deviations from the mean. Under either assumption, we establish high-probability $O(\sqrt T)$ regret bounds, relying on a new concentration inequality of independent interest. Finally, in the adversarial setting with oblivious prices, we design an explore-then-perturb algorithm that guarantees $O(T^{2/3})$ regret in expectation.
  Our results quantify the value of observing the order book in online market making and demonstrate that even limited, action-dependent feedback can substantially improve regret guarantees compared to standard bandit feedback models.

</details>


### [156] [MiMuon: Mixed Muon Optimizer with Improved Generalization for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19619)
*Feihu Huang,Yuning Luo,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器的泛化误差，证明其泛化误差为O(1/(Nκ^T))，并提出混合动量SGD的MiMuon优化器，将泛化误差降低至O(1/N)，同时保持与Muon相同的收敛速度O(1/T^{1/4})，在Qwen3-0.6B和YOLO26m等大模型训练中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作开始研究Muon优化器的收敛性质（优化误差），但其泛化性质（泛化误差）尚未被建立。

Method: 基于算法稳定性和数学归纳法证明Muon的泛化误差；提出混合型MiMuon优化器，谨慎使用梯度正交化，融合Muon和动量SGD，并证明其泛化误差和收敛率。

Result: Muon泛化误差为O(1/(Nκ^T))，MiMuon泛化误差降低至O(1/N)，且MiMuon保持与Muon相同的收敛速率O(1/T^{1/4})。

Conclusion: 提出MiMuon优化器，在保持收敛速度的同时显著降低泛化误差，在大模型训练中验证了其高效性。

Abstract: Matrix-structured parameters frequently appear in many artificial intelligence models such as large language models. More recently, an efficient Muon optimizer is designed for matrix parameters of large-scale models, and shows markedly faster convergence than the vector-wise algorithms. Although some works have begun to study convergence properties (i.e., optimization error) of the Muon optimizer, its generalization properties (i.e., generalization error) is still not established. Thus, in this paper, we study generalization error of the Muon optimizer based on algorithmic stability and mathematical induction, and prove that the Muon has a generalization error of $O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$, where $N$ is training sample size, and $T$ denotes iteration number, and $κ>0$ denotes minimum difference between singular values of gradient estimate. To enhance generalization of the Muon, we propose an effective mixed Muon (MiMuon) optimizer by cautiously using orthogonalization of gradient, which is a hybrid of Muon and momentum-based SGD optimizers. Then we prove that our MiMuon optimizer has a lower generalization error of $O\big(\frac{1}{N}\big)$ than $O\big(\frac{1}{Nκ^{T}}\big)$ of Muon optimizer, since $κ$ generally is very small. Meanwhile, we also studied the convergence properties of our MiMuon algorithm, and prove that our MiMuon algorithm has the same convergence rate of $O(\frac{1}{T^{1/4}})$ as the Muon algorithm. Some numerical experimental results on training large models including Qwen3-0.6B and YOLO26m demonstrate efficiency of the MiMuon optimizer.

</details>


### [157] [Optimal Reconstruction from Linear Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19625)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从带噪声的线性查询中重构未知点的问题，刻画了最优重构误差与查询次数T、维度d和噪声参数δ的关系。在T→∞时，最优误差收敛到√(2d/(d+1))δ；固定维度时，超额误差以双指数速率衰减；维度增长时，需要约exp(d)量级的查询才能实现可忽略的超额误差。技术贡献是提出Jung定理的鲁棒变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于低维遥感、信号恢复、高维数据分析和隐私敏感推理等多个应用场景，核心目标是刻画最优重构误差与查询次数T、维度d和噪声参数δ之间的精确关系。

Method: 理论分析：分别研究T→∞极限情形和有限T情形；固定维度时分析超额误差衰减速率；维度增长时分析所需查询次数下界。技术核心是推广Jung定理（1901），利用对称性和李群作用通过几何与动力学论证证明鲁棒变体。

Result: 1) T→∞时，最优重构误差收敛到√(2d/(d+1))δ；2) 固定维度时超额误差以双指数速率衰减；3) 维度增长时需Ω(exp(d))量级查询才能实现可忽略超额误差；4) 提出了Jung定理的鲁棒推广。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了线性查询重构问题的信息论极限，揭示了维度与查询次数之间的权衡关系，并为几何分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of reconstructing an unknown point in $\mathbb{R}^d$ from approximate linear queries. This setting arises naturally in applications ranging from low-dimensional remote sensing and signal recovery to high-dimensional data analysis and privacy-sensitive inference. Our main goal is to characterize the optimal reconstruction error as a function of the number of queries $T$, the ambient dimension $d$, and the noise parameter $δ$.
  We first analyze the limit $T \to \infty$ and show that the optimal reconstruction error converges to the explicit value $\sqrt{2d/(d+1)} δ$, which plays a role analogous to the Bayes optimal error in supervised learning. When the dimension is fixed, we show that the excess error above this limit decays doubly exponentially fast as $T \to \infty$, a rate that is significantly faster than those typically encountered in learning curves. When the dimension grows, we show that a number of queries on the order of $\exp(d)$ is necessary and sufficient to achieve vanishing excess error. Finally, we introduce and analyze an improper variant of the reconstruction problem.
  From a technical perspective, our main contribution is a generalization of Jung's theorem (1901). The classical theorem bounds the maximum possible radius of a set of diameter 1 and characterizes extremal bodies. Our generalization provides a robust variant that characterizes near-extremal bodies and is proved via geometric and dynamical arguments exploiting symmetry and Lie group actions.

</details>


### [158] [Agentic Discovery of Cryomicroneedle Formulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19677)
*Hao Li,Lifu Du,Nurul Hameed,Shemonti Saha Authai,Zlata Stefanovic,Chenjie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI辅助的闭环工作流程，结合文献整理、高斯过程替代建模、贝叶斯优化和湿实验验证，用于发现冷冻微针低温保护剂配方。经过10轮迭代和106次湿实验，模型预测误差从41.21降至6.86个百分点，最佳配方实现了95.15%的存活率，但高存活率并不保证完整的冷冻微针成型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻微针为活细胞微创皮内递送提供了途径，但其冷冻配方必须在细胞保护、毒性和器件制造约束之间取得平衡，现有方法难以高效发现合适的低温保护剂配方。

Method: 采用AI辅助闭环工作流程：从42篇研究中整理198个间充质干细胞冷冻保存配方数据集，提取21种成分特征；训练基于文献先验的高斯过程替代模型；结合贝叶斯优化进行配方推荐；通过10轮迭代和106次湿实验进行渐进式校正。

Result: 模型批次均方根误差从41.21降至6.86个百分点；累积湿实验预测与实测R²达0.942；最佳配方（低DMSO、依克多因、乙二醇和胎牛血清）实现95.15%冻后存活率；但高存活率不保证冷冻微针完整成型。

Conclusion: AI辅助的计算基础设施能够以数据高效的方式使配方发现过程更易被缺乏数据专业知识的实验室使用，但未来需要多目标优化来同时满足细胞活性和器件完整性要求。

Abstract: Cryomicroneedles offer a route to minimally invasive intradermal delivery of living cells, but their cryogenic formulations must reconcile cell protection with constraints on toxicity and device fabrication. Here we report an AI-assisted, closed-loop workflow for cryomicroneedle cryoprotectant discovery that combines literature curation, Gaussian-process surrogate modelling, Bayesian optimization, and sequential wet-lab validation. A curated dataset of 198 mesenchymal stem-cell cryopreservation formulations from 42 studies was converted into 21 ingredient features and used to train an uncertainty-aware literature prior. This model captured moderate structure in the literature data but failed prospectively, motivating iterative wet-lab correction. Across ten validation iterations and 106 wet-lab observations, the model progressively adapted to cryomicroneedle-specific outcomes: batch RMSE decreased from 41.21 to 6.86 percentage points, later-stage rank correlations became consistently positive, and the cumulative wet-lab predicted-versus-measured summary reached $R^2 = 0.942$. The best validated formulation achieved 95.15\% post-thaw viability with low DMSO, ectoin, ethylene glycol, and fetal bovine serum. However, high viability alone did not ensure intact cryomicroneedle formation, highlighting the need for future multi-objective optimization. These results demonstrate that agent-assisted computational infrastructure can make data-efficient formulation discovery more accessible to labs with minimal data expertise in-house. Project code is available at https://github.com/baitmeister/ML-for-CryoMN.

</details>


### [159] [MSAlign: Aligning Molecule and Mass Spectra Foundation Models for Metabolite Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19752)
*Paul Krzakala,Gabriel Melo,Camille Lançon,Charlotte Laclau,Rémi Flamary,Etienne Thévenot,Florence d'Alché-Buc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架MSAlign，通过对齐两个冻结的基础模型（DreaMS和ChemBERTa）实现代谢物检索，在多个基准上超越现有方法，并正式化了数据拆分策略中分布偏移的度量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢物鉴定是代谢组学的核心挑战，但现有方法因数据预处理复杂、缺乏统一实现导致难以复现和比较。此外，数据拆分策略中数据泄露与领域偏移之间的权衡缺乏定量分析。

Method: 提出MSAlign框架，受视觉-语言模型多模态对齐启发，通过轻量MLP投影层将两个冻结的基础模型（DreaMS用于质谱，ChemBERTa用于分子）对齐到共享表示空间，并使用基于候选的对比学习目标进行训练。同时引入定量分布偏移度量来形式化数据拆分策略的权衡。

Result: MSAlign实现简单、训练快速，在所有基准测试上一致超越现有方法。公开了数据集、拆分方案、候选集以及统一实现。

Conclusion: 统一框架MSAlign有效解决了代谢物检索任务中的可复现性和性能问题，并提供了对数据拆分策略中分布偏移的定量理解，促进了可重复研究。

Abstract: Accurately identifying metabolites i.e. small molecules from mass spectrometry data remains a core challenge in metabolomics, with broad applications in drug discovery, environmental analysis, and clinical research. We address the Molecule Retrieval task, which consists in recovering the chemical structure of a metabolite from its MS/MS spectrum given a set of candidate molecules. While the recent release of benchmark datasets such as MassSpecGym and Spectraverse has considerably accelerated the development of novel machine learning approaches, the complexity of data preprocessing pipelines and the lack of unified implementations make methods and results difficult to reproduce and compare. We make three contributions. First, we propose a unified framework encompassing recent approaches based on representation alignment and contrastive learning. Second, we introduce MSAlign, inspired by multimodal alignment in vision-language models, which learns a shared representation space by aligning two frozen foundation models (DreaMS for mass spectra and ChemBERTa for molecules) through lightweight MLP projections trained with a candidate-based contrastive objective. MSAlign is simple to implement, fast to train and consistently outperforms existing approaches across all benchmarks. Third, we investigate a long-standing evaluation problem: data splitting strategies in molecule retrieval implicitly trade off data leakage against domain shift. We formalize this tension by introducing a quantitative measure of distribution shift, and use it to evaluate splitting strategies in existing benchmarks. All datasets, splits, candidate sets, and a unified implementation of MSAlign and baselines are publicly released to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [160] [AR1-ZO: Topology-Aware Rank-1 Zeroth-Order Queries for High-Rank LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19767)
*Ziye Chen,Hongbin Lin,Chenyu Zhang,Xiangda Yan,Yongjie Yang,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现零阶优化与LoRA结合时存在秩悖论，并提出AR1-ZO方法，通过交替查询秩-1原子并调整缩放因子，在不增加额外前向传播的前提下，解决了高秩LoRA在零阶优化中信号坍缩的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化与LoRA结合时存在秩悖论：增大LoRA秩会提升适配器容量，但标准两点零阶优化要么扰动与秩相关的坐标数，要么在原子级更新下使有限差分信号不可观测。本文旨在解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出AR1-ZO方法，核心包括：(1) 每次只查询一个秩-1原子（完整因子-坐标块），将秩r从单次查询的扰动维度中移除；(2) 采用拓扑感知缩放γ=αr代替传统LoRA缩放α/r，恢复秩无关的有效信号，无需辅助基、激活钩子、曲率估计或额外前向查询。

Result: 理论证明了原子最小性、秩无关的活跃查询维度、方向坍缩与恢复机制。实验在OPT和Qwen3模型上验证了信号机制，表明AR1-ZO在标准两前向传播查询预算下，使高秩LoRA在同等预算的零阶方法中表现有效。

Conclusion: AR1-ZO通过交替秩-1原子查询和拓扑感知缩放，有效解决了零阶优化中LoRA的秩悖论，使高秩适配器在零阶微调中变得可行且高效。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization enables large-language-model fine-tuning without storing backpropagation activations, while LoRA supplies compact trainable adapters. Combining them creates a rank paradox: increasing LoRA rank improves adapter capacity, but standard two-point ZO either perturbs a rank-dependent number of coordinates or, under atomwise updates, can make the finite-difference signal unobservable. This paper shows that the bottleneck is a measurement-topology problem rather than a need for an external subspace. LoRA already decomposes into matched rank-$1$ atoms, each a complete factor-coordinate block of dimension $d_\text{out}+d_\text{in}$. Querying one atom per step keeps the stored adapter rank $r$ while removing $r$ from the single-query perturbation dimension. The naive atomwise query is still miscalibrated: if it inherits canonical LoRA scaling $α/r$, the active finite-difference signal shrinks as $1/r$ and the active finite-difference signal-to-noise ratio (FD-SNR) as $1/r^2$, producing directional collapse under a fixed residual evaluation-noise floor. AR1-ZO pairs alternating rank-$1$ atom queries with topology-aware scaling $γ=αr$, restoring rank-invariant active signal without auxiliary bases, activation hooks, curvature estimates, or extra forward queries. Theory proves atom minimality, rank-independent active query dimension, directional collapse and restoration, and the remaining rank dependence as an amortized coverage cost. Experiments on OPT and Qwen3 models validate the signal mechanism and show that AR1-ZO makes high-rank LoRA effective among matched-budget ZO methods under the standard two-forward-pass query budget.

</details>


### [161] [Latent Laplace Diffusion for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19805)
*Zinuo You,Jin Zheng,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff)，一种针对不规则多变量时间序列长程预测的生成式框架。该框架将目标建模为低维潜在轨迹，利用随机端口-哈密顿动力学引导逆过程，在拉普拉斯域中参数化均值演化，并通过更新平均分析处理不规则采样间隙。实验表明其在长程预测和缺失值插补上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则多变量时间序列在长程预测中存在两难：离散方法通过重网格化扭曲时序结构，连续模型则依赖顺序求解器易产生漂移。现有方法难以在不规则时间点上直接高效地生成完整预测。

Method: 1) 将目标建模为低维潜在轨迹，实现无需逐步时间积分的长程生成；2) 利用随机端口-哈密顿动力学引导逆过程，在拉普拉斯域通过可学习的共轭复极点参数化均值演化，支持在不规则时间戳上直接求值；3) 通过更新平均分析将采样间隙映射为有效事件域极点，设计间隙感知的历史汇总器。

Result: 在长程预测任务上优于多个基线方法，同时支持在同一模型上通过查询历史时间戳进行缺失值插补。

Conclusion: LLapDiff有效弥合了离散与连续方法在不规则时间序列长程预测上的差距，生成式连续时间特性使其具备良好的缺失值插补能力。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series impose a trade-off for long-horizon forecasting: discrete methods can distort temporal structure via re-gridding, while continuous-time models often require sequential solvers prone to drift. To bridge this gap, we present Latent Laplace Diffusion (LLapDiff), a generative framework that models the target as a low-dimensional latent trajectory, enabling horizon-wide generation without step-by-step integration over physical time. We guide the reverse process utilizing a stable modal parameterization motivated by stochastic port-Hamiltonian dynamics, and parameterize its mean evolution in the Laplace domain via learnable complex-conjugate poles, enabling direct evaluation over irregular timestamps. We also link continuous dynamics to irregular observations through renewal-averaging analysis, which maps sampling gaps to effective event-domain poles and motivates a gap-aware history summarizer. Extensive experiments show that LLapDiff improves over baselines in long-horizon forecasting, and its continuous-time generative nature supports missing-value imputation by querying the same model at historical timestamps. Code is available at https://github.com/pixelhero98/LLapDiffusion.

</details>


### [162] [FLUXtrapolation: A benchmark on extrapolating ecosystem fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19812)
*Anya Fries,Jacob A Nelson,Martin Jung,Markus Reichstein,Jonas Peters*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLUXtrapolation是一个用于评估生态系统通量外推的基准测试，专门针对逐渐加重的分布偏移（包括协变量偏移P_X和条件偏移P_{Y|X}）设计。它定义了时间、空间和温度三种外推场景，并通过尾部误差和多尺度评估来全面衡量模型性能。初步研究表明，现有基线在常规指标上表现相似，但在新提出的评估方法下差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态系统通量（碳、水、能量）对理解全球循环至关重要，但只能通过稀疏分布的测量塔直接观测。因此需要利用全球可获取的协变量对观测站点训练模型，并预测未观测区域（即升尺度）。然而，通量升尺度面临严重的领域泛化问题：协变量分布在不同气候、生态系统类型和环境条件下发生偏移，同时重要驱动因子在全球尺度上不可观测导致条件偏移。现有基准未能系统量化这些偏移，亟需一个能真实反映这些挑战的评估框架。

Method: FLUXtrapolation基于通量升尺度的领域知识设计：定义了三种外推场景（时间外推、空间外推、基于温度的外推），并采用多个评估维度（保留域评估、时间聚合评估、尾部误差评估）。论文首先对P_X（协变量分布偏移）和P_{Y|X}（条件偏移）进行了定量分析，然后通过初步实验对比基线模型在传统中位数小时RMSE与新提出的尾部聚焦和多尺度评估下的表现差异。

Result: 初步实验发现，不同基线模型在传统的中位数小时RMSE指标下表现相似，但在新提出的尾部误差聚焦评估和多尺度评估方法下能够明显区分开来。

Conclusion: FLUXtrapolation为机器学习方法在分布偏移下提供了一个真实且具有挑战性的基准测试；同时，在该基准上取得的进展将直接支持改进通量升尺度这一科学目标。

Abstract: We introduce FLUXtrapolation, a benchmark for extrapolating ecosystem fluxes under progressively harder distribution shifts. Ecosystem fluxes are central to understanding the carbon, water, and energy cycles, yet they can only be measured directly at sparsely located measurement towers. Producing global flux estimates therefore requires training models on observed sites using globally available covariates and predicting in unobserved regions, that is, upscaling. Flux upscaling is a challenging domain generalization problem that is affected by a shift in covariate distribution across climates, ecosystem types, and environmental conditions, as well as by conditional shift: important drivers remain unobserved at global scale. We provide a quantitative analysis of both these shifts in $P_X$ and $P_{Y\mid X}$. FLUXtrapolation is designed based on domain expertise on flux upscaling: it defines temporal, spatial, and temperature-based extrapolation scenarios and evaluates performance across held-out domains, temporal aggregations, and tail errors. In a pilot study, we find that baselines perform similarly under median hourly RMSE, but separate under the proposed tail-focused and multi-scale evaluation. FLUXtrapolation therefore poses a realistic and thus relevant challenge for machine learning methods under distribution shift; at the same time, progress on this benchmark would directly support the scientific goal of improving flux upscaling.

</details>


### [163] [General Lower Bounds for Differentially Private Federated Learning with Arbitrary Public-Transcript Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19813)
*Yicheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对满足差分隐私的联邦学习协议，提出了一种基于任意公开交互转录的通用下界，并建立了联邦van Trees下界，用于在客户端级zCDP约束下的参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中的差分隐私保护需要理论下界来指导协议设计，但现有工作缺乏针对任意回合数、样本可重用的联邦协议的统一下界框架。

Method: 利用隐私-信息收缩不等式处理完全公开转录，结合van Trees不等式推导出满足zCDP约束的估计器的联邦下界。

Result: 得到了一个适用于均值估计、线性回归和非参数回归的通用下界，展示了隐私、通信轮次和样本重用对估计精度的联合约束。

Conclusion: 该下界揭示了联邦差分隐私协议中隐私预算、交互复杂度和样本效率之间的基本权衡，为设计最优协议提供了理论基准。

Abstract: We prove a general lower bound for differentially private federated learning protocols with arbitrary public-transcript interactions. The protocol may use any number of adaptive rounds, and each client's local samples may be reused across rounds. For parameter estimation under squared \(\ell_2\) loss, we establish a federated van Trees lower bound for every estimator satisfying a total clientwise sample-level zero-concentrated differential privacy (zCDP) constraint. The main technical ingredient is a privacy-information contraction inequality for complete public transcripts. We illustrate the bound through applications to mean estimation, linear regression, and nonparametric regression.

</details>


### [164] [Set-Valued Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19830)
*Laura Fuentes-Vicente,Mathieu Even,Gaëlle Dormion,Antoine Chambaz,Uri Shalit,Julie Josse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多治疗场景的集合值策略学习范式，输出一组可行治疗方案而非单一推荐，通过策略集大小反映决策模糊程度，并引入了基于最大下界的学习延迟框架和保形策略学习，实现了内在的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的点值治疗策略对估计不确定性、模型设定和有限样本波动高度敏感，且无法提供推荐动作的置信度，缺乏不确定性量化能力。

Method: 提出了集合值策略学习范式；扩展了学习延迟框架到多治疗场景，使用最大下界方法；引入保形策略学习，结合噪声标签领域的随机性注入方法，无需对最优治疗规则做假设即可保证边际覆盖。

Result: 在合成数据和体外受精真实应用中，该方法生成了稳健且可操作的策略，自然地纳入临床考量，有效平衡了性能与可靠性。

Conclusion: 集合值策略学习通过输出治疗集合而非单一点估计，提供了内在的不确定性量化，为临床决策提供了更具鲁棒性和可解释性的指导。

Abstract: Conventional treatment policies map patient covariates to a single recommended intervention in order to maximize expected clinical outcomes. Although a rich body of causal inference methods has been developed to estimate such policies, point-valued recommendations can be highly sensitive to estimation uncertainty, model specification, and finite-sample variability, while typically providing little guidance about how confident one should be in the recommended action. In this work, we propose a set-valued policy learning paradigm for the multiple-treatment setting, in which policies output a set of plausible treatments rather than a single recommendation. This formulation enables intrinsic uncertainty quantification, with the size of the predicted set reflecting the degree of decision ambiguity. We extend the learning-to-defer framework to multiple treatments via a novel \textit{greatest Lower Bound} method, and introduce \textit{conformal policy learning}, which bridges the gap between unobserved ground-truth optimal treatments and estimated optimal treatment rules. Drawing on insights from the noisy-label literature, we develop a randomness-injection approach that guarantees marginal coverage without requiring assumptions on underlying black-box optimal treatment rules. Through experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application to In-Vitro Fertilization (IVF), we demonstrate that our methods produce robust and actionable policies that naturally incorporate clinical considerations while effectively balancing performance and reliability.

</details>


### [165] [A Closed-loop, State-centric, Multi-agent Framework for Passenger Load Estimation from Heterogeneous Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19834)
*Yiyao Xu,Hao Zhou,Yuhang Wang,Jingran Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种闭环、状态中心的多智能体框架，用于从异构数据流中鲁棒估计乘客负载，通过物理可行性约束、动态信任分配和残差反馈训练，解决了计数误差、证据冲突和传感器可靠性变化等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共交通机构需要可靠的乘客负载轨迹来支持运营和面向乘客的服务，但现有负载估计通常来自不完善的感知系统，且自动乘客计数（APC）系统的准确性因车站布局、客流强度和运行条件而异，存在增量计数误差、证据冲突和传感器可靠性依赖上下文等问题。

Method: 提出闭环、状态中心的多智能体框架，包含统一停站事件骨干、耦合的感知-物理-融合循环（用于逐站推理）、可选的行程级宏观修正和闭环校准模块。该方法在每一步强制物理可行性，在证据源之间动态分配信任，并将物理推导的违反残差反馈回训练以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 该框架通过物理可行性约束和动态信任分配解决异构数据流中的计数误差和证据冲突问题，并通过闭环校准实现鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 提出的多智能体框架能够有效应对乘客负载估计中的核心挑战，为公共交通运营提供更可靠的负载轨迹支撑。

Abstract: To support operations and passenger-facing services, transit agencies need reliable passenger load trajectories. Currently, load estimates are typically inferred from imperfect sensing systems rather than fully observed, and the accuracy of modern automatic passenger counting (APC) systems still varies with station layout, flow intensity, and operating conditions. To address the challenges of robust passenger load estimation from heterogeneous data streams, including incremental count errors, evidence conflicts, and context-dependent sensor reliability, we propose a closed-loop, state-centric, multi-agent framework. This method enforces physical feasibility at every step, allocates trust dynamically among evidence sources, and feeds physics-derived violation residuals back into training for robustness improvement. The architecture consists of a unified stop-event backbone, a coupled Perception--Physical--Fusion loop for stop-by-stop inference, and optional trip-level macro-correction and closed-loop calibration modules.

</details>


### [166] [JAXenstein: Accelerated Benchmarking for First-Person Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19926)
*Ruo Yu Tao,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAXenstein是一个基于JAX的开源基准测试，实现了《德军总部3D》渲染引擎，用于快速可扩展的视觉第一人称任务实验，比同类基准测试快数倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JAX强化学习生态系统中缺乏测试视觉第一人称任务的基准，而这些领域对于测试探索能力和处理部分可观测性至关重要。

Method: 开发了基于JAX的开源基准测试JAXenstein，实现了《德军总部3D》渲染引擎，支持快速和可扩展的视觉第一人称任务实验。

Result: JAXenstein比同类视觉基准测试快数倍，且易于扩展到更复杂的第一人称领域。

Conclusion: JAXenstein填补了JAX强化学习生态中视觉第一人称基准的空白，为快速迭代算法开发提供了高效平台。

Abstract: The progression of reinforcement learning algorithms have been driven by challenging benchmarks. The rate in which a researcher can iterate on a problem setting directly impacts the speed of algorithm development. Modern machine learning has produced tools that allow for fast and scalable algorithm development like the JAX library. With the availability of these tools, a serious bottleneck in algorithm development is the availability of large and complex domains for experimentation. Most notably, the JAX reinforcement learning ecosystem does not have any benchmarks that test visual first-person tasks; these domains are crucial for testing both exploration and an agent's ability to overcome partial observability. We introduce JAXenstein: an open-source JAX-based benchmark that implements the Wolfenstein 3D rendering engine for fast and scalable experimentation in visual first-person tasks. JAXenstein is several times faster than comparable vision-based benchmarks, and is easily extensible to more complex first-person domains.

</details>


### [167] [Exploiting Non-Negativity in DAG Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19947)
*Samuel Rey,Madeline navarro,Gonzalo Mateos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性结构方程模型中的有向无环图（DAG）学习问题，通过引入非负边权约束，提出了一种基于乘子法的正则化非负DAG学习算法。在总体条件下证明了真实DAG是唯一全局最小化器，且不存在虚假内点驻点，数值实验表明该方法优于现有连续DAG学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续无环约束方法虽将离散DAG约束转化为平滑等式约束，但面临非凸优化困难和退化一阶最优性条件的问题。为克服这些挑战，本文利用非负边权结构简化无环性表征，从而获得更良性的优化景观。

Method: 限制DAG边权为非负，利用该结构获得更简单的无环性表征；提出正则化非负DAG学习问题，并基于乘子法（Augmented Lagrangian Method）设计优化算法；在总体条件下分析优化景观，证明真实DAG是唯一全局最小化器且无虚假驻点。

Result: 在总体条件下，真实DAG是提出的增广拉格朗日形式的唯一全局最小化器；优化景观中不存在虚假内点驻点，真实DAG是唯一的无环KKT点。合成数据和真实数据实验表明该方法优于现有连续DAG学习方法。

Conclusion: 非负边权约束能有效简化DAG学习的无环性表征，带来更良性的优化景观，所提方法在理论和实验上均优于现有连续DAG学习技术。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) from nodal observations generated by a linear structural equation model. DAG learning is a central task in signal processing, machine learning, and causal inference, but it remains challenging because acyclicity is a global combinatorial property. Continuous acyclicity constraints have led to important algorithmic advances by replacing the discrete DAG constraint with smooth equality constraints. However, existing formulations still involve difficult non-convex optimization landscapes and may suffer from degenerate first-order optimality conditions. Here, we restrict attention to DAGs with non-negative edge weights and exploit this additional structure to obtain a simpler characterization of acyclicity. Building on this characterization, we formulate a regularized non-negative DAG learning problem and develop an algorithm based on the method of multipliers. We further analyze the benign optimization landscape induced by non-negativity. In the population regime, we show that the true DAG is the unique global minimizer of the proposed augmented-Lagrangian formulation; moreover, the landscape contains no spurious interior stationary points, and the true DAG is the only acyclic KKT point. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real-world data show that the proposed method improves over state-of-the-art continuous DAG-learning alternatives.

</details>


### [168] [Detecting Fluent Optimization-Based Adversarial Prompts via Sequential Entropy Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19966)
*Mohammed Alshaalan,Miguel R. D. Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CPD，一种基于在线变点检测的对抗后缀检测方法，利用token级熵流和CUSUM统计量，无需训练即可在线检测LLM的优化型对抗后缀攻击，在六个开源模型上优于基于困惑度的基线方法，并可将检测点精确定位到对抗后缀内部。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化型对抗后缀能够欺骗基于困惑度的检测器，现有的静态或窗口式困惑度检测方法难以有效识别此类攻击，亟需一种更精准、可在线运行的检测方法。

Method: 将对抗后缀检测转化为token级next-token熵流上的在线变点检测问题；利用LLM系统提示估计鲁棒基线，标准化用户token熵值，施加单侧CUSUM统计量进行检测。

Result: 在1,012个优化攻击（GCG、AutoDAN等）和1,012个良性提示的基准上，CPD在六个开源模型上均优于窗口困惑度基线；在LLaMA-2-7B上AUROC达0.88，F1达0.82；79.6%的触发点落在对抗后缀内部；作为LLaMA Guard的轻量级门控可减少17-22%的防护调用。

Conclusion: CPD是一种模型无关、无需训练、可在线运行的高效对抗后缀检测方法，具有精准定位能力，并可作为门控机制降低安全防护成本。

Abstract: Optimization-based adversarial suffixes can jailbreak aligned large language models (LLMs) while remaining fluent, weakening static and windowed perplexity-based detectors. We cast adversarial suffix detection as an online change-point detection problem over the token-level next-token entropy stream. Using the LLM system prompt to estimate a robust baseline, we standardize user-token entropies and apply a one-sided CUSUM statistic. The resulting detector, CPD Online (CPD), is model-agnostic, training-free, runs online, and localizes the adversarial suffix onset. On a benchmark of 1,012 optimization-based suffix attacks (GCG, AutoDAN, AdvPrompter, BEAST, AutoDAN-HGA) and 1,012 perplexity-controlled benign prompts, CPD improves F1 over the strongest windowed-perplexity baseline on all six open-weight chat models (LLaMA-2-7B/13B, Vicuna-7B/13B, Qwen2.5-7B/14B). On LLaMA-2-7B at the canonical CUSUM setting ($k=0$), CPD reaches AUROC $0.88$ and F1 $0.82$. Beyond prompt-level detection, CPD concentrates 79.6% of its triggers inside the adversarial suffix, versus 17-46% for windowed perplexity. Finally, when used as a lightweight gate for LLaMA Guard, CPD reduces guard calls by 17-22% on a high-volume, benign-dominated deployment while preserving guard-level detection quality

</details>


### [169] [Your Neighbors Know: Leveraging Local Neighborhoods for Backdoor Detection in Decentralized Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19969)
*Sayan Biswas,Antoine Boutet,Davide Frey,Romaric Gaudel,Rachid Guerraoui,Maxime Jacovella,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Dimitri Lerévérend,François Taïani,Martijn de Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Argus是一种去中心化学习中原生的后门攻击检测框架，无需中央协调器或触发器先验知识。通过节点本地分析模型更新，利用结构相似性度量区分真正后门与数据异构性导致的误报，并从理论上证明了该方法能在高概率下过滤可疑更新的同时保持与标准DL相当的收敛速度。实验表明，Argus能将攻击成功率降低多达90个百分点，且模型效用与全知预言机相差在5个百分点以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化学习中后门攻击研究不足，现有防御方法往往忽视DL的约束条件（如无中央服务器、数据异构性等），亟需一种原生适配DL特性的后门检测机制。

Method: Argus框架中，诚实节点本地分析接收到的模型更新以识别潜在后门触发器；节点间通过邻居共享触发器信息，并采用结构相似性度量区分真正后门与数据异构性导致的误报——核心洞察在于假阳性触发器在不同节点间不一致，而真阳性触发器呈现一致模式。未通过协作测试的模型更新被拒绝，持续发送恶意更新的节点最终被驱逐。

Result: 在三个标准数据集上对比三种基线方法，Argus可将攻击成功率降低最多90个百分点；模型效用与全知预言机相差在5个百分点以内；且数据异构性越大，Argus相对于基线的优势越明显。

Conclusion: Argus是首个为DL后门检测提供理论收敛保证的工作，证明了高概率过滤可疑更新能保持与标准DL相当的收敛速度。该方法在去中心化环境下有效防御后门攻击，尤其适用于高数据异构性的实际场景。

Abstract: Decentralized learning (DL) is an emerging machine learning paradigm where nodes collaboratively train models without a central server. However, the collaborative nature of DL makes it vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a model is taught to behave normally on standard inputs while executing hidden, malicious actions when encountering data with specific triggers. Backdoor attacks in DL remain understudied and existing defenses often overlook DL constraints. We introduce Argus, a novel backdoor detection framework native to DL that requires neither a central coordinator nor prior knowledge of the trigger. In Argus, honest nodes locally analyze received model updates to identify potential backdoor triggers. Nodes then collectively share their triggers with their neighbors and use a structural similarity metric to separate true backdoors from false alarms induced by data heterogeneity. A key insight is that false positive triggers exhibit inconsistencies across participants while true positive ones show consistent patterns. Model updates that fail this collaborative test are rejected, and persistently malicious senders are eventually evicted. We provide the first theoretical convergence guarantees for a DL-specific backdoor detection mechanism, showing that filtering out suspicious model updates with high probability preserves a convergence rate comparable to standard DL. We implement and evaluate Argus on three standard datasets and against three state-of-the-art baselines. Across settings, Argus reduces attack success rates by up to 90 points compared to no defense, while preserving model utility within 5 percentage points of an omniscient oracle. Furthermore, the effectiveness of Argus compared to baselines improves as data heterogeneity increases.

</details>


### [170] [Block-Sphere Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19972)
*Heesang Ann,Joongkyu Lee,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文统一比较了基于旋转的量化方法（EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant），揭示了其性能优劣依赖于失真标准而非绝对；并提出了新的Block-Sphere Quantization（BlockQuant）算法，通过球形分块量化在理论上和实验上改进了基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旋转量化器（EDEN、RabitQ、TurboQuant）在不同的失真标准、概率假设和实现条件下难以进行公平比较，亟需一个统一的理论框架来厘清它们之间的相对优势。

Method: 首先对EDEN、RabitQ和TurboQuant进行了统一理论对比；然后提出了Block-Sphere Quantization（BlockQuant），一种基于旋转向量球面几何的分块量化算法，在球面上进行分块而非逐坐标量化。

Result: 理论分析表明：EDEN和TurboQuant在MSE失真上更优，EDEN在期望内积失真上也有效，RabitQ在大概率控制上更强；BlockQuant在重建MSE和期望内积失真上均优于基线。实验在真实嵌入数据集和长上下文LLM推理任务中验证了理论改进。

Conclusion: 旋转量化器的选择应基于具体的失真标准；BlockQuant通过球形分块设计更好地保留了旋转向量几何结构，实现了理论一致的实际性能提升。

Abstract: Vector quantization is a fundamental primitive for scalable machine learning systems, enabling memory-efficient storage, fast retrieval, and compressed inference. Recent rotation-based quantizers such as EDEN, RabitQ, and TurboQuant have introduced strong guarantees and empirical performance, but the surrounding comparisons have been difficult to interpret because they rely on different distortion criteria, probability regimes, and implementation assumptions. As our first contribution, we provide a unified theoretical comparison of these methods and show that their relative advantages are criterion-dependent rather than absolute: EDEN and TurboQuant are favorable for MSE distortion, EDEN is also effective for expected inner-product distortion, and RabitQ provides strong high-probability control. This comparison further clarifies that EDEN provides particularly strong guarantees for expected distortion measures. As our second contribution, we introduce Block-Sphere Quantization (BlockQuant), a new rotation-based block quantization algorithm designed around the spherical geometry of randomly rotated vectors. Unlike coordinate-wise quantizers, BlockQuant quantizes blocks on the sphere, preserving the geometry of rotated embeddings more faithfully. We prove that this block-spherical design theoretically improves over the baselines considered in this paper for both reconstruction MSE and expected inner-product distortion. Our experiments on real embedding datasets and long-context LLM inference tasks show practical gains that are consistent with our theoretical improvements.

</details>


### [171] [Learning with Foresight: Enhancing Neural Routing Policy via Multi-Node Lookahead Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19975)
*Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Yew-Soon Ong,Yingqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多节点前瞻预测（MnLP）训练策略，通过同时预测多个未来节点来扩展监督学习范式，解决神经策略在车辆路径问题中因短视决策导致的长程规划能力不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经策略训练范式主要聚焦于单步（next-node）预测进行解构建，导致短视决策，限制了长程规划能力。

Method: 引入因果性和可丢弃的MnLP模块（仅在训练时使用），通过多深度辅助监督扩展损失函数，使模型具备多步决策预见能力，且不增加推理开销。

Result: MnLP在多种问题规模、分布及真实世界基准上提升了神经策略的泛化能力，优于现有训练方法。

Conclusion: MnLP可无缝集成到多种神经架构中，在不引入额外推理开销的情况下显著提升长程上下文理解能力。

Abstract: Neural policies have shown promise in solving vehicle routing problems due to their reduced reliance on handcrafted heuristics. However, current training paradigms suffer from a fundamental limitation: they primarily focus on next-node prediction for solution construction, resulting in myopic decision-making that undermines long-horizon planning capacity. To this end, we introduce Multi-node Lookahead Prediction (MnLP), a novel training strategy that extends the supervised learning paradigm to predict multiple future nodes simultaneously. We incorporate causal and discardable MnLP modules that operate exclusively during training, facilitating models to anticipate multi-step decisions while preserving inference-time efficiency. By incorporating multi-depth auxiliary supervision into the loss function, MnLP equips neural policies with the ability of long-range contextual understanding. Experimentally, MnLP outperforms existing training methods, improving the generalization capability of neural policies across various problem sizes, distributions, and real-world benchmarks. Moreover, MnLP can be seamlessly integrated into diverse neural architectures without introducing additional inference overhead.

</details>


### [172] [LLM Benchmark Datasets Should Be Contamination-Resistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19999)
*Ali Al-Lawati,Jason Lucas,Dongwon Lee,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基准数据集应具有抗污染性（不可学习但支持推理），利用Transformer架构中训练与推理流程的不对称性，并呼吁社区推进抗污染方法、平台和评估管线的建设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量基准数据集被包含在预训练语料中（即污染），导致其作为模型泛化能力可靠指标的价值降低，亟需抗污染的评估方法。

Method: 首先强调基准数据集污染的普遍性并定义抗污染数据集属性；其次利用Transformer架构中训练与推理流程的不对称性实现抗污染；最后提出数学上的改进使其在不同LLM架构间可互操作。

Result: 提出了抗污染基准数据集的概念框架和实现方向，但未给出具体的实验结果或定量评估。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区从方法论、支撑平台和评估管线三方面推进抗污染基准的采纳，以确保LLM评测的可靠性。

Abstract: Benchmark datasets are critical for reproducible, reliable, and discriminative evaluation of LLMs. However, recent studies reveal that many benchmark datasets are included in pretraining corpora, i.e., $\textit{contaminated}$, which diminishes their value as reliable measures of model generalization.
