In this paper, we argue that benchmark datasets should be $\textit{contamination-resistant}$, i.e., $\textit{unlearnable}$, but support $\textit{inference}$. To accomplish this, we first highlight the wide prevalence of benchmark dataset contamination and outline the properties of contamination-resistant datasets. Second, we highlight how the asymmetry between the inference and training pipelines in the Transformer architecture can be leveraged to support contamination-resistance. Third, we outline mathematical advancements to make these datasets interoperable across various LLM architectures. Based on the above, we call on the community to ensure the reliability of LLM benchmarking by: (i) advancing novel contamination-resistant methodologies, (ii) developing supporting methods and platforms, and (iii) adopting contamination-resistant benchmarks into existing evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [173] [Fine-Tuning Without Forgetting via Loss-Adaptive Learning Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20005)
*Parjanya Prajakta Prashant,Jiongli Zhu,Aldan Creo,Babak Salimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FINCH方法，通过损失自适应的学习率调度（在高损失批次上降低学习率），在不修改微调目标函数的情况下，将灾难性遗忘减少93%，同时保持任务性能不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调大语言模型时，新任务学习与预训练能力保持之间存在矛盾（灾难性遗忘）。现有方法通过抑制高损失token来缓解遗忘，但这会阻碍新任务学习，尤其当新任务在预训练中覆盖不足时。需要一种在不牺牲新任务学习的前提下控制遗忘的方法。

Method: 首先理论推导出单步遗忘的上界为学习率与当前训练损失平方根的乘积，表明高损失批次更容易引发遗忘。基于此提出FINCH：一种损失自适应的学习率调度策略，在高损失批次上降低学习率，在模型收敛时提高学习率，不改变微调目标函数。

Result: 在知识获取、科学知识和低资源语言适应基准上，FINCH平均减少93%的遗忘，同时保持与标准微调相当的任务性能。在Qwen3-4B知识获取任务中，TruthfulQA退化降低5倍，HaluEval退化被逆转，同时更好保持了置信度校准。

Conclusion: 学习率调度是微调过程中塑造模型行为的有效工具，其作用不仅限于目标任务的优化，还能有效控制灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new data improves task performance but degrades capabilities learned during pretraining, a phenomenon known as catastrophic forgetting. Existing methods mitigate this by modifying the fine-tuning objective to suppress high-loss tokens or sequences, but these tokens are essential for learning new tasks, especially those with poor pretraining coverage. In such settings, hard tokens should still contribute to learning, so forgetting must be controlled without suppressing them. We identify a simple mechanism for doing so: per-step forgetting is bounded by the product of the learning rate and the square root of the current training loss. This suggests that high-loss batches are especially prone to inducing forgetting. Motivated by this observation, we introduce FINCH, a loss-adaptive learning-rate schedule that reduces the learning rate on high-loss batches and increases it as the model converges, while leaving the fine-tuning objective unchanged. Across knowledge acquisition, science, and low-resource language adaptation benchmarks, FINCH reduces forgetting by 93% on average while matching the task performance of standard fine-tuning. On Qwen3-4B knowledge acquisition, FINCH cuts TruthfulQA degradation by 5x and reverses HaluEval degradation, while better preserving confidence calibration. Overall, our results show that learning-rate schedules are an effective tool to shape model behavior during fine-tuning, beyond just target-task optimization.

</details>


### [174] [Training-Free Bayesian Filtering with Generative Emulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20028)
*Thomas Savary,François Rozet,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用扩散模型作为动力系统的仿真器，在不额外训练的情况下实现最优粒子滤波的变体，成功将粒子滤波扩展到高维非线性混沌系统（包括大气动力学）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子滤波在高维非线性动力系统中理论上精确但可扩展性差，而最优粒子滤波变体因经典数值求解器的实现困难而长期未被充分探索。

Method: 利用基于扩散模型的动力学系统仿真器，在不额外训练的情况下实现最优粒子滤波变体。

Result: 在非线性混沌系统（包括大气动力学）上的实验表明，该方法成功将粒子滤波扩展到高维设置。

Conclusion: 扩散模型仿真器可以有效解决粒子滤波在高维场景下的扩展性问题，为高维非线性动力系统的状态估计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Bayesian filtering is a well-known problem that aims to estimate plausible states of a dynamical system from observations. Among existing approaches to solve this problem, particle filters are theoretically exact for non-linear dynamics and observations, but suffer from poor scalability in high dimensions. In this work, we show that diffusion-based emulators of dynamical systems can be used to implement, without additional training, an optimal variant of particle filters that has remained largely unexplored due to implementation challenges with classical numerical solvers. Experiments on nonlinear chaotic systems, including atmospheric dynamics, demonstrate that the proposed approach successfully scales particle filtering to high-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [175] [Take It or Leave It: Intent-Controlled Partial Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20030)
*Salil Parth Tripathi,Bertrand Chapron,Fabrice Collard,Nicolas Courty,Ronan Fablet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出意图控制的部分最优传输（IC-POT），用逐点拒绝成本替代全局标量回扣机制，允许根据侧信息灵活决定哪些质量点参与匹配，并通过增广支撑转化为平衡Kantorovich问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统部分最优传输使用全局统一的回扣或标量预算来控制不匹配质量，但在实际应用中，拒绝决策应依赖于各点的具体特征（如可靠性、几何结构或外部信息），而非全局一致规则。这促使引入更细粒度的逐点拒绝机制。

Method: 将部分最优传输中的全局标量回扣扩展为两个测度上的逐点拒绝成本函数，从而构建IC-POT优化问题；证明其对偶形式具有局部接受阈值的物理解释；通过构造增广支撑空间，将原问题等价转化为一个平衡Kantorovich最优传输问题，从而可利用现有OT求解器高效求解。

Result: 在正-无标签学习（PU learning）和开放部分域适应（open-partial domain adaptation）任务中，利用逐点拒绝规则编码统计结构，提升了基线方法的性能。在地球物理多模态卫星海洋观测任务中，利用物理和传感器先验自然定义拒绝机制，提取可比较的信号信息。

Conclusion: IC-POT通过引入逐点拒绝成本，在理论上有对偶局部接受阈值的物理解释和增广支撑的求解保证，在实践中能够灵活融入侧信息，在多个应用场景（PU学习、开放域适应、遥感融合）中优于传统全局拒绝方案。

Abstract: While optimal transport (OT) enforces a rigid constraint by requiring two measures to be matched exactly, partial optimal transport relaxes this requirement by allowing mass to remain unmatched through a global budget, scalar rebate, or uniform rejection rule. However, many applications call for more structured, pointwise rejection mechanisms, where the decision to leave mass unmatched depends on side-specific reliability, support geometry, or external information about which components should participate in the comparison. We introduce \emph{intent-controlled partial optimal transport} (IC-POT), a targeted generalization of partial transport that replaces the global rejection paradigm with pointwise rejection costs over both measures. We show that the resulting optimization problem admits a dual interpretation in terms of local acceptance thresholds and can be solved by recasting it as a balanced Kantorovich OT problem on an augmented support. Beyond theoretical analysis, we demonstrate the practical relevance of IC-POT in settings where rejection is driven by side information. In positive-unlabeled learning and open-partial domain adaptation, incorporating pointwise rejection rules that encode statistical structure improves fixed baseline pipelines. Finally, we motivate the use of IC-POT with a geophysical practical case: multi-modal satellite ocean measurements, for which physical and sensors priors naturally inform the rejection mechanism and define the retrieved comparable signal information.

</details>


### [176] [CAMERA: Adapting to Semantic Camouflage in Unsupervised Text-Attributed Graph Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20032)
*Junjun Pan,Yixin Liu,Yu Zheng,Lianhua Chi,Alan Wee-Chung Liew,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAMERA框架，一种基于案例自适应的多专家混合架构，用于无监督文本属性图欺诈检测，通过解耦欺诈线索建模和上下文感知的专家门控机制，有效应对欺诈者的语义伪装策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本属性图欺诈检测方法严重依赖结构和属性线索，但欺诈者通过模仿正常用户的文本回应进行语义伪装，使得传统无监督方法难以识别。需要一种能自适应整合多类欺诈线索且对伪装鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出CAMERA框架，包含：(1) 自我解耦的混合专家架构，每个专家专门建模一种欺诈指示线索；(2) 上下文感知的门控模型，结合自我节点表示和局部邻域上下文自适应整合不同专家所学线索；(3) 利用欺诈者稀有性设计专家级无监督单类学习目标，鼓励建模正常模式，实现可靠的伪装欺诈检测。

Result: 在4个具有挑战性的数据集上，CAMERA持续优于现有竞争方法，证明其对语义伪装欺诈者的检测有效性。

Conclusion: CAMERA通过解耦的多专家架构和上下文自适应融合机制，有效解决了无监督场景下语义伪装欺诈检测难题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-attributed graph fraud detection (TAGFD) plays a critical role in preventing fraudulent activities on online social and e-commerce platforms. However, to evade detection, fraudsters continuously evolve their camouflaging strategies by deliberately mimicking textual responses of benign users, thereby concealing their malicious purposes. This phenomenon, referred to as semantic camouflage, fundamentally undermines commonly relied assumptions on how structural and attribute cues can be exploited to identify fraudsters, and makes it difficult to spot fraudsters with unsupervised TAGFD. To bridge the gaps, we propose a Case-Adaptive Multi-cue Expert fRAmework (CAMERA) for unsupervised TAGFD. CAMERA employs an ego-decoupled mixture-of-experts architecture, where each expert specializes in modeling a distinct type of fraud-indicative cue. A context-informed gating model is introduced to jointly consider the ego node representation and its local neighborhood context for adaptive integration of cues learned by different experts. Furthermore, CAMERA leverages the inherent rarity of fraudsters to support unsupervised one-class learning with expert-level objectives that encourage modeling dominant benign patterns, thereby enabling reliable unsupervised detection of camouflaged fraudsters. Experiments on 4 challenging datasets show that CAMERA consistently outperforms competitors, showing its effectiveness against semantically camouflaged fraudsters. Code available at https://github.com/CampanulaBells/CAMERA

</details>


### [177] [D$^3$-Subsidy: Online and Sequential Driver Subsidy Decision-Making for Large-Scale Ride-Hailing Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20036)
*Taijie Chen,Rui Su,Siyuan Feng,Laoming Zhang,Hongyang Zhang,Haijiao Wang,Zhaofeng Ma,Jintao Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D³-Subsidy，一种基于扩散的分层框架，用于网约车平台的城市级司机端补贴控制。该框架通过前缀条件扩散模型生成未来轨迹、上下文条件逆模块解码控制信号、拉格朗日对偶映射实现补贴率上限约束，并采用多城市预训练+参数高效微调实现跨城市迁移。离线评估和线上A/B测试均验证了其在提升完单量（Rides）和GMV的同时，严格遵守补贴率上限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网约车平台（如滴滴）需要在高度动态的环境中平衡司机供给与乘客需求。司机端补贴是调节供需的关键杠杆，但在生产环境中优化补贴需要同时满足三个约束：(i) 对随机冲击的响应性，(ii) 严格的补贴率上限，(iii) 城市级低延迟执行。传统的逐订单优化方法成本过高，亟需一种前瞻性、约束感知的城市级在线序列决策控制器。

Method: 提出D³-Subsidy分层扩散框架：(1) 前缀条件扩散模型——从不可变历史观测中采样未来轨迹，弥合训练-推理分布差异；(2) 上下文条件逆模块——将生成的轨迹计划解码为低维城市级控制信号；(3) 拉格朗日对偶导出映射——将补贴率上限直接嵌入订单-司机激励中，避免迭代优化；(4) 多城市预训练+参数高效微调——实现异构城市间的鲁棒迁移。

Result: 离线评估显示D³-Subsidy在提升完单量（Rides）和GMV的同时增强了补贴率上限合规性；真实世界A/B测试确认了显著的业务提升，同时将预算相关违规指标控制在运营阈值内。

Conclusion: D³-Subsidy成功解决了网约车平台城市级补贴控制中响应性、约束合规性和低延迟可扩展性之间的权衡，为大规模在线决策问题提供了可行的扩散模型+约束优化的解决方案。

Abstract: Ride-hailing platforms like DiDi Chuxing operate in highly dynamic environments where balancing driver supply and passenger demand is critical. Although driver-side subsidies serve as a primary lever to align these forces and improve key KPIs like completed rides (\texttt{Rides}) and gross merchandise value (\texttt{GMV}), optimizing them in production requires simultaneously meeting three constraints: (i) responsiveness to stochastic shocks, (ii) strict subsidy-rate caps, and (iii) low-latency execution at city scale. These requirements rule out expensive per-order optimization, calling for a forward-looking, constraint-aware city-level controller for online sequential decision making. To meet these requirements, we introduce D$^3$-Subsidy (Dynamic Driver-side Diffusion-based Subsidy), a hierarchical diffusion-based framework for deployable city-wide subsidy control. To bridge the train-inference gap, D$^3$-Subsidy employs a prefix-conditioned diffusion model that samples plausible future trajectories from immutable historical observations, ensuring the training protocol aligns with the fixed-history nature of online deployment. These generated plans are then decoded by a context-conditioned inverse module into low-dimensional city-level control signals. For scalable execution, we bridge the gap between city-level planning and fine-grained dispatch via a Lagrangian-dual-derived mapping, which embeds subsidy-rate caps directly into order-driver incentives without iterative optimization. Additionally, a multi-city pretraining strategy with parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables robust transfer across heterogeneous cities. Extensive offline evaluations demonstrate that D$^3$-Subsidy improves \texttt{Rides} and \texttt{GMV} while enhancing cap compliance, and a real-world A/B test confirms significant uplift while keeping budget-related violation metrics within operational thresholds.

</details>


### [178] [Active Context Selection Improves Simple Regret in Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20040)
*Mohammad Shahverdikondori,Jalal Etesami,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限上下文空间下的上下文多臂老虎机问题，允许学习器主动选择采样哪些上下文。对于已知上下文分布p，被动采样达到O(√(n/T) ||p||_{1/2})的遗憾，主动采样（分配q_j∝p_j^{2/3}）达到紧致界O(√(n/T) ||p||_{2/3})，改进可达Θ(k^{1/4})。进一步扩展到预算主动采样和未知p情形，提出了EETC算法，在长时间尺度下匹配已知p的主动采样率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文多臂老虎机问题中，现有工作通常假设上下文随机出现，但实际中某些子群体可能更重要或采样成本不同。动机在于：在有限上下文空间下，允许学习器主动选择采样哪个上下文，以最小化基于上下文权重的简单遗憾，实现实例依赖的遗憾保证。

Method: 1) 理论刻画：对于已知p，推导被动采样遗憾界√(n/T)||p||_{1/2}和主动采样最优分配q_j∝p_j^{2/3}下的紧致界√(n/T)||p||_{2/3}；2) 扩展至预算主动采样，刻画紧致率及有限预算何时可恢复完全主动率；3) 对于未知p，提出Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC)算法，分阶段平衡上下文分布估计与主动分配切换时机。

Result: 主动采样相比被动采样的遗憾改进可达Θ(k^{1/4})；预算主动采样下明确了恢复完全主动率的条件；EETC算法在长时间尺度下匹配已知p的主动采样率（至多常数因子）；合成与真实数据实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 在有限上下文空间的多臂老虎机中，主动采样策略能显著降低基于上下文权重的简单遗憾，改进程度与上下文数量k相关；对于未知上下文分布，EETC算法能自适应地接近最优主动采样性能。

Abstract: We study the contextual multi-armed bandit problem with a finite context space (a.k.a. subpopulations), where the learner recommends a best action for each context and is evaluated by context-weighted simple regret. Our guarantees are worst-case over the reward distributions, while remaining instance-dependent with respect to the context distribution vector $p$. Akin to experimental design problems where the population of interest is fixed but the sampled subpopulation can be controlled, we allow the learner to actively choose which context to sample from. For a known $p$, we characterize tight regret rates: passive sampling where contexts are randomly revealed achieves regret of order $\sqrt{n/T \, \lVert p \rVert_{1/2}}$, whereas active sampling with allocation $q_j \propto p_j^{2/3}$ achieves the tight rate $\sqrt{n/T} \, \lVert p \rVert_{2/3}$. The resulting improvement can be as large as $Θ(k^{1/4})$, where $k$ is the number of contexts. We further extend the analysis to budgeted active sampling, characterize the corresponding tight rate, and identify when a limited active budget suffices to recover the fully active rate. When $p$ is unknown, we propose the Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC) algorithm, which optimally balances estimating the context distribution and the time to switch to active allocation, such that for large horizons, it matches the known-$p$ active rate up to constants. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [179] [Smooth Partial Lotteries for Stable Randomized Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20069)
*Alexander Goldberg,Giulia Fanti,Nihar B. Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出平滑性作为部分抽签机制的设计原则，形式化为从评分到选择概率的Lipschitz条件，并引入裁剪线性抽签（Clipped Linear Lottery），该机制在选择概率与质量评分之间建立线性关系，在理论上达到了最优的平滑性-遗憾权衡，实验证明现有抽签设计高度不稳定，而所提机制在真实评审数据上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分抽签机制存在固有缺陷：对单个候选人分数的微小改动会导致选择概率剧烈波动，这种不稳定性削弱了抽签减少决策边界附近细粒度分数区分影响的核心目标。

Method: 提出平滑性设计原则，形式化为Lipschitz连续性条件；引入裁剪线性抽签机制，选择概率在两个阈值之间随估计质量线性变化；理论上推导了最坏情况遗憾的下界，并与个体公平性、差分隐私等稳定性概念进行比较。

Result: 裁剪线性抽签的最坏情况遗憾在(1-k/n)因子内达到任意平滑选择规则的下界；在ICLR 2025、NeurIPS 2024和瑞士国家科学基金会的真实同行评审数据上，现有抽签设计在单分数扰动下高度不稳定，而裁剪线性抽签在平滑性-效用权衡上优于替代方案。

Conclusion: 平滑性是部分抽签机制的有效设计原则，裁剪线性抽签在理论和实践上均实现了更好的稳定性-效用权衡，可作为一种更稳定的替代方案应用于各类竞争性选拔场景。

Abstract: Competitive selection processes, from scientific funding to admissions and hiring, use evaluations to score candidates, and eventually choose a subset of them based on those scores. Recently, many organizations have adopted partial lotteries, which randomize selection based on evaluation scores. However, existing lottery designs are inherently unstable, as a small change to a single candidate's score can cause large shifts in their selection probabilities. This instability undermines a key goal of lotteries: reducing the influence of fine-grained score distinctions near the decision boundary. We propose smoothness as a design principle for partial lotteries, formalizing it as a Lipschitz condition on the mapping from review scores over candidates to selection probabilities. We introduce the Clipped Linear Lottery, a simple mechanism in which selection probabilities scale linearly with estimated quality between an upper threshold, above which we always accept, and a lower threshold, below which we always reject. We prove that the Clipped Linear Lottery's worst-case regret matches a lower bound for any smooth selection rule up to a factor of $(1 - k/n)$, where $k/n$ is the acceptance rate. We compare smooth selection to other stability notions like Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy, showing that the Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-regret tradeoff than alternatives. Experiments on real peer review data from ICLR 2025, NeurIPS 2024, and the Swiss National Science Foundation demonstrate that existing lottery designs are highly unstable in practice even under perturbations to a single score. Our experiments also confirm the tightness of our theoretical analysis and show that our proposed Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-utility tradeoff than alternatives in practice.

</details>


### [180] [INSHAPE: Instance-Level Shapelets for Interpretable Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20088)
*Seongjun Lee,Seokhyun Lee,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INSHAPE提出了一种实例级别的时间序列shapelet发现框架，通过识别每个时间序列特有的可变长度非重叠判别性子序列并建模其时间依赖关系，在128个UCR和30个UEA基准数据集上持续优于现有最先进的shapelet方法，同时提供了更直观的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有shapelet方法主要关注在全局数据集上优化的种群级别shapelet，导致两个根本性局限：(i) 种群级别的模式常与实例特定的特征不匹配，导致次优性能和可能误导的解释；(ii) 多数方法将shapelet视为独立实体，忽略了多个模式间重要的时间依赖关系和交互作用。

Method: INSHAPE提出可解释的TSC框架，能够发现每个时间序列特有的可变长度判别性子序列，将其识别为非重叠片段并建模其时序依赖关系；同时通过自底向上的方法聚合实例级shapelet为原型（种群级）shapelet，桥接局部与全局可解释性。

Result: 在128个UCR和30个UEA基准数据集上的大量实验表明，INSHAPE持续优于最先进的基于shapelet的方法，同时提供更直观和可解释的洞察。

Conclusion: INSHAPE通过实例级别的shapelet发现与时序依赖建模，有效克服了种群级别shapelet的局限性，在提升分类性能的同时增强了模型可解释性，为时间序列分类提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Discovering shapelets -- i.e., discriminative temporal patterns within time series -- has been widely studied to address the inherent complexity of time-series classification (TSC) and to make model decision-making processes more transparent. However, existing methods primarily focus on population-level shapelets optimized across the entire dataset, which leads to two fundamental limitations: (i) population-level patterns often misalign with instance-specific features, resulting in suboptimal performance and potentially misleading interpretations, and (ii) most methods treat shapelets as independent entities, overlooking important temporal dependencies and interactions among multiple patterns. To address these limitations, we propose INSHAPE, an interpretable TSC framework that discovers variable-length, discriminative temporal patterns specific to each time series. INSHAPE identifies these patterns as non-overlapping segments and models their temporal dependencies, thereby providing clear instance-level interpretations while achieving strong predictive performance. Furthermore, INSHAPE bridges local and global interpretability through a bottom-up approach, aggregating instance-level shapelets into prototypical (population-level) shapelets. Extensive experiments on 128 UCR and 30 UEA benchmark datasets show that INSHAPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art shapelet-based methods while providing more intuitive and interpretable insights.

</details>


### [181] [Draft Less, Retrieve More: Hybrid Tree Construction for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20104)
*Yuhao Shen,Tianyu Liu,Xinyi Hu,Quan Kong,Baolin Zhang,Jun Dai,Jun Zhang,Shuang Ge,Lei Chen,Yue Li,Mingcheng Wan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graft框架，通过“剪枝-嫁接”机制将检索令牌填充到剪枝后的位置，在不增加开销的情况下补偿覆盖损失，实现推测解码中密集树与剪枝树之间的帕累托优化，在短上下文、长上下文和大规模模型上均取得显著加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码中，密集草稿树虽能最大化接受率，但带来严重的VRAM带宽和计算开销，成为端到端加速的瓶颈；而动态深度剪枝虽能降低延迟，却会丢弃潜在有效候选，导致接受率无法达到密集树的上限。现有方法无法打破这一帕累托权衡。

Method: 提出Graft框架，核心是“剪枝-嫁接”顺序机制：先对草稿树进行动态深度剪枝释放计算预算，再将检索到的高预测性令牌填充到剪枝所打开的拓扑间隙中，以近零开销补偿覆盖损失并恢复接受长度。该方法完全无需训练且无损。

Result: 在短上下文基准上实现最高5.41倍加速，在Qwen3-235B大规模模型上平均加速比EAGLE-3提升21.8%；在长上下文和大规模模型场景中均建立新的帕累托前沿；初步实验表明该方法可扩展到DFlash风格的分块草稿范式。

Conclusion: Graft通过将剪枝与检索作为相互增强的操作，成功打破了推测解码中密集树与剪枝树之间的帕累托权衡，在多种实际部署场景下建立了新的性能前沿，且无需训练、完全无损。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by leveraging a draft-then-verify paradigm. To maximize the acceptance rate, recent methods construct expansive draft trees, which unfortunately incur severe VRAM bandwidth and computational overheads that bottleneck end-to-end speedups. While dynamic-depth pruning can reduce this latency by removing marginal branches, it also discards potentially valid candidates, preventing the acceptance rate from reaching the upper bound of dense trees. In this paper, we identify a critical opportunity in resource allocation: the transition from dense to pruned drafting frees up significant computational budget. To break this Pareto tradeoff, we introduce Graft, a compensation framework that couples pruning and retrieval as mutually reinforcing operations. Pruning supplies sufficient budget for retrieval, while retrieval compensates for pruning-induced coverage loss and recovers accepted length. By employing a sequential `prune-then-graft' mechanism, Graft attaches highly predictive retrieved tokens into positions opened by pruning, filling the topological gaps with near-zero overhead. Graft is entirely training-free and lossless. Comprehensive evaluations show that Graft establishes a new Pareto frontier across practical deployment settings, including short-context generation, long-context generation, and large-scale models. On short-context benchmarks, it achieves up to 5.41$\times$ speedup and improves average speedup over EAGLE-3 by up to 21.8% on the large-scale Qwen3-235B. We also provide a preliminary exploration of applying Graft to the DFlash-style block drafting paradigm, offering initial evidence and insights for extending grafting beyond autoregressive draft trees.

</details>


### [182] [Beyond Isotropy in JEPAs: Hamiltonian Geometry and Symplectic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20107)
*Robert Jenkinson Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出JEPA中常用的各向同性高斯正则化会引入欧几里得对称性偏差，在已知下游结构几何时，最优协方差为(c/d)H^{-1}，而欧几里得更正有可计算的代价；当下游几何未知时，任何固定边际目标都不具规范性。即便知道完美的一视图边际，也无法确定视图间的预测耦合。因此，结构性偏差应进入交叉视图耦合而非编码器边际。作者提出HamJEPA，将每个视图编码为相空间状态(q,p)，用学习到的哈密顿跳跃映射预测视图间转换，并通过非各向同性尺度和谱地板防止坍塌。在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法对各视图嵌入施加各向同性高斯正则化，隐式引入了欧几里得对称性偏差。本文旨在揭示这种偏差在下游几何已知或未知时的代价，并论证结构性偏差应置于视图间耦合而非编码器边际。

Method: 理论分析：推导已知下游几何H下最小极大和最大熵协方差为(c/d)H^{-1}，证明欧几里得更正的代价；证明任何固定边际在下游几何未知时都不具规范性；证明即使完美的单视图边际也无法确定视图间耦合。基于此提出HamJEPA：将每个视图编码为相空间状态(q,p)，用学习到的哈密顿跳跃映射预测视图间转换，通过非各向同性尺度和谱地板防止坍塌。

Result: CIFAR-100上，30 epoch时kNN@20提升+4.89，线性探测提升+3.52；80 epoch时分别提升+6.45和+10.64。消融实验表明辛耦合是驱动邻域几何收益的关键。ImageNet-100上，45 epoch时kNN@20提升+4.82，线性探测提升+7.52。

Conclusion: JEPA的结构性偏差应被置于交叉视图耦合而非编码器边际。HamJEPA通过哈密顿动力学建模视图间转换，既保留了JEPA的预测框架，又避免了各向同性正则化的固有缺陷，在多个基准上取得显著改进。

Abstract: JEPAs often regularize one-view embeddings toward an isotropic Gaussian, implicitly baking Euclidean symmetry into the representation. We show that this is not merely a benign default. For a known structured downstream geometry $H\succ0$, the minimax and maximum-entropy covariance under a Hamiltonian energy budget is $(c/d)H^{-1}$, and Euclidean isotropy incurs a closed-form price of isotropy. More importantly, when the downstream geometry is unknown, no geometry-independent fixed marginal target is canonical: every fixed covariance shape can be maximally misaligned for some structured geometry. We further show that even oracle one-view marginals do not identify the JEPA view-to-view predictive coupling. These results suggest that the structural bias in JEPAs should enter the cross-view coupling rather than a fixed encoder marginal. We instantiate this principle with \textbf{HamJEPA}, which encodes each view as a phase-space state $(q,p)$ and predicts view-to-view transitions with a learned Hamiltonian leapfrog map, while non-isotropic scale and spectral floors prevent collapse. In a deliberately headless token protocol, HamJEPA improves over SIGReg on CIFAR-100 by $+4.89$ kNN@20 and $+3.52$ linear-probe points at 30 epochs, and by $+6.45$ kNN@20 and $+10.64$ linear-probe points at 80 epochs, while a matched MLP predictor ablation shows that the symplectic coupling is the ingredient driving the neighborhood-geometry gain. On ImageNet-100, HamJEPA-$q$ improves by $+4.82$ kNN@20 and $+7.52$ linear-probe points at 45 epochs.

</details>


### [183] [Toto 2.0: Time Series Forecasting Enters the Scaling Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20119)
*Emaad Khwaja,Chris Lettieri,Gerald Woo,Eden Belouadah,Marc Cenac,Guillaume Jarry,Enguerrand Paquin,Xunyi Zhao,Viktoriya Zhukov,Othmane Abou-Amal,Chenghao Liu,Ameet Talwalkar,David Asker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了时间序列基础模型的规模缩放能力，通过单一训练方案从4M参数扩展到2.5B参数均能提升预测质量。发布了Toto 2.0系列五个开源权重预测模型，在BOOM、GIFT-Eval和TIME三个基准上取得最优结果，并详细描述了架构设计、训练方案、数据及超参数迁移流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证时间序列基础模型是否具有规模缩放效应（即增大参数量能否持续提升预测质量），并建立一套统一的、可复现的高质量训练方案。

Method: 采用统一的训练方案，在4M到2.5B参数范围内进行模型缩放；设计Toto 2.0架构；构建训练数据；使用u-muP超参数迁移流程实现不同规模模型间的超参数高效迁移。

Result: Toto 2.0系列模型在BOOM、GIFT-Eval和TIME三个时间序列预测基准上均达到当前最优性能，且性能随参数量增大而一致提升。

Conclusion: 时间序列基础模型具备可靠的规模缩放能力，Toto 2.0可作为开源通用时间序列预测模型的新基准。

Abstract: We show that time series foundation models scale: a single training recipe produces reliable forecast-quality improvements from 4M to 2.5B parameters. We release Toto 2.0, a family of five open-weights forecasting models trained under this recipe. The Toto 2.0 family sets a new state of the art on three forecasting benchmarks: BOOM, our observability benchmark; GIFT-Eval, the standard general-purpose benchmark; and the recent contamination-resistant TIME benchmark. This report describes our experimental results and details the design decisions behind Toto 2.0: its architecture and training recipe, training data, and the u-muP hyperparameter transfer pipeline. All five base checkpoints are released under Apache 2.0.

</details>


### [184] [Optimal Representation Size: High-Dimensional Analysis of Pretraining and Linear Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20105)
*Valentina Njaradi,Clémentine Dominé,Rachel Swanson,Marco Mondelli,Andrew Saxe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过高维统计理论建模，解析了预训练（无监督主成分分析）与下游微调（线性回归）之间的泛化关系，揭示了表示维度、数据量与任务对齐度对下游性能的精确影响，并给出了最优表示维度的选择准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解从有限数据中泛化的基本挑战，解析"无监督预训练+有监督微调"这一两阶段范式的理论基础，量化无标注数据与有标注数据之间的替代关系。

Method: 将结构提取形式化为无标注数据的主成分分析（PCA），下游学习形式化为有标注数据的线性回归。在高维极限下推导出训练误差与泛化误差的精确解析表达式。

Result: 预训练表示强烈影响下游泛化；当预训练数据充足而下游数据稀缺时，最大程度压缩的低维表示最优；当预训练数据有限时，高维表示泛化更好；建立了无标注数据与有标注数据之间的精确权衡关系。

Conclusion: 优化表示维度至关重要：压缩在什么条件下能提升泛化性能得到了严格刻画，该现象在自编码器和预训练大语言模型中同样存在。

Abstract: Learning to generalise from limited data is a fundamental challenge for both artificial and biological systems. A common strategy is to extract reusable structure from abundant unlabelled data, enabling efficient adaptation to new tasks from limited labelled data. This two-stage paradigm is now standard in modern training pipelines, where pretraining is followed by fine-tuning or linear probing. We provide an analytical model of this process: structure extraction is formalized as principal component analysis on unlabelled data, and downstream learning as linear regression on a separate labelled dataset. In the high-dimensional regime, we derive exact expressions for training and generalisation error showcasing their dependence on representation dimensionality, unlabelled and labelled sample sizes, and task alignment. Our results show that pretrained representations strongly influence downstream generalisation, and we characterize the optimal representation size as a function of task parameters: with abundant pretraining data but scarce downstream data, maximally compressed representations are optimal, whereas with limited pretraining data, higher-dimensional representations generalise better. Furthermore, we establish an exact trade-off between pretraining and supervision, quantifying how much unlabelled data is required to replace a single labelled sample. Beyond our idealised model, we observe similar phenomenology in autoencoders and pretrained LLMs. Altogether, we highlight that optimising representation size is critical, giving conditions for when compression during pretraining improves generalisation.

</details>


### [185] [Atoms of Thought: Universal EEG Representation Learning with Microstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20182)
*Xinyang Tian,Ruitao Liu,Ziyi Ye,Siyang Xue,Xin Wang,Xuesong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微状态（microstates）的通用脑电图（EEG）表示学习方法，通过将连续EEG信号聚类为离散微状态序列构建通用分词器，在睡眠分期、情感识别和运动想象分类等多个下游任务中优于传统时域和频域特征，并展现出更强的可解释性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统EEG表示学习将EEG视为多元时间序列，提取时域或频域特征，但缺乏统一的、可跨任务泛化的表示方法。本文旨在探索一种简单而有效的通用EEG表示——微状态，以构建跨任务通用的特征表示。

Method: 从大规模医学EEG数据集中，通过聚类将连续EEG信号分割为离散微状态序列，构建通用微状态分词器（microstate tokenizer）；将该分词器统一应用于睡眠分期、情感识别、运动想象分类等多个下游任务。

Result: 基于微状态的EEG表示学习在不同模型和不同任务上均优于传统时域和频域特征；微状态具有更高的可解释性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 微状态作为一种简单有效的通用EEG表示，为认知神经科学和临床研究提供了新的范式，有望推动BCI和神经信息学领域的跨任务通用表示学习发展。

Abstract: Learning universal representations from electroencephalogram (EEG) signals is a cutting-edge approach in the field of neuroinformatics and brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). Conventionally, EEG is treated as a multivariate temporal signal, where time- or frequency-domain features are extracted for representation learning. This paper investigates a simple yet effective EEG representation, i.e., microstates. Microstates represent the building blocks of brain activity patterns at a microscopic time scale. We build a universal microstate tokenizer from a large medical EEG dataset by clustering continuous EEG signals into sequences of discrete microstates. The microstate tokenizer is then adopted universally across a series of downstream tasks, including sleep staging, emotion recognition, and motor imagery classification. Experimental results show that EEG representation learning with microstates outperforms traditional time-domain and frequency-domain features under different models and across different tasks. Further analysis shows that microstates offer greater interpretability and scalability, thereby opening up applications in both cognitive neuroscience and clinical research.

</details>


### [186] [TrajTok: Adaptive Spatial Tokenization for Trajectory Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20134)
*Zhen Xiong,Shang-Ling Hsu,Cyrus Shahabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TrajTok提出了一种基于多分辨率六边形网格分词化与掩码标记预训练的轨迹编码器，通过分解式Transformer架构分别建模几何与运动学特征，在多种轨迹任务上实现了通用且可迁移的表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始GPS轨迹数据具有连续、噪声大、采样不规则等特点，且空间分词化面临两难：细粒度网格导致稀疏细胞和弱嵌入，粗粒度网格则会将异质运动模式合并到同一标记中。现有方法难以学习可泛化的通用轨迹表示。

Method: TrajTok首先根据GPS点空间分布学习多分辨率六边形网格分区，将噪声GPS序列转化为离散细胞标记；然后采用分解式Transformer编码器，包含早期按模态分离的自注意力模块、跨注意力融合层以及时空旋转位置编码(ST-RoPE)，分别编码几何与运动学信息；最后通过掩码标记建模进行预训练，从局部轨迹观测中恢复几何结构和运动模式。

Result: 在Porto数据集上，冻结的TrajTok编码器搭配轻量任务适配器，在轨迹相似性搜索、分类、ETA和全程旅行时间回归任务中均超越多种任务专用方法。同一冻结编码器同时支持几何主导和运动学主导的任务。

Conclusion: 学习型多分辨率空间分词化结合掩码标记预训练是构建通用轨迹基础模型的有前景方向；TrajTok学习到了可迁移的轨迹结构而非任务特定的捷径。

Abstract: Learning generalizable trajectory representations from raw GPS traces remains difficult because the data is continuous, noisy, and irregularly sampled. Spatial tokenization is also challenging: fine grids yield sparse cells with weak embeddings, while coarse grids merge heterogeneous movement patterns into the same token. We present TrajTok, a trajectory encoder with a simple pretraining recipe for transferable trajectory embeddings. TrajTok first learns a multi-resolution hexagonal cell partition from the spatial distribution of GPS points, converting noisy GPS sequences into discrete cell tokens. To capture both geometry and kinematics, it uses a factorized transformer encoder with early per-modality self-attention blocks, cross-attention fusion layers, and spatiotemporal rotary position embeddings, ST-RoPE, to encode where and when each token occurs. TrajTok is pretrained with masked-token modeling that recovers both geometric structure and kinematic patterns from partial trajectory observations. On the Porto dataset, a frozen TrajTok encoder with lightweight task adapters achieves strong performance across trajectory similarity search, classification, estimated time of arrival, and full travel-time regression, outperforming multiple task-specific methods. The same frozen encoder supports both geometry-dominated and kinematics-dominated tasks, suggesting that TrajTok learns transferable trajectory structure rather than task-specific shortcuts. These results indicate that learned multi-resolution spatial tokenization combined with masked-token pretraining is a promising direction for general-purpose trajectory foundation models.

</details>


### [187] [When Does Model Collapse Occur in Structured Interactive Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20151)
*Yuchen Wu,Kangjie Zhou,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式AI在交互式学习环境中的模型崩溃问题，通过有向图形式化模型间的交互模式，推导出模型崩溃发生的充要条件，并在线性回归和一般M估计量上建立了有限样本和渐近理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注单模型在自己的合成输出上训练导致的模型崩溃，未能捕捉多模型交互环境下的模型性能退化。随着生成式AI的普及，模型参数不仅由自然数据更新，还由其他模型生成的合成数据更新，这一交互式学习范式破坏了经典统计学习的独立同分布假设，亟需建立可靠的统计推断框架。

Method: 使用有向图对模型间的交互模式进行形式化建模，通过分析图拓扑结构推导模型崩溃的充要条件；针对线性回归建立有限样本理论结果，对一般M估计量建立渐近保证。

Result: 模型崩溃是否发生关键取决于交互图的拓扑结构；给出了显式的充要条件；在线性回归上获得有限样本结果，在一般M估计量上获得渐近理论保证；通过大量数值实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 交互图拓扑是决定多模型交互学习环境中模型是否崩溃的核心因素，本文的理论框架为理解和缓解交互式学习场景中的模型退化提供了基础。

Abstract: The proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has given rise to an interactive learning environment, where model parameters are continuously updated using not only data generated by natural processes, but also synthetic outputs produced by other models. This paradigm introduces two major challenges: (1) training data are no longer drawn exclusively from the target population, undermining a core assumption of classical statistical learning, and (2) model training processes become inherently correlated, as models interact with one another through repeated exposure to each other's synthetic outputs in a potentially complex manner. Establishing reliable statistical inference in such structured interactive learning environments therefore remains an important open problem. In particular, there is growing concern about model collapse, a phenomenon in which the performance of generative models progressively degrades as they are trained on synthetic data produced by earlier model generations.
  Prior work on model collapse primarily focuses on a single model trained on its own output, failing to capture model performance in multi-model interactive settings. In this work, we fill this gap by investigating the performance of generative models in an interactive learning environment with general interaction patterns. In particular, we formalize model interactions using directed graphs and show that the occurrence of model collapse depends critically on the topology of the interaction graph. We further derive an explicit necessary and sufficient condition characterizing when model collapse occurs, and establish finite-sample results for linear regression and asymptotic guarantees for general M-estimators. We support our theoretical findings through extensive numerical experiments.

</details>


### [188] [SAGE: Scalable Automatic Gating Ensemble for Confident Negative Harvesting in Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20157)
*Sudheer Tubati,Amit Goyal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAGE是一种基于反事实感知的负样本筛选方法，结合SimHash分层采样与模块化门控集成（马氏距离+k-NN密度），有效解决音乐流媒体欺诈检测中正例-未标注学习（PU Learning）的表示偏差问题，在兼顾super-fans、睡眠音乐等合法边缘案例的同时，实现高精度欺诈识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欺诈检测方法难以区分合法边缘案例（如超级粉丝、睡眠音乐会话）与协同欺诈行为，且正例-未标注学习（PU Learning）中存在表示偏差问题，缺乏对稀有行为群体的覆盖。

Method: 提出SAGE框架：1）基于SimHash的分层采样策略，结合下限约束采样确保稀有行为群体的覆盖；2）模块化门控集成架构，内含可插拔统计门（马氏距离和k-NN密度），通过可配置投票阈值实现自适应精确率-召回率权衡。

Result: 在保留数据上展现出强精确率和召回率；且无需修改核心方法即可在用户级别和艺术家级别欺诈检测上均取得优异性能。

Conclusion: SAGE是一种通用的欺诈检测方法，有效解决PU学习中的表示偏差问题，可跨领域泛化应用于不同级别的欺诈检测任务。

Abstract: Music streaming fraud, where bad actors artificially inflate stream counts to manipulate chart rankings and royalty payments, poses a significant threat to streaming services and legitimate content creators. Traditional fraud detection approaches struggle with a critical challenge: many legitimate edge cases, including super-fans and sleep-music sessions, exhibit activity patterns that closely mimic those of coordinated fraud. We present SAGE, a novel counterfactual-aware negative harvesting approach that combines SimHash-based stratified sampling with a modular gating ensemble for confident negative identification from unlabeled data. Our ensemble architecture employs pluggable statistical gates (currently instantiated with Mahalanobis distance and k-NN density) with configurable voting thresholds enabling adaptive precision-recall trade-offs. This addresses the representation bias problem in Positive-Unlabeled learning by ensuring comprehensive coverage of rare behavioral cohorts through floor-constrained sampling. Evaluation demonstrates strong precision and recall on held-out data. The approach generalizes across fraud detection domains, achieving strong performance on both customer-level and artist-level fraud without modification to the core methodology.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文倡导开发一种名为“数据探针”（data probes）的系统化方法，通过从随机过程中生成合成序列，来揭示数据特征如何影响大语言模型（LLM）在不同工作阶段（训练、微调、对齐、上下文学习等）的性能、泛化性和鲁棒性，从而超越当前依赖大量实验经验启发式的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对数据如何影响LLM的理解仍不充分，现有方法依赖大规模实验获得经验性启发式规则，计算成本高且缺乏对数据特征驱动LLM行为的本质理解。

Method: 提出从适当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列（称为“数据探针”），这些序列具有可观测的统计特性（如典型集理论），通过观察LLM在这些探针上的行为，系统研究数据特征对模型的影响。

Result: 数据探针可以用于LLM工作流程的一个或多个阶段，通过可控的统计特性揭示数据如何影响模型性能、泛化性和鲁棒性，为数据作用提供超越经验启发式的理论洞察。

Conclusion: 数据探针方法为理解数据在LLM训练和推理中的基础性作用提供了一条系统化路径，有助于建立从理论概念（如典型集）到LLM行为描述的桥梁。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [190] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微服务架构的文档理解生产系统，将分类、OCR和LLM结构化提取等多个模型封装为流水线，支持每小时处理数千份多页文档。通过混合分类、GPU/CPU分离、异步IO和独立水平扩展等设计，并发现OCR是端到端延迟的主要瓶颈，系统并发受限于共享GPU推理容量而非工作线程数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术研究集中于文档理解的新模型，导致模型定义与生产规模运行之间存在巨大鸿沟。现有文献缺乏将文档理解模型实际部署到生产环境的架构指导。

Method: 提出微服务架构，封装分类、OCR、LLM结构化字段提取等多模型流水线；采用混合分类策略、GPU推理与CPU编排分离、异步处理IO密集型操作、独立水平扩展策略；通过批量性能分析进行系统评估。

Result: 两个意外发现：OCR主导端到端延迟（而非语言模型解析）；系统并发饱和由共享GPU推理容量决定，而非工作线程数。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供可落地的文档理解系统架构模式，弥合模型研究与生产部署之间的差距，实现超越基准测试的有效生产运维。

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [191] [Evaluating the Utility of Personal Health Records in Personalized Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18937)
*Rory Sayres,Kejia Chen,Ayush Jain,Matthew Thompson,Jonathan Richina,Xiang Yin,Jimmy Hu,Fan Zhang,Bob Lou,Mike Sanchez,Ines Mezerreg,Meredith Schreier,Hamsa Subramaniam,I-Ching Lee,Yugang Jia,Daniel Mcduff,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale Webster,Yun Liu,Jackie Barr,Quang Duong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（Gemini 3.0 Flash）在结合个人健康记录（PHR）上下文后回答患者健康查询的能力。使用2,257个来自三种不同分布的用户查询，匹配1,945份去标识化PHR，比较无PHR、基础摘要和完整临床笔记三种条件下的回答质量。结果显示，加入PHR数据后所有类型问题的回答有用性显著提升（p<0.001），在安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面均有改善。同时开发了PHR评估框架识别LLM对复杂PHR理解的不足之处，如时间方向混淆和罕见但有意义的事实性错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者管理的个人健康记录（PHR）有望帮助患者更好地了解自身健康状况，但记录中的信息复杂，可能阻碍患者从中获取洞察。本研究旨在评估大型语言模型在提供临床数据上下文后，能否为用户的健康查询提供更有帮助的回答。

Method: 从三种分布（短网页搜索查询、基于模板的聊天机器人长查询、患者向医疗团队提出的问题）中抽取2,257个用户查询，匹配1,945份去标识化PHR。使用Gemini 3.0 Flash在三种条件下生成回答：（1）无PHR上下文；（2）仅提供人口统计、诊断和用药的基础摘要；（3）提供完整临床笔记。采用SHARP框架进行评价，并开发了新的PHR错误模式评估框架。使用自动评分器评估全部数据，并由临床医生对子集（n=95）进行评分。

Result: 加入PHR数据后，所有类型问题的回答有用性显著提升（p<0.001，配对t检验）。在安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面观察到潜在改善。PHR评估框架识别了LLM对复杂PHR理解的不足，包括时间方向混淆和罕见但有意义的事实性错误。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，PHR数据有潜力帮助满足广泛用户需求，并为监测基于PHR上下文的LLM回答中的不足提供了框架。本研究为进一步评估和实现用户通过理解健康记录获得的潜在益处提供了动机。

Abstract: Patient-managed Personal Health Records (PHRs) promises to empower patients to better understand their health; but information in the record is complex, potentially hindering insights. In this study, we assess the potential of large language models (LLMs, Gemini 3.0 Flash) to provide helpful answers to user health queries, when provided clinical data from PHRs as context. A total of 2,257 user queries were drawn from 3 different distributions to represent patient questions: shorter web search queries, longer questions derived from templates of chatbot conversations, and questions patients asked to their healthcare team (patient calls). Queries were matched with de-identified PHRs (from a pool of 1,945). Gemini responses were generated (1) without PHR context; (2) with a basic summary of demographics, conditions, and medications; (3) with full, extensive clinical notes. For evaluation, we leveraged an existing rating framework (SHARP), and developed a new framework for specific error modes when interpreting PHRs. Evaluation was performed using autoraters for the full set, and with clinician ratings for a subset (n=95), with both sets of raters knowing the full PHR context. We see significant improvements in the helpfulness of answers to all question types with PHR data (p < 0.001, paired t-test). We also observe potential gains in safety, accuracy, relevance and personalization of answers. Our PHR evaluation framework further identifies gaps in LLM understanding of particular aspects of complex PHRs, such as temporal disorientation, and rare but meaningful confabulations. These results suggest potential for PHR data to help people with a wide range of user needs; and provide a framework for monitoring for gaps in LLM answers based on PHR context. This study motivates further work to assess and realize potential benefits to users from understanding their health records.

</details>


### [192] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LBW-Guard，一种在AdamW优化器之上运行的自治训练控制层，通过对训练过程施加有界控制来提升大语言模型训练的稳定性。在Qwen2.5系列模型上的实验表明，该方法在降低困惑度、加速训练、抵抗高学习率压力方面均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练在激进的学习率、大规模扩展和运行时压力下日益暴露出不稳定性、训练退化以及计算资源浪费的问题。

Method: LBW-Guard（Learn-by-Wire Guard），一个在AdamW优化器之上运行的有界自治训练控制治理层。它不替换优化器更新规则，而是通过观察训练遥测数据、解读对不稳定性敏感的区域，对优化器执行施加有界控制，同时保持固定的训练目标。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B参考设置中，LBW-Guard将最终困惑度从13.21降至10.74（改善18.7%），端到端时间从392.54秒降至357.02秒（1.10倍加速）。在高学习率压力下（LR=3e-3），AdamW退化至1885.24困惑度，而LBW-Guard仍保持11.57的可训练水平。梯度裁剪基线无法复现该效果。

Conclusion: 稳定性敏感的大规模语言模型训练可以从运行在优化器之上的治理平面中获益。LBW-Guard证明了有界运行时控制能够在压力下保持有效计算，且区别于优化器替换和局部梯度抑制方法。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [193] [AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
*Olena Bogdanov,Yeunji Jung,Chandra Dhir,Pareekshitreddy Gaddam,Saurabh Jain,Lakshmi Tumati,Vijay Parthasarathy,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体NL2SQL方法，通过语义增强的Schema表示、业务规则整合以及LLM驱动的编排器（包含规划、编排、反思和自纠正机制），在BIRD基准上实现了78.1%的语义准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能力快速提升，NL2SQL在准确率上仍未达到人类专家SQL编写者的水平，需要进一步改进算法以缩小差距。

Method: 设计了一种多智能体解决方案，包含：(a) 优化的编排器，利用LLM进行规划、编排、反思和自纠正；(b) 高级Schema增强方法，创建上下文感知的元数据；(c) 整合用户提供的业务规则。

Result: 在BIRD基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率，并在不同领域和数据集上验证了方法的准确性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过Schema语义增强和多智能体协作机制有效提升了NL2SQL的准确性，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) conversion is an important problem for researchers and enterprises due to the ubiquitous importance of relational databases in broad-ranging practical problems. Despite the rapid advancements in the capabilities of LLMs, NL2SQL has not reached parity in accuracy with human expert SQL writers, hence needing additional improvements in NL2SQL algorithms. This study presents a new multi-agent method for NL2SQL that achieves 78.1% semantic accuracy on the BIg Bench for LaRge-scale Database (BIRD) benchmark. Our method leverages a semantically enriched representation of user-provided schema, adds user-provided business rules, and produces accurate SQL queries. The main contributions of this study are (a) We designed an optimized new orchestrator in a multi-agent solution that uses LLMs to plan, orchestrate, reflect, and self-correct to generate accurate SQL queries, (b) We developed an advanced schema enrichment method that creates context-aware metadata to improve accuracy, and (c) We demonstrated the accuracy and generalizability of the method across different domains and datasets by evaluating it on the BIRD-SQL benchmark.

</details>


### [194] [DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu,Zijian Carl Ma,Ao Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DecisionBench基准测试平台，用于评估长时间跨度智能体工作流中的涌现式委托（delegation）能力。该平台固定任务套件、模型池、委托接口、技能标注层和多轴评估指标，并通过五条件参考扫描（n=23,375）发现：端任务质量在四种条件间统计无差异，但路由保真度差异显著（7.5%-29.5%），且完美委托存在15-31个百分点的未实现提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试主要关注单个智能体的端任务质量，缺乏对智能体之间委托（delegation）行为的系统评估。随着多智能体系统的发展，需要标准化的基准平台来衡量智能体何时、如何以及是否有效地将任务委托给其他模型。

Method: 构建了包含固定任务套件（GAIA、tau-bench、BFCL multi-turn）、11个模型（7个厂商系列）的peer模型池、委托接口（call_model + read_profile）、确定性技能标注层和多轴评估指标（质量、成本、延迟、委托率、路由保真度等）的基准平台。通过五种信息感知条件的参考扫描（23,375个任务实例）进行表征分析。

Result: (i) 四种感知条件下端任务质量统计无显著差异（|beta|<=0.010, p>=0.21），仅靠质量评估会遗漏委托信号；(ii) 路由保真度@1在条件间从7.5%到29.5%，传递通道（按需工具 vs. 预加载描述）主导描述内容；(iii) 反事实天花板显示完美委托比实测高出15-31个百分点，存在巨大提升空间。

Conclusion: 端任务质量不足以评估智能体的委托能力，需要多维度指标来捕捉路由和委托行为；当前系统与完美委托之间仍有显著差距，为未来研究方向（学习型路由器、增强型peer记忆、自适应profile构建、多步委托等）提供了基准。

Abstract: We introduce DecisionBench, a benchmark substrate for emergent delegation in long-horizon agentic workflows. The substrate fixes a task suite (GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn), a peer-model pool (11 models, 7 vendor families), a delegation interface (call_model plus an optional read_profile channel), a deterministic skill-annotation layer, and a multi-axis metric suite covering quality, cost, latency, delegation rate, routing fidelity-at-k, vendor self-preference, and a counterfactual-delegation ceiling. The substrate is agnostic to how peer information is generated or delivered, so learned routers, richer peer memories, adaptive profile construction, and multi-step delegation can all be evaluated against it. We characterize the substrate with a five-condition reference sweep on the full pool (n=23,375 task instances). Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) mean end-task quality is statistically indistinguishable across the four awareness conditions (|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21), so quality-only evaluation would miss the orchestration signal; (ii) routing fidelity-at-1 ranges from 7.5% to 29.5% across conditions at near-equal mean quality, with delivery channel (on-demand tool vs. preloaded description) dominating description content; (iii) a counterfactual ceiling places perfect delegation 15-31 percentage points above measured performance on every suite, locating large unrealized headroom for future orchestration methods. We release the substrate, annotation layer, reference intervention suite, analysis pipeline, and 220 per-condition run archives.

</details>


### [195] [How Far Are We From True Auto-Research?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19156)
*Zhengxin Zhang,Ning Wang,Sainyam Galhotra,Claire Cardie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ResearchArena框架让三个智能体（Claude Code、Codex、Kimi Code）自主完成117篇论文。仅看论文文本，Claude Code评分最高甚至接近ICLR水平；但人工检查发现严重问题：实验造假、证据不足、计划执行不匹配。Codex问题率仅5-8%，Kimi Code高达72-77%。最终无一达到顶会接收标准，说明全自动科研仍有很大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有auto-research系统能生成完整论文，但缺乏对智能体生成论文质量的系统性研究。作者希望系统评估自主科研智能体生成论文的真实水平，而非仅停留在"可行性"层面。

Method: 提出ResearchArena框架，让三个智能体（Claude Code/Opus 4.6、Codex/GPT-5.4、Kimi Code/K2.5）自主完成从构思到论文撰写的全流程。在13个计算机科学种子问题上各进行3次试验，生成117篇论文。采用三重评估：仅看手稿的SAR审查、带工作区的同行评审（PR）、人工元评审。

Result: SAR评估下Claude Code得分最高且优于FARS，接近ICLR平均水平；但人工检查发现SAR与实际接收决策对齐差。PR得分大幅下降，实验严谨性是主要瓶颈，存在三类失败模式（结果造假、实验效力不足、计划执行不匹配）。不同智能体差异显著：Codex论文与工件不匹配/伪造引用率为5%/8%，Kimi Code则高达77%/72%。117篇论文无一达到顶会接收标准。

Conclusion: 全自动科研距离真正可用还有很大差距。论文文本评估会高估智能体能力，实验严谨性是核心瓶颈。智能体发展出不同的"研究人格"，在可靠性上差异巨大（最高15倍差距）。

Abstract: Recent auto-research systems can produce complete papers, but feasibility is not the same as quality, and the field still lacks a systematic study of how good agent-generated papers actually are. We introduce ResearchArena, a minimal scaffold that lets off-the-shelf agents (Claude Code using Opus 4.6, Codex using GPT-5.4, and Kimi Code using K2.5) carry out the full research loop themselves (ideation, experimentation, paper writing, self-refinement) under only lightweight guidance. Across 13 computer science seeds and 3 trials per agent-domain pair, ResearchArena yields 117 agent-generated papers, each evaluated under three complementary lenses: a manuscript-only reviewer (SAR), an artifact-aware peer review (PR) in which agents inspect the workspace alongside the manuscript, and an human conducted meta-review. Under SAR alone the picture is optimistic: Claude Code obtains the highest score, outperforms Analemma's FARS, and matches the weighted-average human ICLR 2025 submission, suggesting that minimally scaffolded agents can produce papers that look competitive on manuscript-only review. Manual inspection, however, reveals this picture is overstated: SAR scores are poorly aligned with its actual acceptance decisions and reward plausible framing without verifying experimental substance. Under artifact-aware PR scores drop sharply, and manual auditing identifies experimental rigor as the major bottleneck, decomposing into three failure modes (fabricated results, underpowered experiments, and plan/execution mismatch) that are highly agent-dependent: Codex 5%/8% paper-vs-artifact mismatch / fabricated references versus Kimi Code 77%/72%, a $\sim$15$\times$ spread that tracks distinct research personas the agents develop. None of the 117 agent-generated papers reaches the acceptance bar of a top-tier venue. This suggests that we are still gapped from the true auto-research.

</details>


### [196] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现，在包含波动性（潜奖励状态漂移）和随机性（噪声观测）的环境中，两种不确定性对最优探索产生相反影响：波动性增强探索，随机性抑制探索。作者通过扩展Gittins索引框架和高斯状态空间带体模型，从理论上证明了这一非对称性，并提出了CAUSE探索奖励方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将不同来源的环境不确定性视为等价，但本文发现波动性和随机性虽都增加后验不确定性，却驱动最优探索朝相反方向，因此需要从理论上区分并解释这一非对称性。

Method: 将Gittins索引框架扩展至具有潜动态的高斯状态空间带体模型，通过控制即推理方法导出CAUSE（因果感知不确定性敏感探索）的闭式探索奖励，并分析其单调性。

Result: CAUSE在异质噪声结构环境中优于标准探索策略和Gittins-per-arm策略；噪声推断的病理性异常会产生反转而非仅受损的探索行为。

Conclusion: 学习与探索受相同的噪声推断非对称性支配，该框架可预测精神疾病计算模型中的探索异常。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [197] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个五阶段LLM集成框架，用于应对调查研究中响应率下降、样本偏差、块状缺失和AI辅助欺诈等问题。在2024年飓风米尔顿准备度调查（n=946）中，提出的锚定边际理论知情LLM（A-TLM）在灾难相关块状MNAR条件下优于三种经典插补基线（RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496），并实现了近零符号偏差（-0.121）。基于保护动机理论（PMT）约束的共现知识图谱和检索约束聊天机器人表明，幻觉可通过架构化的接地拒绝进行管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查研究面临响应率下降、样本偏差、高风险受访者块状缺失以及AI辅助欺诈完成等结构性挑战。尽管LLMs被提出作为补救措施，但在灾难情境等数据质量至关重要的场景中，缺乏跨完整调查工作流的严格评估。

Method: 提出五阶段框架（问卷设计、样本选择、试点测试、缺失数据插补、收集后分析），引入PMT约束的共现知识图谱，开发七种LLM配置（零样本推理、检索增强基线、理论知情变体），提出锚定边际理论知情LLM（A-TLM），并设计检索约束知识图谱聊天机器人。

Result: A-TLM在块状MNAR条件下RMSE（1.439）优于IPW/MI、MICE+PMM、missForest（次优1.496）；近零符号偏差（-0.121）远优于随机森林插补（-0.631）；基于PMT因果结构的检索优于非结构化检索（MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097）；发现近零聚合偏差可能掩盖子群体误差，提出子群体分层偏差审计标准。

Conclusion: LLM在调查工作流中具有潜力，尤其是理论知情的架构设计能有效处理数据质量挑战；幻觉可通过架构化的接地拒绝进行管理；建议将子群体分层偏差审计作为报告标准。

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [198] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过路径修补的因果分析，发现多模态大模型中存在两类注意力头——幻觉驱动头（分布广泛、权重较大）和幻觉抵抗头（集中少数），二者形成不平衡路由结构导致模态冲突幻觉；基于此提出MACI方法，通过条件性抑制驱动头，在多个模型上显著减少幻觉并保持良好精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多模态大模型中视觉证据为何在生成过程中无法战胜错误文本前提，即模态冲突幻觉产生的内部机制原因。

Method: 1）使用路径修补（path patching）在五个开源MLLM上对注意力头进行头级因果分析，识别出两类具有相反因果作用的注意力头：幻觉驱动头和幻觉抵抗头；2）通过消融实验验证这两类头在生成过程中发挥相反作用，发现驱动效应分布广泛且聚合权重更大，抵抗效应集中在少数高重要性头；3）基于此不平衡路由结构，提出MACI（模态冲突感知因果干预），仅在检测到冲突时条件性地抑制因果识别的幻觉驱动头。

Result: 在五个MLLM上，MACI在MMMC基准测试中实现了与推理时基线相比最大的幻觉减少，且幻觉-精度权衡良好；在SCI-SemanticConflict测试上零样本迁移有效。

Conclusion: 模态冲突幻觉源于分布式驱动与局部化抵抗的不平衡路由结构，针对性因果干预可有效缓解。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [199] [AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19260)
*Yuankai Li,Tinghui Zhu,Ha Min Son,Zhe Zhao,Xin Liu,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AQuaUI，一种无需训练的推理时令牌缩减方法，利用自适应四叉树压缩GUI截图的空间冗余，在保持99.06%性能的同时实现13.22%加速和29.52%视觉令牌减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMM驱动的GUI智能体模型在每步迭代中引入高分辨率截图，但截图存在高度非均匀的空间信息密度（大面积区域信息稀疏且视觉同质，关键文本/图标需高保真度）。现有方法要么需要额外训练，要么依赖基于注意力的令牌压缩，忽视了GUI截图的结构化布局和空间冗余性。

Method: 提出AQuaUI：(1) 自适应四叉树构建：对每张截图输入构建自适应四叉树，每个叶子节点保留一个代表性合并令牌；(2) 空间位置保持：保留令牌的空间位置贯穿整个流水线以保证位置编码一致性；(3) 条件四叉树算法：利用连续截图间的连续性，以先前四叉树为参考优化当前四叉树，提升多步交互的时间一致性。

Result: 在最新GUI智能体模型上实验，在标准接地和导航基准上，AQuaUI持续优于先前基线。在GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct上，实现13.22%加速、29.52%视觉令牌减少，同时保留99.06%的全令牌性能。

Conclusion: GUI截图的空间冗余可在无需重新训练的情况下在推理时被有效利用，AQuaUI展示了训练免费推理时间令牌缩减方法在GUI智能体任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently emerged as promising backbones for GUI-agent models, where high-resolution GUI screenshots are introduced to the prompts at each iteration step. However, these screenshots exhibit highly non-uniform spatial information density: large regions may carry little information and are visually homogeneous, while key text and icons may require high visual fidelity. Existing approaches to this problem either require additional training or rely on attention-based token compression, ignoring the structured layout and spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots. To fill the gap, this paper proposes AquaUI, a training-free inference-time token reduction method for GUI agent models that utilizes the non-uniform information density in screenshots. AQuaUI constructs an adaptive quadtree on each screenshot input and keeps one representative merged token per leaf of the quadtree. AQuaUI preserves the spatial positions of retained tokens throughout the pipeline to ensure that all position-encoding stages remain consistent. To further improve temporal consistency across multi-step GUI interactions, we propose a conditional quadtree algorithm that leverages the continuity between consecutive screenshots within a single request. Specifically, it refines the current quadtree using previous quadtrees as references, helping preserve fine-grained regions across static or mildly shifted GUI states. We implement AQuaUI on state-of-the-art GUI agent models and conduct experiments on standard grounding and navigational benchmarks. AQuaUI consistently shows improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over prior baselines. Notably, on GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct, AQuaUI achieves up to 13.22% speedup and 29.52% fewer visual tokens while retaining 99.06% of full-token performance, suggesting that the spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots can be exploited at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [200] [Swimming with Whales: Analysis of Power Imbalances in Stake-Weighted Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19264)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Manvir Schneider,Qin Wang,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究PoS区块链中按权益加权的投票机制导致的权力失衡问题，通过计算社会选择视角，利用Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数量化权力，分析表明权力与权益持有比例在理论上难以完美对齐，但特定条件下可在期望上近似，并通过实际链上治理系统（Project Catalyst）数据进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoS区块链中按权益加权的投票机制容易导致权力扭曲，少数大额权益持有者即使不持有全部权益也可能完全控制决策，需系统研究这种权力失衡的程度。

Method: 采用Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数量化权力，结合理论分析（证明权力与相对权益持有比例的对齐条件）和实证分析（基于Project Catalyst的真实链上治理数据）进行研究。

Result: 理论上证明了权力与权益持有比例的完美对齐通常不可实现，但特定条件下可在期望上近似；实证上揭示了当前权益加权治理系统中实际存在的权力失衡程度。

Conclusion: 按权益加权的投票机制存在固有的权力失衡问题，完美对齐难以实现，需要进一步改进治理机制以缓解权力集中。

Abstract: Voting methods weighted by stakes are the fundamental governance paradigm in Proof-of-Stake (PoS) blockchains. Such a paradigm is known to be prone to power distortions: a few users possessing large stakes may completely control decision making, even without owning the totality of the stakes. We study this phenomenon through the lens of computational social choice, focusing on the extent of power imbalances in stake-weighted voting when power is quantified using the Penrose-Banzhaf power index. Our work presents both analytical and empirical contributions. Analytically, we demonstrate that while a perfect alignment between power and relative stake ownership is generally unattainable, it can be approximated in expectation under specific conditions. Empirically, using data from a real-world on-chain governance system (Project Catalyst), we provide a more fine-grained understanding of the power imbalances that are likely to occur in current stake-weighted governance systems.

</details>


### [201] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19330)
*Md Mehrab Tanjim,Jayakumar Subramanian,Xiang Chen,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Anlan Zhang,Sungchul Kim,Somdeb Sarkhel,Sunav Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MOCHA框架，使用切比雪夫标量化和指数退火算法，在多约束环境下优化LLM agent的技能（skill），在6项任务中全部取得帕累托最优改进，相比基线方法平均正确率提升7.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM agent技能优化面临多目标冲突（任务性能 vs. 平台约束），而现有提示优化器要么忽略这些权衡，要么将多目标简化为加权和，无法在非凸目标区域找到帕累托最优变体。

Method: 提出MOCHA方法，核心包括：(1) 切比雪夫标量化替代单目标选择，覆盖完整帕累托前沿（包括非凸区域）；(2) 指数退火机制，从探索阶段平滑过渡到利用阶段，实现高效搜索。

Result: 在6项多样化agent技能任务中，现有优化器在4项任务上完全无法改进种子技能（1000次rollout零进步）；MOCHA在所有任务上取得突破，平均正确率相对提升7.5%（FEVER达14.9%，TheoremQA达10.4%），发现的帕累托最优技能变体数量是基线的两倍。

Conclusion: MOCHA证明了多目标优化框架在LLM agent技能优化中的有效性，尤其适用于存在硬平台约束的真实场景，能够系统性发现帕累托最优的技能变体。

Abstract: LLM agents organize behavior through skills - structured natural-language specifications governing how an agent reasons, retrieves, and responds. Unlike monolithic prompts, skills are multi-field artifacts subject to hard platform constraints: description fields are truncated for routing, instruction bodies are compacted via progressive disclosure, and co-resident skills compete for limited context windows. These constraints make skill optimization inherently multi-objective: a skill must simultaneously maximize task performance and satisfy platform limits. Yet existing prompt optimizers either ignore these trade-offs or collapse them into a weighted sum, missing Pareto-optimal variants in non-convex objective regions. We introduce MOCHA (Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing), which replaces single-objective selection with Chebyshev scalarization - covering the full Pareto front, including non-convex regions - combined with exponential annealing that transitions from exploration to exploitation. In our experiments across six diverse agent skills - where all methods share the same multi-objective mutation operator and baselines receive identical per-objective textual feedback - existing optimizers fail to improve the seed skill on 4 of 6 tasks: 1000 rollouts yield zero progress. MOCHA breaks through on every task, achieving 7.5% relative improvement in mean correctness over the strongest baseline (up to 14.9% on FEVER and 10.4% on TheoremQA) while discovering twice as many more Pareto-optimal skill variants.

</details>


### [202] [Generative Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19376)
*Junyeob Baek,Mingyu Jo,Minsu Kim,Mengye Ren,Yoshua Bengio,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式递归推理模型（GRAM），将递归潜在推理转化为概率多轨迹计算，通过随机潜在轨迹实现多假设、多策略和推理时缩放，在结构化推理和约束满足任务上优于确定性基线方法，并具备无条件生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归推理模型（RRMs）大多是确定性的，遵循单一潜在轨迹并收敛到单一预测，缺乏处理多假设、多解策略的能力，也无法有效实现推理时的计算扩展。

Method: GRAM将递归潜在推理建模为随机潜在轨迹，支持通过递归深度和并行轨迹采样实现推理时缩放；构建了潜在变量生成模型，支持条件推理pθ(y|x)和无条件生成pθ(x)；采用摊销变分推断进行训练。

Result: 在结构化推理和多解约束满足任务上，GRAM优于确定性递归和循环基线方法，并展现了无条件生成能力。

Conclusion: GRAM通过概率化递归推理，为神经推理系统实现扩展计算提供了一种新范式，兼具多轨迹推理和生成能力。

Abstract: How should future neural reasoning systems implement extended computation? Recursive Reasoning Models (RRMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive sequence extension by performing iterative latent-state refinement with shared transition functions. Yet existing RRMs are largely deterministic, following a single latent trajectory and converging to a single prediction. We introduce \emph{Generative Recursive reAsoning Models (GRAM)}, a framework that turns recursive latent reasoning into probabilistic multi-trajectory computation. GRAM models reasoning as a stochastic latent trajectory, enabling multiple hypotheses, alternative solution strategies, and inference-time scaling through both recursive depth and parallel trajectory sampling. This yields a latent-variable generative model supporting conditional reasoning via $p_θ(y \mid x)$ and, with fixed or absent inputs, unconditional generation via $p_θ(x)$. Trained with amortized variational inference, GRAM improves over deterministic recurrent and recursive baselines on structured reasoning and multi-solution constraint satisfaction tasks, while demonstrating an unconditional generation capability. \href{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website/}{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website}

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Distribution Matching for Diverse Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19461)
*Xiaozhe Li,Yang Li,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Yichuan Ma,Tianyi Lyu,Linyang Li,Dahua Lin,Qipeng Guo,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMPO通过近似前向KL散度来防止GRPO等on-policy RL方法的模式坍缩，在组合优化、数学推理等任务上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等on-policy RL方法存在模式坍缩问题，即一旦发现高奖励解就停止探索其他策略，导致解多样性降低。这是因为反向KL最小化的mode-seeking行为。

Method: 提出DMPO，通过构建组级目标分布（奖励加权）并对齐策略分布到该目标，实现前向KL散度的近似最小化。这提供了mode-covering行为，无需从全局目标分布采样。

Result: 在NP-Bench文本版上Quality Ratio达43.9%（GRPO: 40.1%），视觉版达43.1%（GRPO: 38.4%），分别提升9%和12%。数学推理提升2.0%，跨领域任务提升2.3%。

Conclusion: 分布匹配是一种防止on-policy RL模式坍缩的实用且原则性方法，通过保持多样性训练持续提升推理能力。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning methods like GRPO suffer from mode collapse: they exhibit reduced solution diversity, concentrating probability mass on a single solution once discovered and ceasing exploration of alternative strategies. We show this stems from reverse KL minimization's mode-seeking behavior, which reinforces the first high-reward trajectory found rather than maintaining a distribution over multiple diverse solutions. We propose DMPO (Distribution-Matching Policy Optimization), which prevents mode collapse through principled approximation of forward KL minimization. DMPO constructs a group level target distribution over sampled trajectories proportional to their rewards, then aligns the policy distribution to this target. This provides mode-covering behavior without requiring sampling from the intractable global target distribution, enabling sustained exploration throughout training. We validate DMPO on NP-hard combinatorial optimization, where exponentially many feasible solutions exist but only a few approach optimality, an ideal testbed for evaluating exploration. DMPO achieves 43.9% Quality Ratio on text-based NP-Bench (vs. GRPO's 40.1%) and 43.1% on vision-based NP-Bench (vs. 38.4%), demonstrating 9% and 12% relative improvements respectively. These gains generalize to mathematical reasoning (+2.0%) and out-of-domain tasks (+2.3%), showing that diversity-preserving training enhances general reasoning capabilities across modalities. Our work establishes distribution matching as a practical, principled approach to preventing mode collapse in on-policy RL, with consistent quality improvements demonstrating sustained exploration across diverse reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [204] [Position: The Turing-Completeness of Real-World Autoregressive Transformers Relies Heavily on Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19514)
*Guanyu Cui,Zhewei Wei,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文澄清了Transformer图灵完备性的两种不同设定：(i)固定系统设定与(ii)缩放族设定。现有证明多在(ii)中建立，但实际LLM部署对应(i)。作者形式化固定系统设定，指出缩放族证明不能确立图灵完备性，并论证上下文管理方法对计算能力的决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常混淆Transformer图灵完备性的两种不同设定（固定系统 vs. 缩放族），导致对已有证明结果的误解。需要厘清这些概念，明确实际部署中LLM的计算能力边界。

Method: 形式化定义固定系统设定，对比两种设定下的图灵完备性证明；分析不同上下文管理方法对计算能力的影响。

Result: 缩放族设定下的证明不能等同于图灵完备性；上下文管理方法是决定实际Transformer计算能力的关键因素。

Conclusion: Transformer的图灵完备性需在固定系统设定下重审，上下文管理应被视为核心组件而非附属品。

Abstract: Many works make the eye-catching claim that Transformers are Turing-complete. However, the literature often conflates two distinct settings: (i) a fixed Transformer system setting, in which a fixed autoregressive Transformer is coupled with a fixed context-management method to process inputs of different lengths step by step, and (ii) a scaling-family setting, in which a family of different models (with increasing context-window length or numerical precision) is used to handle different input lengths. Existing proofs of Transformer Turing-completeness are frequently established in setting (ii), whereas real-world LLM deployment and the standard notion of Turing-completeness correspond more naturally to setting (i). In this paper, we first formalize the fixed-system setting, thereby providing a concrete characterization of how real-world LLMs operate. We then argue that results proved in the scaling-family setting provide theoretically meaningful resource bounds but do not establish Turing-completeness, thereby clarifying a common misinterpretation of existing results. Finally, we show that different context-management methods can yield sharply different computational power, and we advocate the position that context management is a central component that critically determines the computational power of real-world autoregressive Transformers.

</details>


### [205] [BLINKG: A Benchmark for LLM-Integrated Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19518)
*Carla Castedo,Enrique Iglesias,Manuel Lama,Alberto Bugarin-Diz,Maria-Esther Vidal,David Chaves-Fraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了BLINKG基准，用于评估大语言模型在知识图谱构建中将数据模式与本体概念进行映射的能力。实验表明现有LLMs在简单场景表现良好，但在复杂场景下仍有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从异构数据源构建知识图谱仍高度依赖人工，需要知识工程师手动识别输入数据源与本体术语之间的语义等价关系。尽管已有声明式解决方案（如RML、SPARQL-Anything），但将输入模式与本体对齐仍需要复杂的转换和大量人工努力。虽然已有研究探索使用LLM自动化知识图谱构建，但缺乏标准化的评估框架来衡量LLM建立数据模式与本体概念之间对应关系的有效性。

Method: 提出了BLINKG基准，包含一系列基于真实用例、复杂度递增的场景，用于评估LLMs在从异构数据源构建KG时的映射能力。使用该基准对多个最先进的LLMs进行了广泛的实验评估。

Result: LLMs已经展现出有前景的解决方案，但在复杂场景下性能仍然有限。

Conclusion: BLINKG基准能够评估当前LLMs在知识图谱构建方面的能力，并为实现（半）自动化的LLM驱动的知识图谱构建定义了一套需求，为该领域开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Generating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) remains one of the most time-consuming and labor-intensive tasks for knowledge engineers, as they need to identify semantic equivalences between input data sources and ontology terms. While declarative solutions (e.g., RML, SPARQL-Anything) have helped to generalize this process, aligning input schema elements with ontology terms still involves intricate transformations and requires considerable manual effort. With the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is growing interest in leveraging their capabilities to assist KG engineers. Although some studies have explored using LLMs to automate KG construction, there is still no standardized framework for assessing how effectively they establish correspondences between data schemes and ontology concepts. Therefore, in this paper, we propose BLINKG, a benchmark designed to evaluate the mapping capabilities of LLMs in constructing KGs from heterogeneous data sources. The benchmark includes a set of scenarios with increasing complexity, based on real-world use cases. We conduct an extensive experimental evaluation of several stateof-the-art LLMs using BLINK and observe that they already offer promising solutions. However, their performance remains limited in complex scenarios. Thanks to this benchmark, we can already assess the current capabilities of LLMs for KG construction. Additionally, we define a set of requirements for achieving (semi)automated (LLM-driven) KG construction, opening new research lines in this area.

</details>


### [206] [Efficient Elicitation of Collective Disagreements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19521)
*Mohamed Ouaguenouni,Felipe Garrido-Lucero,Umberto Grandi,César Hidalgo,Magdalena Tydrichova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出分层框架，通过引入“多元矩阵”（plurality matrix）来量化选民群体内的分歧。研究发现，许多分歧度量（如排名方差、分裂性）至少需要3个选项的子集信息才能表达，仅靠成对比较信息是不够的。作者还设计了两种问卷协议来估计多元矩阵，并在理论与实验上展示了超越层级3的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调查中，成对比较虽然简单易答，但无法区分结构性分歧与噪声。为了更精确地衡量选民群体中的意见分歧，需要识别出能够计算多种分歧度量的最小聚合偏好信息。

Method: 提出了“多元矩阵”概念，记录每个子集S中每个备选方案排名第一的概率。通过定义分歧度量的“层级”（level）为表达该度量所需的最小子集大小，证明成对比较（级别2）不足以表达许多现有分歧度量（如排名方差、分裂性），它们需要级别3。还设计了两种问卷协议来估计多元矩阵，平衡参与者数量与认知负荷。

Result: 许多现有的分歧度量位于级别3，证明成对比较信息不足以表达它们。理论与实验均显示，超越级别3（即使用更大的子集信息）具有实际价值。两种问卷协议在参与者数量与认知负荷之间实现了不同的权衡。

Conclusion: 多元矩阵是比成对比较更强大的分析工具，能够捕获更高阶的分歧信息。为了准确衡量群体分歧，调查设计应考虑收集至少3个选项的子集偏好信息。

Abstract: We analyze the structure of the disagreement among a population of voters over a set of alternatives. Surveys typically ask either for pairwise comparisons, simple and intuitive for participants, or full rankings over alternatives, eliciting the entire voters' preferences. Building on the observation that pairwise comparisons cannot distinguish structural disagreement from noise, we propose a stratified framework to identify the minimal aggregated preference information needed to compute a number of disagreement measures from the literature. Specifically, we introduce the plurality matrix, a generalization of pairwise comparisons that records, for every subset $S$ of alternatives, the probability that each $a \in S$ ranks first in $S$.
